Motivation: Ego4D STA任务要求从第一人称视频中预测即将发生的人-物交互，包括物体的位置、类别、动词、接触时间等。现有方法难以同时建模空间细节与短时序上下文。

Method: 采用StillFast双流设计：(1) 空间流：COCO预训练的Faster R-CNN从最后一帧高分辨率图像提取目标提议；(2) 时序流：冻结的V-JEPA 2.1提取视频片段的自我中心时序上下文。通过特征调制和ROI级融合将时序信息注入检测分支，最后用多头预测器同时输出框、名词、动词、接触时间和置信度。

Result: 在EgoVis 2026 Ego4D STA挑战赛官方榜单上取得第一名。

Conclusion: VISTA证明StillFast设计（空间检测+短时序上下文）对第一人称交互预测任务有效，V-JEPA作为时序编码器能提供有益的自我中心视频表征。

Abstract: We propose VISTA, a V-JEPA Integrated StillFast Temporal Anticipator for the Ego4D Short-Term Object Interaction Anticipation (STA) Challenge at EgoVis 2026. Given an egocentric video timestamp, the task requires anticipating the next human-object interaction, including the future active object's bounding box, noun category, verb category, time-to-contact, and confidence score. VISTA follows a StillFast-style design that combines object-centric spatial detection with short-horizon temporal context. Specifically, a COCO-pretrained Faster R-CNN ResNet-50 FPN detector generates object proposals from the last observed high-resolution frame, while a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 temporal branch extracts clip-level egocentric context from the observed video. The temporal representation is injected into the detection pathway through feature modulation and ROI-level context fusion. The fused proposal features are then passed to multi-head STA predictors for box refinement, noun classification, verb classification, time-to-contact regression, and interaction confidence estimation. For the final submission, we further ensemble complementary predictions to improve robustness. Experimental results on the official challenge server show that VISTA achieves first place in the EgoVis 2026 Ego4D STA Challenge. Our code will be released at https://github.com/CorrineQiu/VISTA.

</details>


### [72] [Deep Attention Reweighting: Post-Hoc Attention-Based Feature Aggregation in CNNs for Disentangling Core and Spurious Features under Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20732)
*Kin Whye Chew,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep Attention Reweighting (DAR)模块，通过可学习的注意力聚合替代全局平均池化(GAP)，在微调分类头的同时训练注意力权重，有效缓解特征纠缠问题，减少模型对虚假相关性的依赖，性能优于Deep Feature Reweighting (DFR)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN常利用数据集中的虚假相关性进行预测，导致泛化能力和公平性下降。现有后处理方法DFR受限于纠缠特征（由GAP层导致），无法同时放大核心特征并抑制虚假特征。

Method: 提出DAR模块，用可学习的注意力聚合机制替代GAP层，对特征图的空间位置进行自适应加权，与分类头联合微调，实现选择性抑制虚假特征。

Result: 在多个数据集和指标上，DAR一致优于DFR，验证了基于注意力的聚合能缓解GAP导致的特征纠缠，减少模型对虚假相关性的依赖。

Conclusion: DAR作为后处理模块有效解决了GAP造成的特征纠缠问题，为减少模型依赖虚假相关性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) often exploit spurious correlations in datasets, learning superficially predictive yet causally irrelevant features, leading to poor generalization and fairness issues. Deep Feature Reweighting (DFR) is a post-hoc technique that reduces a trained model's reliance on spurious correlations by retraining its classification head on a target dataset. However, we show that DFR is fundamentally constrained by operating on entangled features, limiting its ability to amplify the core features while simultaneously suppressing the spurious ones. We trace this entanglement to the ubiquitous Global Average Pooling (GAP) layer, which indiscriminately collapses spatially distinct core and spurious features into a single representation. To address this, we propose Deep Attention Reweighting (DAR), a post-hoc attention-based aggregation module that replaces GAP and is retrained jointly with the classification head. DAR computes an adaptive weighting of spatial locations across feature maps, enabling selective suppression of spurious features before the collapse into entangled features. Across various datasets, metrics, and ablations, DAR consistently outperforms DFR, demonstrating that our attention-based aggregation mitigates GAP-induced entanglement and reduces spurious reliance.

</details>


### [73] [Focus-then-Context: Subject-Centric Progressive Visual Token Reduction for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20950)
*Yulin Zhao,Yun Wang,Dehua Zheng,Borui jiang,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPpruner范式，通过模拟人类"焦点-上下文"视觉机制，在保留关键视觉主体的同时高效减少视觉token，实现2.53倍加速且仅保留22.2%的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token缩减方法虽然缓解了VLM推理计算成本高的问题，但未能充分挖掘显著主体及其上下文关系，导致视觉表征不够全面。

Method: 1) 焦点识别模块：显式建模视觉显著性与语义相关性，挖掘全面的视觉主体谱系；2) 上下文感知结构扫描模块：聚合邻域上下文线索，恢复全局关系依赖，保持主体结构完整性。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL上仅保留22.2%的视觉token实现2.53倍加速；在LLaVA上减少67% FLOPs，精度仅下降0.6%。

Conclusion: SPpruner通过模拟人类视觉感知的"焦点-上下文"机制，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持了视觉表征的保真度与结构完整性，优于现有SOTA方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) face a bottleneck of prohibitive computational costs arising from massive visual token sequences during inference. Existing vision token reduction methods alleviate this burden, but they unintentionally preserve the isolated visual subject strictly aligned with the user's query, which fails to substantially explore salient subjects and their contextual relationships. In this paper, we propose SPpruner, a subject-centric progressive reduction paradigm that emulates the \textit{Focus-then-Context} mechanism of the human visual perception system. Specifically, we first construct a focus identification module to explicitly model the interplay between visual saliency and semantic relevance. Herein, it can excavate the comprehensive visual subject spectrum to ensure a high-fidelity representation of visual input. Subsequently, a context-aware structural scanning module is developed to aggregate contextual cues from neighboring regions. As such, it can effectively restore global relational dependencies to uphold the structural integrity of the preserved subjects. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our paradigm consistently outperforms SOTA methods, achieving up to 2.53 times speedup with only 22.2% of visual tokens retained in Qwen2.5-VL and a 67% FLOPs reduction on LLaVA with a negligible 0.6% accuracy drop.

</details>


### [74] [Sketch2MinSurf: Vision-Language Guided Generation of Editable Minimal Surfaces from Hand-Drawn Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20733)
*Wenda Wang,Anqi Liu,Junqi Yang,Lei He,Luying Wang,Jiachen Lu,Weixin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sketch2MinSurf是一个将手绘草图转换为可编辑3D曲面的混合框架，结合视觉语言引导与极小曲面理论，在100张草图测试中达到0.844的拓扑相似度，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（GAN、NeRF、扩散模型）难以从手绘草图生成可直接用于下游设计的可编辑流形，主要挑战在于非欧几里得表面的表示和拓扑一致性的保持。

Method: 提出混合视觉语言与几何优化框架，核心是空间-拓扑编码（将几何表示为节点坐标与实/虚边骨架元组）以及S2MS-Loss（奖励调制的联合约束几何重建与拓扑一致性目标函数）。

Result: 在100张草图测试集上达到0.844的拓扑相似度，优于现有草图到形状基线，生成的流形可直接编辑且无伪影。

Conclusion: Sketch2MinSurf有效弥合了手绘草图与结构化3D几何之间的鸿沟，并且通过公共艺术装置展示了其在人类意图驱动的3D形态生成方面的潜力。

Abstract: Converting hand-drawn sketches into structured 3D geometries remains challenging due to the difficulty of representing non-Euclidean surfaces and maintaining topological consistency. Existing generative models such as GANs, NeRFs, and diffusion architectures often fail to produce editable manifolds directly usable in downstream design workflows. We present Sketch2MinSurf, a hybrid vision-language and geometric optimization framework that integrates vision-language guidance with minimal-surface theory to generate smooth and editable 3D surfaces from hand-drawn sketches. The core of our approach is a spatial-topological encoding that represents geometry as tuples of node coordinates and real/virtual edge skeletons, enabling stable topological control during generation. We further introduce the Sketch2MinSurf Structural Loss (S2MS-Loss), a reward-modulated objective that jointly constrains geometric reconstruction and topological coherence. On a test set of 100 sketches, Sketch2MinSurf achieves a topological similarity score of 0.844, outperforming existing sketch-to-shape baselines. The generated manifolds are directly editable and free from non-manifold artifacts. A public art installation at a university showcases the method's potential for human-intent-driven 3D form generation. The dataset and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Sketch2MinSurf/.

</details>


### [75] [Finding the Correct Visual Evidence Without Forgetting: Mitigating Hallucination in LVLMs via Inter-Layer Visual Attention Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20965)
*Yutong Xie,Zhenglin Hua,Ran Wang,Wing W. Y. Ng,Xizhao Wang,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于层间视觉注意力差异（ILVAD）的无需训练的方法，通过增强视觉证据的注意力来缓解LVLMs的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs在生成时对正确视觉证据关注不足且逐渐遗忘，从而导致幻觉。

Method: 1) 利用跨层的注意力权重构建视觉显著性图；2) 在生成过程中增强对视觉证据的注意力；3) 选择与视觉证据强相关的文本token进行强调。

Result: 在5个最新模型上多个基准测试中，该方法一致地缓解了不同架构LVLMs的幻觉。

Conclusion: ILVAD是一种无需训练、即插即用的幻觉缓解方法，能有效解决视觉注意力不足和遗忘问题。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have shown remarkable performance on a wide range of vision-language tasks. Despite this progress, they are still prone to hallucination, generating responses that are inconsistent with visual content. In this work, we find that LVLMs tend to hallucinate when they pay insufficient attention to the correct visual evidence and gradually forget it during the generation process. We empirically find that although LVLMs overall attend insufficiently to visual evidence, they exhibit sensitivity to the correct visual evidence in specific layers, with notable inter-layer discrepancy. Motivated by this observation, we propose a novel hallucination mitigation method that enhances visual evidence based on Inter-Layer Visual Attention Discrepancy (ILVAD). Specifically, we obtain the attention weights from early generated tokens to visual tokens across layers and identify the tokens that are repeatedly activated as visual evidence, forming a saliency map. We then enhance attention to visual evidence during generation through the saliency map to reduce visual forgetting. In addition, we leverage the saliency map to obtain attention scores of generated text to visual evidence, in order to select and emphasize text tokens that are strongly grounded in visual evidence. Our method is training-free and plug-and-play. Multiple benchmark evaluations conducted on five recently released models show that our method can consistently mitigate hallucinations in different LVLMs over various architectures. Code is available at https://github.com/ytx-ML/ILVAD.

</details>


### [76] [Lowering the Barrier to IREX Participation: Open-Source Algorithms, Toolkit, and Benchmarking for Iris Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20735)
*Siamul Karim Khan,Patrick J. Flynn,Adam Czajka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个新的开源虹膜识别算法（TripletIris和ArcIris），并提供了符合IREX标准的Python和C++实现。同时开源了两种现有方法（HDBIF和CRYPTS）的IREX兼容实现，以及虹膜分割和圆估计模型。大部分方法已通过官方IREX X评估和多个学术基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次对开源虹膜识别方案进行IREX官方协议评估，并为其他团队提供符合IREX标准的C++开源参考实现，降低开源方法参与IREX评估的门槛。

Method: 提出两个神经网络方法：TripletIris（使用Triplet loss + Batch-Hard Triplet mining训练）和ArcIris（使用ArcFace loss训练）。此外提供了HDBIF（基于人眼显著驱动核的虹膜滤波算法）和CRYPTS（Fuchs隐窝检测与比较算法）的IREX兼容实现。

Result: 除CRYPTS因1:N搜索时序限制外，其余方法均通过IREX X官方评估；同时在多个学术基准数据集上进行了全面评估。

Conclusion: 提供了完整的开源虹膜识别工具链，包括新算法、现有算法的兼容实现以及配套的虹膜分割和圆估计模型，推动了开源虹膜识别研究的标准化和可复现性。

Abstract: This paper proposes two new open-source iris recognition algorithms, providing both Python and IREX-compliant C++ implementations to be submitted to the official IREX X program. This work has two primary goals: (a) to conduct the first-ever assessment of open-source iris recognition solutions according to IREX testing protocols, and (b) to offer a model C++ submission that significantly facilitates the entry of other teams' open-source methods into the IREX evaluation. The new methods consist of two Neural Networks trained with: (i) Triplet loss with Batch-Hard Triplet mining (TripletIris), and (ii) ArcFace loss (ArcIris). The paper also provides open-source IREX-compliant C++ implementations of two existing methods: (a) an iris image filtering-based algorithm utilizing human saliency-driven kernels (HDBIF), and (b) a human-interpretable algorithm for detecting and comparing Fuchs' crypts (CRYPTS). Except for CRYPTS, which faced timing constraints during 1:N search, these methods have undergone the official IREX X evaluation and have also been assessed using several popular academic benchmarks: Quality-Face/Iris Research Ensemble, Warsaw-Biobase Post-Mortem Iris, CASIA-Iris-Thousand-V4, CASIA-Iris-Lamp-V4, IIT Delhi Iris Database, IIITD Contact Lens Iris Database, NDIris3D, and Notre Dame Variable Iris Image Quality Release 2. Finally, this paper also provides open-source models for iris segmentation and circle estimation that can be incorporated into any new iris recognition method.

</details>


### [77] [Comparative Evaluation of Deep Learning Models for Fake Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20971)
*Akhitha Pakala,Mohammed Mahir Rahman,Shahzad Memon,Tauseef Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了VGG16、ResNet50、EfficientNetB0和XceptionNet四种预训练CNN在假图检测上的性能，VGG16以91%的准确率最高，但所有模型均面临数据集不平衡、过拟合和可解释性不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GAN的图像篡改技术日益复杂，对数字取证构成严重挑战，因此需要系统比较不同CNN架构在假图检测中的表现，以建立可靠的检测基线。

Method: 采用统一的预处理和训练流程（调整大小、归一化、数据增强），在真实与篡改图像数据集上对比VGG16、ResNet50、EfficientNetB0和XceptionNet四种预训练CNN架构，使用Accuracy、Precision、Recall、F1-score和ROC-AUC评估。

Result: VGG16准确率最高（91%），XceptionNet、ResNet50和EfficientNetB0均为90%；EfficientNetB0对假图灵敏度更高但对真实样本可靠性较低，存在不平衡偏差。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了可复现的基线，强调需要平衡数据集、先进的数据增强和公平性感知训练来开发可靠的假图检测系统。

Abstract: The growing sophistication of GAN-based image manipulation presents significant challenges for digital forensics. This study compares the performance of four pretrained CNN architectures including VGG16, ResNet50, EfficientNetB0, and XceptionNet for fake image detection using a unified preprocessing and training pipeline. A dataset of real and manipulated images was processed through resizing, normalization, and augmentation to address class imbalance and improve generalization. Models were evaluated using Accuracy, Precision, Recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. VGG16 achieved the highest accuracy at 91%, with XceptionNet, ResNet50, and EfficientNetB0 each reaching 90%. EfficientNetB0 showed stronger sensitivity to fake images but reduced reliability on real samples, reflecting imbalance-driven bias. Limitations include dataset imbalance, overfitting, and limited interpretability, which affect cross-domain robustness. The study provides a reproducible baseline and underscores the need for balanced datasets, advanced augmentation, and fairness-aware training to develop reliable fake image detection systems.

</details>


### [78] [Resolving Long-Tail Ambiguity in Unsupervised 3D Point Cloud Segmentation with Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20737)
*Siqi Wei,Hongbin Xu,Feng Xiao,Tian Lan,Chun Li,Ming Li,Qiuxia Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LangTail，一种利用语言模型的世界知识缓解无监督 3D 点云分割中长尾歧义问题的层级学习框架，在三个数据集上显著提升性能（+8.9~13.5 mIoU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督 3D 点云分割方法依赖纯视觉相似性的聚类学习范式，存在长尾歧义问题——少数类特征被主导类簇吸收，导致预测严重不平衡。

Method: 提出 LangTail 语言引导层级学习框架：1) 从语言模型中提取实体级语义先验，捕获平衡且细粒度的世界知识；2) 通过对比对齐将这些先验注入层级聚类框架，引导多粒度语义结构形成，防止少数类被主导类簇吸收。

Result: 在 ScanNet-v2、S3DIS、nuScenes 上分别比现有方法提升 +13.5、+12.9、+8.9 mIoU，显著改善少数类别的表示能力。

Conclusion: 语言先验能有效改善无监督 3D 点云分割中的少数类表示，缓解长尾歧义问题。

Abstract: Existing approaches for unsupervised 3D point cloud segmentation predominantly rely on a purely visual similarity-based learning-by-clustering paradigm, which suffers from a fundamental limitation: long-tail ambiguity. In such a paradigm, features of minor classes are consistently absorbed by dominant clusters, leading to severely imbalanced predictions. To address this issue, we propose LangTail, a language-guided hierarchical learning framework that leverages the balanced world knowledge encoded in language models to mitigate long-tail ambiguity in unsupervised 3D segmentation. The key idea is to establish multi-level associations between language-derived semantic priors and visually underrepresented minor classes, thereby compensating for the biased attention of purely visual clustering toward dominant classes. Specifically, LangTail first constructs an entity-level semantic prior from language models, capturing balanced and fine-grained world knowledge across categories. These priors are injected into a hierarchical clustering framework via contrastive alignment. This guides multi-granularity semantic structure formation and prevents minor classes from being absorbed by dominant clusters, yielding more discriminative representations for underrepresented categories. Extensive experiments on ScanNet-v2, S3DIS, and nuScenes demonstrate that LangTail consistently outperforms existing methods by significant margins, \ie, +13.5, +12.9, and +8.9 mIoU, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of language priors in improving the representation of minority classes in 3D point clouds. The code will be released at: https://github.com/Whisky0129/langtail_official.

</details>


### [79] [Hybrid Machine Learning Model for Forest Height Estimation from TanDEM-X and Landsat Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20997)
*Islam Mansour,Ronny Haensch,Irena Hajnsek,Konstantinos Papathanassiou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将光学Landsat数据扩展到原有的混合物理-机器学习模型中，用于从TanDEM-X干涉相干数据反演森林高度，在加蓬Lopé国家公园的实验表明RMSE降低13.5%，MAE降低16.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的混合ML-PM模型在利用TanDEM-X数据估算森林高度时，存在高度/结构与基线/地形坡度之间的模糊性，仅靠原有的特征空间无法完全解决这些歧义问题。

Method: 在原有基于TanDEM-X干涉相干测量的混合模型基础上，引入光学Landsat数据扩展特征空间，利用其提供的森林类型或结构等互补信息来进一步约束学习过程，从而提升反演准确性。

Result: 在加蓬Lopé国家公园多个TanDEM-X数据集上验证，与原始混合模型相比，RMSE下降13.5%，MAE下降16.6%，证实了多光谱输入的有效性。

Conclusion: 将光学Landsat数据与原有的物理约束机器学习模型相结合，能够有效缓解森林高度反演中的歧义问题，显著提升估算精度，验证了多源遥感数据融合在植被参数反演中的价值。

Abstract: Integrating machine learning (ML) with physical models (PM) has emerged as a promising way of retrieving geophysical parameters from remote sensing data. In this context, a ML model for estimating forest height from TanDEM-X interferometric coherence measurements has recently been proposed, that constrains the learning process through a PM. While the features used for training and inversion where selected to ensure the physical consistency of the solutions, they could not resolve all height / structure and baseline / terrain slope ambiguities in the data. To improve this, the extension of the feature space with optical Landsat data is proposed able to provide complementary information on forest type or structure. The extended model is applied and validated on several TanDEM-X acquisitions over the Gabonese Lopé national park site and assessed against airborne LiDAR measurements. Results show a 13.5% reduction in RMSE and a 16.6% reduction in MAE compared to the original hybrid model, confirming the added value of multispectral inputs.

</details>


### [80] [STAR-IOD: Scale-decoupled Topology Alignment with Pseudo-label Refinement for Remote Sensing Incremental Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20738)
*Yaoteng Zhang,Qing Zhou,Junyu Gao,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对遥感增量目标检测中类内尺度变化和标注缺失问题，提出STAR-IOD框架，包含子空间解耦拓扑蒸馏（STD）和聚类驱动伪标签生成器（CPG），在DIOR-IOD和DOTA-IOD数据集上分别超越SOTA 1.7%和2.1% mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像以连续数据流形式到达，传统检测器学习新类别时会遗忘旧类别。现有方法忽视遥感场景中显著的类内尺度变化，导致知识迁移和旧知识保持效果不佳；同时存在标注缺失问题，使模型将旧类实例误判为背景。

Method: 1) 子空间解耦拓扑蒸馏（STD）：通过显式对齐类间拓扑关系，缓解尺度变化引起的类内表征差异，传递结构知识；2) 聚类驱动伪标签生成器（CPG）：利用K-Means聚类动态识别类别特定阈值，准确区分真正例与背景噪声，缓解旧类标注缺失问题；3) 构建了两个RS-IOD基准数据集DIOR-IOD和DOTA-IOD。

Result: 在DIOR-IOD和DOTA-IOD上分别超越SOTA方法1.7%和2.1% mAP，有效缓解灾难性遗忘，在基类和新类别上均保持强检测性能。

Conclusion: STAR-IOD通过解决类内尺度变化和标注缺失两大挑战，显著提升了遥感增量目标检测的性能，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Remote sensing imagery typically arrives in the form of continuous data streams. Traditional detectors often forget previously learned categories when learning new ones; therefore, research on Remote Sensing Incremental Object Detection (RS-IOD) is of great significance. However, existing methods largely overlook the intra-class scale variations prevalent in remote sensing scenes, which undermines the effectiveness of knowledge transfer and old knowledge preservation. Moreover, RS-IOD also suffers from missing annotations, which cause the model to misclassify old-class instances as background. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework, STAR-IOD. First, we introduce a Subspace-decoupled Topology Distillation (STD) module to transfer structural knowledge, explicitly aligning inter-class topological relationships and mitigating intra-class representation discrepancies induced by scale shifts. Furthermore, we introduce the Clustering-driven Pseudo-label Generator (CPG), a plug-and-play module that leverages K-Means clustering to dynamically identify class-specific thresholds, thereby guaranteeing an accurate distinction between true positive targets and background noise and alleviating the issue of missing annotations for old classes. We also constructed two Remote Sensing Incremental Object Detection datasets, DIOR-IOD and DOTA-IOD to facilitate research on RS-IOD. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches by 1.7% and 2.1% mAP on DIOR-IOD and DOTA-IOD, respectively, effectively alleviating catastrophic forgetting while preserving strong detection performance on both base and novel classes. The code and dataset are released at: https://github.com/zyt95579/STAR-IOD.

</details>


### [81] [DySink: Dynamic Frame Sinks for Autoregressive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21028)
*Bo Ye,Xinyu Cui,Jian Zhao,Tong Wei,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DySink通过检索动态历史帧替代固定早期帧作为长程锚点，解决了自回归长视频生成中上下文僵化与注意力坍塌问题，在分钟级视频上提升了动态程度与时间质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有界内存流方法固定保留早期帧作为长期锚点，当视觉状态大幅偏离时，早期帧成为过时线索，且丢弃了可能更相关的中间历史，导致生成偏向旧有信息、注意力坍塌。

Method: 提出DySink框架：维护一个紧凑的记忆库，通过检索选出视觉相关的历史帧作为动态帧sink；耦合一个sink异常门控，检测检索上下文上过高的头部间共识，抑制易坍塌的上下文。

Result: 在分钟级长视频上的实验表明，DySink在多个强基线上持续提升了动态程度（dynamic degree），同时获得了更高的时间质量。

Conclusion: 动态检索历史帧作为sink比固定早期帧更适应视觉状态变化，能有效避免注意力坍塌，提升长视频生成质量。

Abstract: Autoregressive long video generation often adopts bounded-memory streaming for efficiency, typically combining local windows for short-term continuity with static early-frame sinks as long-range anchors. However, this fixed allocation keeps early frames cached even when the current visual state has substantially diverged from them, while discarding potentially more relevant intermediate history. As a result, the retained long-range context may become less adaptive and bias generation toward outdated cues; in severe cases, RoPE-induced phase re-alignment can homogenize inter-head attention and cause sink collapse, where content regresses toward sink frames. We propose DySink, a retrieval-based framework that maintains a compact memory bank and selects visually relevant historical frames as dynamic frame sinks. DySink couples adaptive retrieval with a sink anomaly gate, which detects excessive inter-head consensus over retrieved context and suppresses collapse-prone context. Experiments on minute-long videos show that DySink consistently improves dynamic degree over strong baselines while also achieving higher temporal quality. The code and model weights will be released at https://github.com/yebo0216best/DySink.

</details>


### [82] [Draw2Think: Harnessing Geometry Reasoning through Constraint Engine Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20743)
*Juncheng Hu,Jiawei Du,Xin Zhang,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Draw2Think提出将几何推理从潜在空间外化到GeoGebra约束引擎上的框架，通过"提议-绘制-验证"循环实现可审计的几何推理，在多项基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在几何问题上的推理过程是隐式的、不可验证的，基于像素渲染或一次性脚本的外化方法无法提供精确的、每步可验证的几何保证。

Method: 提出Draw2Think框架，将几何推理重构为与GeoGebra约束引擎的代理交互过程。在"提议-绘制-验证"循环中，模型将假设外化到可执行画布上，测量精确几何量，并将结构化观测反馈回模型，使后续推理基于已检查的画布状态进行。

Result: 在GeoGoal上达到95.9%谓词级和84.0%严格问题级构造检查通过率；在平面/立体几何基准上分别提升4.1%/16.4%的结果准确率；在GenExam-math上获得68.2%/90.5%的严格/宽松渲染分数。

Conclusion: 通过将几何推理外化到可执行约束引擎，Draw2Think实现了两个可独立审计的属性：模型级的构造保真度和引擎级的测量忠实度，为几何推理的可验证性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models solve geometry problems with rising accuracy, yet their intermediate states remain latent and unverifiable: a relation expressed in textual reasoning or drawing code carries no guarantee that a constraint-satisfying configuration realizes it. We observe that existing externalization methods based on rendered pixels or one-shot scripts fail to provide exact, per-action geometric guarantees. Enforcing geometric relations by algebraic definition closes this gap: the workspace becomes a constraint-checked evolving canvas. We present Draw2Think, a framework that recasts geometric reasoning from latent spatial inference into agentic interaction with the GeoGebra constraint engine. In a Propose-Draw-Verify loop, Draw2Think externalizes hypotheses onto an executable canvas, measures exact geometric quantities, and feeds structured observations back to the model, so subsequent reasoning proceeds from checked canvas state grounded by the shared workspace. This externalization makes two properties separately auditable: model-level Construction Fidelity (whether the canvas realizes the intended configuration) and engine-level Measurement Faithfulness (exact values and relations from canvas constraints). Across construction, outcome, and rendering evaluations, Draw2Think builds canvases that pass 95.9% predicate-level and 84.0% strict problem-level construction checks on GeoGoal, improves outcome accuracy by up to 4.1%/16.4% on planar/solid benchmarks, and attains 68.2%/90.5% strict/relaxed rendering scores on GenExam-math. Project page is available at https://draw2think.github.io/

</details>


### [83] [SpineContextResUNet: A Computationally Efficient Residual UNet for Spine CT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20760)
*K S Nithurshen,Saurabh J. Shigwan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpineContextResUNet，一种轻量级3D残差U-Net，用于CT脊柱分割。通过上下文模块（并行多膨胀卷积）捕获长程依赖，在VerSe2020和CTSpine1K上分别达到88.17%和88.13% Dice，参数量仅1.7M，可在低端硬件（8GB RAM）上运行，远超受限于资源的SwinUNETR和TotalSegmentator。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer或大规模集成的脊柱分割方法依赖大量GPU资源，难以在资源受限的临床环境或边缘设备上部署。作者旨在设计一种计算高效、可在低端硬件上运行的准确脊柱分割模型。

Method: 提出SpineContextResUNet，基于3D Residual U-Net架构，核心是轻量级Context Block，采用并行多膨胀卷积捕获长程空间依赖，避免RNN高延迟和自注意力机制的高内存开销。模型参数量仅约1.7M。

Result: 在VerSe2020上达到88.17% Dice，在CTSpine1K上达到88.13% Dice。在Intel Core i5、8GB RAM的商用硬件上可稳健推理，而TotalSegmentator因内存耗尽失败。被压缩至相同参数量的SwinUNETR性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: SpineContextResUNet在保证高分割精度的同时，实现了极低资源消耗，适合点-of-care诊断和边缘平台（如Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano）部署，是Transformer方法在资源受限场景下的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Automated segmentation of the vertebral column in Computed Tomography (CT) scans is a prerequisite for pathological assessment and surgical planning. However, state-of-the-art methods, particularly those based on Transformers or large-scale ensembles, demand substantial GPU resources, creating a barrier for clinical adoption in resource-constrained environments or on edge devices. To address this, we introduce SpineContextResUNet, a computationally efficient 3D Residual U-Net designed for rapid spinal localization. Our architecture integrates a lightweight Context Block that employs parallel multi-dilated convolutions to capture long-range anatomical dependencies without the high latency of Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) or the memory overhead of Self-Attention mechanisms. Extensive validation on two public benchmarks, VerSe2020 and CTSpine1K, demonstrates that our model achieves a Dice score of 88.17% and 88.13% respectively. To evaluate performance under strict hardware constraints, we compared our model against a bottlenecked SwinUNETR scaled to match our ~1.7M hardware footprint. While the constrained Transformer suffers severe performance degradation due to a lack of spatial inductive biases in a limited-data regime, our CNN-based approach successfully maintains high accuracy. Crucially, heavy baselines like TotalSegmentator fail due to memory exhaustion on commodity hardware (Intel Core i5, 8GB RAM), our model performs robust inference, making it a viable solution for point-of-care diagnostics and deployment on edge platforms like the Nvidia Jetson Orin Nano.

</details>


### [84] [Diffuse to Detect: Bi-Level Sample Rebalancing with Pseudo-Label Diffusion for Point-Supervised Infrared Small-Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20766)
*Zhu Liu,Yuanhang Yao,Ping Qian,Zihang Chen,Risheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理启发的点监督红外小目标检测框架，通过热扩散原理将单点标注扩展为可靠伪掩码，并设计双层双更新机制联合优化检测器权重、样本权重和扩散参数，实现了5倍标注加速和仅用30%训练数据即可达到可比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点监督方法在红外小目标检测中面临两个瓶颈：杂波低对比度场景下伪标签演化不稳定，以及严重的样本分布不平衡问题。现有方法难以同时解决这两个耦合问题。

Method: 提出物理启发的标注策略（利用热辐射与热扩散的一致性扩展单点标签为伪掩码），以及双层双更新框架：上层元分类器动态预测样本级损失权重，下层可微扩散模块利用检测反馈优化伪标签，实现训练与超参数优化的自适应交互。

Result: 多数据集实验表明：标注速度提升5倍，检测精度优异，仅用30%训练数据即可达到与全监督相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了物理启发的点监督范式在红外小目标检测中的高效性和实用性，有效解决了伪标签不稳定和样本不平衡问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Point supervision has become a scalable solution to address dense annotation for infrared small target detection, but its performance is limited by two coupled bottlenecks: unstable pseudo-label evolution in cluttered, low-contrast infrared imagery and severe sample-distribution imbalance. In this paper, we present a more adaptive and stable framework to address these issues. Leveraging the intrinsic consistency between thermal radiation patterns and heat diffusion, we propose a physics-induced annotation strategy that expands single-point labels into reliable pseudo-masks. To further enhance supervision and alleviate sample imbalance, we develop a bi-level dual-update framework that jointly optimizes detector weights, sample weights, and diffusion parameters. A meta-classifier dynamically predicts sample-wise loss weights, while a differentiable diffusion module refines pseudo-labels with detection feedback, enabling adaptive interaction between training and hyperparameter optimization. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate five-fold annotation acceleration, superior detection accuracy, and comparable performance with 30% of the training data, validating the efficiency and practicality of our approach. Our code is available at https://github.com/yuanhang-yao/diffuse-to-detect.

</details>


### [85] [SAM-Sode: Towards Faithful Explanations for Tiny Bacteria Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21186)
*Wanying Tan,Shuo Yan,Dazhi Huang,Yazheng Liu,Zili Shao,Rufeng Chen,Hechang Chen,Mude Shi,Tianxing Ji,Sihong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAM-Sode框架，利用基础模型SAM3的先验知识，将特征归因图转化为几何感知提示，通过空间细化和形态重建实现小目标检测的可解释性，并引入双重约束机制进行实例级去噪，有效抑制背景冗余，提升微小细菌检测的决策透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解释方法在微小细菌检测中面临前景边界模糊和特征归因扩散问题，无法提供逻辑连贯的形态学证据，需要一种能克服稀疏形态特征和复杂背景干扰的可解释AI框架。

Method: 提出SAM-Sode框架：(1) 将初始特征归因图转化为几何感知提示，利用SAM3基础模型的先验知识进行空间细化和形态重建；(2) 引入基于物理意义和几何对齐的双重约束机制进行实例级去噪，生成符合专家直觉的一致性解释。

Result: 在自建的含复杂电路背景的细菌数据集（2524张图像）及其他公开数据集上，该方法有效抑制了背景冗余，显著提升了小目标检测的决策透明度。

Conclusion: SAM-Sode通过几何感知提示和双重约束机制，有效解决了微小目标检测中可解释性不足的问题，为临床辅助诊断提供了更可靠的形态学证据支持。

Abstract: Interpretability in object detection provides crucial confidence support for clinical auxiliary diagnosis. However, in tiny bacteria detection, traditional explanation methods often suffer from blurred foreground boundaries and diffuse feature attribution due to the extreme sparsity of target morphological features and severe interference from complex backgrounds. Such limitations hinder the provision of logically coherent morphological evidence. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel eXplainable AI (XAI) framework, SAM-Sode. The framework innovatively transforms initial feature attribution maps into geometry-aware prompts, leveraging the prior knowledge of the foundation model (SAM3) to achieve spatial refinement and morphological reconstruction of the explanatory mappings. Furthermore, we introduce a dual-constraint mechanism based on physical significance and geometric alignment to perform instance-level denoising, generating coherent explanations that better align with human expert intuition. Experimental results on our self-constructed bacteria dataset with complex circuit backgrounds (containing 2,524 images) and other public datasets demonstrate that the proposed method effectively suppresses background redundancy and significantly enhances the decision-making transparency of tiny object detection.

</details>


### [86] [VIHD: Visual Intervention-based Hallucination Detection for Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20772)
*Jiayi Chen,Benteng Ma,Zehui Liao,Winston Chong,Yasmeen George,Jianfei Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VIHD，一种通过视觉Token掩码校准语义熵来检测医学多模态大模型幻觉的方法，在三个医学VQA基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法多依赖外部启发式扰动，忽略了解码过程中生成token与视觉token之间的内部跨模态依赖关系，导致检测效果不佳。

Method: 通过视觉依赖探测（VDP）定位视觉主导的解码层，执行视觉干预解码（VID）进行Token掩码以校准语义分布，并计算校准语义熵（CSE）作为幻觉信号。

Result: 在三个医学VQA基准和两个医学MLLM上，VIHD一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 细粒度的视觉依赖关系对于幻觉检测至关重要，VIHD有效利用了跨模态内部依赖性提升检测性能。

Abstract: While medical Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promise in assisting diagnosis, they still frequently generate hallucinated responses that appear linguistically plausible but lack visual evidence. Such hallucinations pose risks to clinical decision-making and necessitate effective detection. Existing introspective detection methods primarily perform uncertainty estimation or logical verification by analyzing model responses conditioned on original or perturbed inputs. However, such external perturbations are often heuristic and context-agnostic, which overlooks the internal cross-modal dependency between generated tokens and related visual tokens during decoding. To address this issue, we propose VIHD, a Visual Intervention-based Hallucination Detection method that leverages targeted visual token masking to calibrate semantic entropy for more effective hallucination detection. VIHD locates visually dominant decoder layers via Visual Dependency Probing (VDP), executes Visual Intervention Decoding (VID) via token masking to calibrate the semantic distribution, and quantifies the resulting Calibrated Semantic Entropy (CSE) as a reliable hallucination signal. Extensive experiments on three medical VQA benchmarks with two medical MLLMs demonstrate that VIHD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, underscoring the importance of fine-grained visual dependency for hallucination detection. The code will be available at https://github.com/Jiayi-Chen-AU/VIHD

</details>


### [87] [RePCM: Region-Specific and Phenotype-Adaptive Bi-Ventricular Cardiac Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21237)
*Xuan Yang,Xiaohan Yuan,Hao Li,Lingyu Chen,Yanan Liu,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RePCM方法，通过两阶段框架（运动描述子学习+分区条件VAE）从单张舒张末期网格生成完整心动周期序列，在三个数据集上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏周期运动对量化区域功能至关重要，但密集网格序列难以获取。现有生成模型过度平滑，无法捕捉区域特异性和疾病差异。

Method: 两阶段方法：Stage I通过重建网络学习顶点运动描述子并进行聚类得到数据驱动的功能分区；Stage II采用区域特异性注入模块和表型自适应混合专家先验（条件于ED形状），实现区域同步交换并保留局部动态。

Result: 在三个涵盖不同心血管疾病的数据集上，几何和功能指标一致提升，区域特异性动态得到更好保留。

Conclusion: RePCM有效解决了从单帧到全周期心脏运动合成的区域过平滑问题，验证了数据驱动分区和表型自适应建模的有效性。

Abstract: Cardiac motion over a cardiac cycle is crucial for quantifying regional function and is strongly affected by cardiovascular diseases. Since temporally dense mesh sequences are difficult to obtain in practice, we focus on leveraging the more accessible end-diastolic frame to infer a full-cycle sequence. Due to strong regional and disease-specific differences, traditional methods often oversmooth the data by relying on generative models that are optimized for global patterns. To address this problem, we propose Region-Aware and Phenotype-Adaptive Bi-Ventricular Cardiac Motion Synthesis (RePCM) for single frame Bi-ventricular mesh motion completion. In Stage I, a reconstruction network learns vertex wise motion descriptors and clustering yields a data driven functional partition, providing an explicit motion derived region structure. In Stage II, a Region-Specific Injection Module enforces masked, synchronized region exchange within a conditional VAE, preserving localized specific dynamics and restricting cross-region mixing. A Phenotype-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts prior conditioned on ED shape uses anatomy-guided cues to model latent motion trends and capture inter-disease variability. Experiments on three datasets covering different cardiovascular diseases show consistent gains in geometric and functional metrics and improved preservation of region specific dynamics.

</details>


### [88] [AttriStory: Fine-grained Attribute Realization for Visual Storytelling with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20777)
*Manogna Sreenivas,Rohit Kumar,Soma Biswas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AttriStory基准和AttriLoss模块，在视觉故事生成中实现细粒度属性（颜色、纹理等）的忠实渲染，无需修改现有模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉故事生成方法虽能保持角色一致性，但无法系统性地确保衣物颜色、纹理等细粒度属性被准确渲染。

Method: 1) 构建200个多场景故事（10种艺术风格）的AttriStory基准；2) 提出AttriLoss损失函数，在去噪早期阶段优化交叉注意力图，增强属性-对象对齐并抑制虚假关联。

Result: AttriLoss在所有基线上均取得一致改进，能即插即用兼容现有故事生成流程。

Conclusion: 将属性实现定位为与角色一致性互补的独立维度，推动了细粒度属性控制的故事生成发展。

Abstract: Visual storytelling with diffusion models has made impressive strides in maintaining character consistency across narrative scenes. However, a critical gap remains: while these methods ensure a character remains consistent across scenes, they provide no systematic method to ensure if fine-grained attributes such as color and textures of clothing, accessories are faithfully rendered in the generated images. Towards this goal, we introduce AttriStory, a benchmark enabling attribute realization in visual storytelling. We curate 200 multi-scene stories across 10 distinct artistic styles using Large Language Model. Each scene is constructed with detailed attribute specifications to enable rich visual narratives. Further, to address attribute realization, we propose a plug-and-play latent optimization module that operates during early denoising steps, when the model establishes structural and semantic content. We achieve this through AttriLoss objective designed to maximize alignment between the cross-attention maps for desired attribute-object pairs while suppressing spurious associations, guiding models to localize attributes correctly. This approach operates orthogonally to existing consistency mechanisms, integrating seamlessly with current story generation pipelines without requiring architectural modifications. Our experiments demonstrate consistent improvements on incorporating AttriLoss across all baselines. This work positions attribute realization as a distinct, complementary dimension of visual storytelling, alongside character consistency, advancing the field toward fine-grained attribute-controlled story generation. Project-page:https://manogna-s.github.io/attristory/

</details>


### [89] [Findings of the Counter Turing Test: AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20787)
*Rajarshi Roy,Nasrin Imanpour,Ashhar Aziz,Shashwat Bajpai,Gurpreet Singh,Shwetangshu Biswas,Kapil Wanaskar,Parth Patwa,Subhankar Ghosh,Shreyas Dixit,Nilesh Ranjan Pal,Vipula Rawte,Ritvik Garimella,Amitava Das,Amit Sheth,Vasu Sharma,Aishwarya Naresh Reganti,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Defactify 4.0研讨会提出的反图灵测试（CT2）用于AI生成图像检测。竞赛包含两个任务：图像真假二分类（F1>0.83）和识别具体生成模型（最高F1仅0.4986），使用了包含50,000张合成图像的MS COCOAI数据集，表明检测AI图像可行但识别具体模型仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI技术（如Stable Diffusion、DALL-E、Midjourney）快速发展，使得合成视觉内容日益逼真，带来了 misinformation、disinformation 和有偏内容生成等严重挑战，亟需有效的AI生成图像检测方法。

Method: 举办Defactify 4.0研讨会及反图灵测试（CT2）竞赛；构建包含50,000张多模型合成图像的MS COCOAI数据集；设置两个任务（二分类与模型溯源）；参赛者采用CNN、ViT、频域分析、对比学习、多模态等多种检测策略。

Result: 二分类任务F1-score > 0.83，表现良好；模型溯源任务最高F1-score仅0.4986，准确率较低。

Conclusion: 虽然AI生成图像的整体检测可行，但识别具体生成模型仍面临显著挑战，未来需加强模型指纹识别、对抗鲁棒性和实时检测机制的研究。

Abstract: The rapid advancements in generative AI technologies, such as Stable Diffusion, DALL-E, and Midjourney, have significantly transformed the creation of synthetic visual content. While these models enable innovation across industries, they also pose serious challenges, including misinformation, disinformation, and biased content generation. The increasing realism of AI-generated images makes their detection a pressing concern for researchers, policymakers, and industry stakeholders.
  In this paper, we present the findings of the Defactify 4.0 workshop, which introduced the Counter Turing Test (CT2) for AI-Generated Image Detection. The competition consisted of two key tasks: (1) binary classification of images as either AI-generated or real and (2) identification of the specific generative model responsible for an AI-generated image. To facilitate this, we developed the MS COCOAI dataset, consisting of 50,000 synthetic images from multiple generative models alongside real-world images from the MS COCO dataset.
  Participants employed diverse detection strategies, including convolutional neural networks (CNNs), Vision Transformers (ViTs), frequency-based analysis, contrastive learning, and multimodal techniques. The results demonstrated that while AI-generated images can be detected with high accuracy (F1-score > 0.83), identifying the exact model used remains significantly more challenging (highest F1-score: 0.4986). These findings highlight the need for improved model fingerprinting, adversarial robustness, and real-time detection mechanisms.

</details>


### [90] [MONET: A Massive, Open, Non-redundant and Enriched Text-to-image dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21272)
*Benjamin Aubin,Gonzalo Iñaki Quintana,Onur Tasar,Sanjeev Sreetharan,Urszula Czerwinska,Damien Henry,Clément Chadebec*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MONET是一个包含约1.049亿图像-文本对的开源数据集，从29亿原始对中通过安全过滤、去重、重新标注等多阶段处理得到，训练出的4B参数扩散模型取得了有竞争力的生成质量评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量、带详细标注的大规模图文数据集成本高、获取复杂，阻碍了文本到图像领域的开放可复现研究。

Method: 从29亿原始图文对出发，经过安全过滤、领域过滤、精确和近似去重、多视觉语言模型重新标注（覆盖短到长描述），并合成增强样本；最终提供预计算嵌入和注释以加速下游使用。

Result: 基于MONET训练的4B参数潜在扩散模型在GenEval和DPG评分上达到有竞争力的水平。

Conclusion: MONET降低了大规模、可复现文本到图像研究的门槛。

Abstract: Training large text-to-image models requires high-quality, curated datasets with diverse content and detailed captions. Yet the cost and complexity of collecting, filtering, deduplicating, and re-captioning such corpora at scale hinders open and reproducible research in the field. We introduce MONET, an open Apache 2.0 dataset of approx. 104.9M image--text pairs collected from 2.9B raw pairs across heterogeneous open sources through successive stages of safety filtering, domain-based filtering, exact and near-duplicate removal, and re-captioning with multiple vision-language models covering short to long-form descriptions, and further augmented with synthetically generated samples. Each image is shipped with pre-computed embeddings and annotations to accelerate downstream use. To validate the effectiveness of MONET, we train a 4B-parameter latent diffusion model exclusively on it and reach competitive GenEval and DPG scores, demonstrating that our dataset lowers the barrier to large-scale, reproducible text-to-image research.

</details>


### [91] [What Semantics Survive the Connector? Diagnosing VLM-to-DiT Alignment in Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20795)
*Hangyu Lin,Chao Wen,Chengming Xu,Jianxiong Gao,Jiangning Zhang,Xiaobin Hu,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE-Edit诊断数据集和系统化评估协议，揭示了当前基于Flow Matching的视频编辑模型中VLM-to-DiT对齐过程会严重退化细粒度结构语义，推翻了"无损语义迁移"的假设，证明该对齐是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑模型广泛使用VLM前置模块，假设连接器能无缝对齐VLM的多模态推理与DiT的文本嵌入空间。但作者怀疑这种对齐存在语义瓶颈，会退化细粒度结构变量。由于端到端评估难以区分对齐失败与生成错误，且天然数据集缺乏解耦标注，导致这一问题难以被深入研究。

Method: 1. 提出基于视频合成的受控数据处理流程，构建名为TRACE-Edit的诊断数据集，聚焦关系型编辑任务；2. 设计全面的诊断协议，分析现有视频编辑模型中meta-query和connector两个关键设计；3. 对四种代表性模型案例进行系统性评估。

Result: 系统性评估发现，细粒度结构语义在对齐过程中严重退化，推翻了"无损语义迁移"的既有假设，明确了VLM-to-DiT对齐是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来多模态对齐架构提供了新的诊断基础，表明需要重新思考VLM与DiT之间的对齐机制，而非简单假设连接器能实现无损语义传递。

Abstract: Flow matching based video generative models have been increasingly relying on prepended Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to handle complex, instruction-based video editing. The prevailing assumption underlying this paradigm is that a connector module can seamlessly align the VLM's rich multi-modal reasoning with the original text embedding space of DiTs. However, we hypothesize that this alignment acts as a severe semantic bottleneck, degrading fine-grained structural variables. Verifying this is challenging, as end-to-end evaluations conflate alignment failures with generation errors, and natural datasets lack disentangled annotations. To rigorously investigate this, we propose a controlled data processing pipeline based on video composition that results in TRACE-Edit, a diagnostic dataset focusing on relation-based editing. Leveraging this dataset, we propose a comprehensive diagnostic protocol to analyze two important designs of meta-query and connector in the existing video editing models. Systematic evaluation of four representative model cases reveals that fine-grained structural semantics can be severely degraded during alignment. Our findings overturn the assumption of lossless semantic transfer, identifying the VLM-to-DiT alignment as a major bottleneck and providing a new diagnostic foundation for future multi-modal alignment architectures.

</details>


### [92] [Deformba: Vision State Space Model with Adaptive State Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21308)
*Hongyu Ke,Jack Morris,Yongkang Liu,Satoshi Kitai,Kentaro Oguchi,Yi Ding,Haoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Deformba 提出一种上下文自适应方法，动态增强空间结构信息并保持SSM线性复杂度，同时支持多模态融合，在2D和3D视觉任务上均取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉SSM依赖于手工设计的固定扫描方法，增加了复杂性；且SSM固有的因果性和自引用特性阻碍了其在不同信息流之间进行查询式交互（如多视图3D融合）的应用。

Method: 提出Deformba，一种上下文自适应方法，动态增强空间结构信息，同时保持SSM的线性复杂度，并支持类似交叉注意力的多模态融合机制。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、分割等2D任务以及BEV感知等3D任务上，Deformba均取得了强性能表现。

Conclusion: Deformba有效解决了视觉SSM的两大核心局限，验证了其在不同视觉感知任务中的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) have emerged as a powerful and efficient alternative to Transformers, demonstrating linear-time complexity and exceptional sequence modeling capabilities. However, their application to vision tasks remains challenging. First, existing vision SSMs largely depend on manually designed fixed scanning methods to flatten image patches into sequences, which imposes predefined geometric structures and increases the complexity. Second, the broader adoption of vision SSMs is hindered in domains that require query-based interactions between distinct information streams. This is a result of the inherently causal and self-referential nature of SSMs designed for 1D sequence modeling tasks. This fusion mechanism is indispensable for critical perception tasks such as multi-view 3D fusion. To address these limitations, we propose Deformba, a context adaptive method that dynamically augments the spatial structural information while maintaining the linear complexity of SSMs. Deformba also allows multi-modal fusion like cross attention. To demonstrate the effectiveness and general applicability of Deformba, we test its performance on general 2D vision tasks such as image classification, object detection, and segmentation, as well as 3D vision tasks like BEV perception. Extensive experiments show that Deformba achieves strong performance across various visual perception benchmarks.

</details>


### [93] [OlmoEarth v1.1: A more efficient family of OlmoEarth models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20804)
*Gabriel Tseng,Yawen Zhang,Favyen Bastani,Henry Herzog,Joseph Redmon,Hadrien Sablon,Piper Wolters,Patrick Alan Johnson,Christopher Wilhelm,Patrick Beukema*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一系列改进方案，在保持模型性能的前提下，将OlmoEarth模型的训练GPU时长减少1.7倍，推理MACs减少2.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低OlmoEarth系列模型在训练和推理阶段的计算成本，同时维持模型整体性能。

Method: 提出一组针对OlmoEarth家族的改进措施，具体改进细节未在摘要中详述。

Result: Base模型训练GPU时长减少1.7倍；Sentinel-2任务推理MACs减少2.9倍；模型整体性能保持不变。

Conclusion: 所提出的改进方案能够有效降低计算成本，且不影响模型性能，并开源了训练代码。

Abstract: We present a set of improvements to the OlmoEarth family. These improvements allow us to cut compute costs during training ($1.7 \times$ reduction in GPU hours required to train our Base models) and inference ($2.9\times$ reductions in MACs on Sentinel-2 tasks), while maintaining the models' overall performance. All training code is available at github.com/allenai/olmoearth_pretrain.

</details>


### [94] [Decomposing Subject-Driven Image Generation via Intermediate Structural Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20807)
*Hanzhong Guo,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架，先预测Canny边缘图再渲染最终图像，以解决主体驱动生成中高频身份细节（如标志、图案、文字）保留问题，并构建了10万对文本感知数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主体驱动文本到图像生成方法在RGB空间直接操作，导致重大编辑时高频细节（如标志、图案、文字）严重退化。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段预测Canny边缘图（解耦结构与外观），第二阶段基于源外观和预测结构条件渲染最终图像；同时构建了10万对文本感知数据集，采用全自动管道实现跨视图文本一致性。

Result: 通过GPT-4.1评估和知识蒸馏实验，该方法在选定基线上取得明显提升。

Conclusion: 中间结构预测是实现高保真主体驱动生成的有效途径。

Abstract: Subject-driven text-to-image generation still struggles to preserve high-frequency identity details such as logos, patterns, and text. Existing methods typically operate directly in RGB space, which often leads to detail degradation under substantial edits. We propose a two-stage framework that decouples structure from appearance by first predicting a Canny map and then rendering the final image conditioned on both the source appearance and the predicted structure. To improve text handling, we further introduce a fully automatic pipeline that constructs a 100k-pair text-aware dataset with cross-view textual consistency. Experiments, including GPT-4.1-based evaluation and a knowledge distillation study, show clear gains over selected baselines and suggest that intermediate structural prediction is an effective route for high-fidelity subject-driven generation. Our dataset and code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [95] [Ordering Matters: Rank-Aware Selective Fusion for Blended Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21417)
*Junghyun Lee,Hyunseo Kim,Hanna Jang,Junhyug Noh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种排序感知的多编码器融合框架，通过注意力门控模块选择最有效的编码器、解耦情绪预测为存在头和显著头、并引入无监督域适应，在BlEmoRE挑战中取得第二名，证明了选择性融合在混合情绪识别中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合情绪识别因情绪常以细微、重叠的多模态线索混合表达而具有挑战性，现有方法未能有效处理多编码器的选择性融合及域偏移问题。

Method: 1) 将异构编码器特征投影到共享隐空间；2) 注意力门控模块估计样本级别的编码器重要性，仅融合top-n最有效的编码器；3) 解耦预测为存在头(presence)和显著头(salience)，通过概率级融合对齐；4) 引入无伪标签的特征级无监督域适应提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在BlEmoRE挑战中优于单个强编码器和朴素多编码器融合基线，最终获得竞赛第二名。

Conclusion: 排序感知的选择性融合框架能有效处理混合情绪识别中多模态线索的重叠与细微差异，提升识别性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Blended emotion recognition is challenging because emotions are often expressed as mixtures of subtle and overlapping multimodal cues rather than a single dominant signal. We propose a rank-aware multi-encoder framework that selectively combines complementary representations from diverse pre-extracted video and audio encoders. Our method projects heterogeneous encoder features into a shared latent space, estimates sample-wise encoder importance through an attention-based gating module, and fuses only the top-n most informative encoders. To better model blended emotions, we decouple prediction into presence and salience heads and align them through probability-level fusion. We further incorporate feature-level unsupervised domain adaptation without pseudo-labeling to improve robustness under distribution shift. Experiments on the BlEmoRE challenge show that the proposed framework outperforms strong individual encoders and naïve multi-encoder fusion baselines. Our final system ranked 2nd in the competition, supporting the effectiveness of rank-aware selective fusion for fine-grained blended emotion recognition.

</details>


### [96] [Spatial Gram Alignment for Ultra-High-Resolution Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20808)
*Jinjin Zhang,Xiefan Guo,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Spatial Gram Alignment (SGA)框架，通过对齐生成特征与基础模型特征的自相似性，解决超高清图像合成中直接特征蒸馏导致的可学习性-保真度冲突，实现全局结构与局部细节的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示对齐方法在超高清分辨率下直接进行逐块特征蒸馏会扰动预训练潜在流形，导致生成质量下降，存在可学习性与保真度之间的冲突。

Method: 提出SGA框架，通过对齐生成特征与视觉基础模型特征的内在自相似性（而非直接特征对齐），施加非侵入式空间约束，同时保留LDM原生生成目标以维持微观像素保真度。

Result: 在超高清文本到图像合成任务上达到SOTA性能，有效调和了全局结构完整性与细粒度视觉细节之间的冲突。

Conclusion: SGA通过空间Gram对齐策略，在不扰动预训练潜在流形的前提下引入视觉先验，是一种通用且有效的超高清图像合成框架。

Abstract: Modern ultra-high-resolution image synthesis relies heavily on the robust generative capacity of large-scale pre-trained Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs). While recent representation alignment methods have proven effective by distilling visual priors from foundation models (e.g., SAM or DINO) into generative latent features, scaling these approaches to pre-trained LDMs at extreme resolutions exposes a critical learnability-fidelity conflict. Specifically, forcing direct patch-wise feature distillation inherently perturbs the pre-trained latent manifold, ultimately leading to generation degradation. To address this bottleneck, we propose Spatial Gram Alignment (SGA), a novel framework that explicitly leverages the representation priors of vision foundation models while preserving the native generative capacity of LDMs. Moving beyond restrictive direct alignment, SGA imposes a non-invasive spatial constraint by aligning the internal self-similarities of the generative features with those of the foundation priors. This spatial constraint effectively establishes macroscopic structural coherence, while the native generative objectives retain the microscopic pixel-level fidelity inherent to the original LDMs. Notably, this versatile strategy integrates seamlessly across both intermediate diffusion features and VAE latents within pre-trained LDMs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SGA achieves state-of-the-art performance for ultra-high-resolution text-to-image synthesis, yielding an effective reconciliation between global structural integrity and fine-grained visual details. Code is available at https://github.com/zhang0jhon/SGA.

</details>


### [97] [TempGlitch: Evaluating Vision-Language Models for Temporal Glitch Detection in Gameplay Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21443)
*Yakun Yu,Ashley Wiens,Adrián Barahona-Ríos,Benedict Wilkins,Saman Zadtootaghaj,Nabajeet Barman,Cor-Paul Bezemer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有VLM视频游戏故障检测研究主要关注空间故障（单帧可见），忽略了时间故障（需跨帧变化才能发现）。作者提出TempGlitch基准数据集，涵盖5种时间故障类型，评测12个VLM模型后发现它们表现接近随机，密集采样和大模型无法可靠解决这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频游戏故障检测研究将故障视为静态视觉异常，仅从单帧检测，忽略了时间维度上的故障。作者通过初步实验证实时间故障对VLM更具挑战性，因此需要构建专门评估时间故障检测能力的基准。

Method: 构建TempGlitch基准数据集：包含5种时间故障类型，提供平衡的样本以及配对的无故障视频用于二分类评估。在12个开源和闭源VLM上，采用多种帧采样策略进行系统评测。

Result: 当前VLM在TempGlitch上表现接近随机（几乎等于瞎猜），要么过于保守漏掉大多数故障，要么过于敏感将正常视频误判为故障。更密集的帧采样和更大的模型规模无法可靠改善性能。

Conclusion: 时间故障检测是VLM尚未解决的重要挑战，TempGlitch为评估时间推理、鲁棒游戏理解和自动化故障检测提供了聚焦的测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being explored for video game quality assurance, especially gameplay glitch detection. Most existing evaluations, however, treat glitches as static visual anomalies, asking models to detect failures from a single frame. We argue that this framing misses a key distinction: some glitches are spatial and visible in an isolated frame, whereas others are temporal and become evident only through changes across ordered frames. A preliminary study confirms this gap, showing that temporal glitches are substantially harder for VLMs to detect than spatial ones. To enable systematic evaluation of this underexplored setting, we introduce TempGlitch, a controlled gameplay video benchmark for temporal glitch detection. TempGlitch covers five temporal glitch types with balanced per-category samples, together with paired glitch-free videos that enable reliable binary evaluation. We evaluate 12 proprietary and open-weight VLMs across multiple frame-sampling settings. Our results show that current VLMs remain near chance on TempGlitch, often collapsing into either overly conservative behavior that misses most glitches or overly sensitive behavior that flags clean videos as glitchy. Moreover, denser frame sampling and larger model size do not reliably resolve these failures. TempGlitch provides a focused testbed for temporal reasoning, robust gameplay understanding, and automated glitch detection with VLMs. Code and data are available at the project website.

</details>


### [98] [OSGNet with MLLM Reranking @ Ego4D Episodic Memory Challenge 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20818)
*Yisen Feng,Leigang Qu,Haoyu Zhang,Qiaohui Chu,Meng Liu,Xuemeng Song,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于重排序的框架，结合OSGNet候选段与MLLM推理能力，在Ego4D赛道中取得双料冠军。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频时间定位任务中，传统方法效率高但推理能力弱，MLLM推理强但效率低。如何兼顾两者是核心动机。

Method: 先由OSGNet生成候选时间片段，再利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对候选段重排序，选出与查询最匹配的片段。

Result: 在Ego4D Episodic Memory Challenge的Natural Language Queries和GoalStep两个赛道均取得第一名。

Conclusion: 重排序框架能有效融合传统定位模型的高召回与MLLM的强语义理解，是长视频时间定位的有效范式。

Abstract: In this report, we present our champion solutions for the Natural Language Queries and GoalStep tracks of the Ego4D Episodic Memory Challenge at CVPR 2026. Both tracks require accurately localizing temporal segments from long untrimmed egocentric videos. To address these tasks, we propose a reranking-based framework that effectively leverages the strong video-language reasoning capability of multimodal large language model (MLLM) while preserving the efficiency and candidate recall of conventional localization pipelines. Specifically, we first obtain a set of candidate segments from existing localization model OSGNet, and then employ MLLM to select the segment that best matches the given query, thereby refining the final prediction. Ultimately, our method achieved first place in both the Natural Language Queries and GoalStep tracks. Our code can be found at https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/CVPR25-OSGNet.

</details>


### [99] [AIR: Amortized Image Reconstruction Framework for Self-Supervised Feed-Forward 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20820)
*Zhaojie Zeng,Yuesong Wang,Yawei Luo,Tao Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AIR是一个自监督前馈框架，通过阶段式残差架构和显式阶段控制机制，将迭代式2D高斯拟合摊销为单次网络前向传播，无需每图像测试时优化，在Kodak和DIV2K上达到优于现有高斯基方法的重建质量，编码时间仅160-300ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于2D高斯泼溅的方法要么需要昂贵的每图像迭代优化，要么依赖手工设计的先验进行基元分配，缺乏高效且自适应的前馈式解决方案。

Method: 提出AIR框架：(1) 阶段式残差架构，从重建残差中逐步预测新的高斯基元；(2) 显式阶段控制机制，仅在欠重建区域激活新基元；(3) Predict-Optimize-Distill训练策略，通过蒸馏短视野优化的高斯增量来稳定多阶段预测；(4) 联合微调各阶段并配备图像自适应量化器以实现紧凑存储。

Result: 在Kodak和DIV2K数据集上，AIR相比代表性高斯基基线方法取得更好的重建质量，同时将编码时间降低至160-300ms。

Conclusion: AIR证明了通过前馈方式摊销高斯拟合的可行性，在保持高质量重建的同时大幅提升编码效率，为2D高斯泼溅的实际应用提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: 2D Gaussian splatting provides an efficient explicit representation for image reconstruction, but existing methods still require costly per-image iterative optimization or rely on handcrafted priors for primitive allocation. We present AIR, a self-supervised feed-forward framework that amortizes iterative Gaussian fitting into a single network pass, eliminating per-image test-time optimization. AIR adopts a stage-wise residual architecture that progressively predicts additional Gaussian primitives from reconstruction residuals, together with an explicit Stage Control mechanism that activates new primitives only in under-reconstructed regions. A Predict--Optimize--Distill training strategy stabilizes multi-stage prediction by distilling short-horizon optimized Gaussian increments back into the predictor. The stabilized predictor is then jointly finetuned across stages and equipped with an image-adaptive quantizer for compact Gaussian storage. Experiments on Kodak and DIV2K show that AIR achieves better reconstruction quality than representative Gaussian-based baselines while reducing encoding time to 160--300\,ms. Code: https://github.com/whoiszzj/AIR.git

</details>


### [100] [WikiVQABench: A Knowledge-Grounded Visual Question Answering Benchmark from Wikipedia and Wikidata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21479)
*Basel Shbita,Pengyuan Li,Anna Lisa Gentile*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了WikiVQABench，一个需要外部知识才能正确回答的视觉问答基准数据集，通过结合维基百科图片、标题和维基数据知识构建，并经过人工审核。评估15个视觉语言模型显示准确率范围24.7%-75.6%，有效区分模型在知识密集型推理上的能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQA基准大多强调仅凭视觉内容即可解决的感知型任务，但现实场景中许多问题需要外部知识（无法直接从图像中观察到）。因此需要构建一个需要外部知识才能正确回答的VQA基准来评估模型的知识推理能力。

Method: 1) 系统性地结合维基百科图片、文章标题和维基百科结构化知识；2) 使用大语言模型生成候选的多选题-图片-答案组合；3) 通过人工审核确保事实正确性、视觉-文本一致性以及问题需要外部知识才能解答。

Result: 评估了15个视觉语言模型（256M-90B参数），准确率范围从24.7%到75.6%，证明该基准能有效区分不同模型在知识密集型推理上的能力差异。

Conclusion: WikiVQABench是一个有效评估视觉语言模型知识推理能力的基准，数据集和测评代码已公开，可推动知识驱动型VQA研究。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) benchmarks have largely emphasized perception-based tasks that can be solved from visual content alone. In contrast, many real-world scenarios require external knowledge that is not directly observable in the image to answer correctly. We introduce WikiVQABench, a human-curated knowledge-grounded VQA benchmark constructed by systematically combining Wikipedia images, their associated article captions, and structured knowledge from Wikidata. Our pipeline uses large language models (LLMs) to generate candidate multiple-choice image-question-answer sets. All generated instances are subsequently reviewed and curated by human annotators to ensure factual correctness, visual-text consistency, and that each question requires external knowledge in addition to visual evidence for correct resolution. WikiVQABench comprises a substantial collection of Wikipedia images with curated multiple-choice questions designed to benchmark knowledge-aware vision-language models (VLMs). Evaluation of fifteen VLMs (256M-90B parameters) reveals a wide performance range (24.7%-75.6% accuracy), demonstrating that the benchmark effectively discriminates model capabilities on knowledge-intensive reasoning. The dataset and benchmarking code are publicly available.

</details>


### [101] [TERDNet: Transformer Encoder-Recurrent Decoder Network for Scene Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20822)
*Jiae Yoon,Ue-Hwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TERDNet，一种结合Transformer编码器与循环3门控GRU解码器的场景变化检测网络，通过多级特征提取、相关体融合、迭代精炼及组合上采样模块，在四个公开数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SCD模型存在三个局限：忽视不同层特征的重要性差异、使用单步解码器限制了精炼能力、对编码器预训练策略的理解不足。

Method: TERDNet包含四部分：1) 基于Transformer的编码器提取多级特征；2) 特征融合模块整合相关体与多级特征；3) 循环3门控GRU解码器进行迭代精炼；4) 组合卷积-插值上采样器恢复高分辨率变化掩码。

Result: 在四个公开基准上持续优于先前方法，生成更准确、更细节的变化掩码。消融实验验证了分割预训练的有效性。在视角未对齐的鲁棒性测试中表现出色。

Conclusion: TERDNet有效解决了SCD中特征重要性差异、解码器精炼不足和预训练策略问题，具备在实际机器人系统中部署的潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we address the challenge of Scene Change Detection (SCD), where the goal is to identify variations between two images of the same location captured at different times. Existing SCD models often overlook the varying importance of features across layers, employ single-step decoders that confine refinement, and provide limited insight into encoder pretraining strategies. We propose TERDNet, a Transformer Encoder-Recurrent Decoder Network designed to overcome these limitations. TERDNet consists of a transformer-based encoder that extracts multi-level representations, a feature fusion module that integrates correlation volumes with these features, a recurrent 3-gate-GRU decoder that performs iterative refinement, and a combined convolution-interpolation upsampler that restores fine-grained resolution. Extensive experiments on four public benchmarks show that TERDNet consistently outperforms prior approaches and produces more accurate and detailed change masks. Ablation studies confirm the benefit of segmentation-based pretraining and the effectiveness of our fusion design. In addition, robustness tests under viewpoint misalignment confirm TERDNet's potential for deployment in real-world robotic systems, where reliable perception is critical. Our code is available at https://github.com/AutoCompSysLab/TERDNet.

</details>


### [102] [RelWitness: Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graph Generation with Visual-Geometric Relation Witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20823)
*Minh Anh Nguyen,Quang Huy Tran,Bao Ngoc Le,Tuan Kiet Pham,Sui Yang Guang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RelWitness提出了一种基于"关系见证者"机制的方法，通过视觉-几何线索从不完整的3D场景图标注中学习开放词汇的关系识别，有效区分未标注的正例和负例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇3D场景图生成的关键难点不仅在于词汇扩展，更在于关系标注的不可靠性——数据集中许多有效的物体对关系未被标注，导致监督信息不完整。

Method: 提出关系见证者（Relation Witness）概念，即具体的视觉-几何线索使关系在捕获场景中可观测；构建包含RGB视图、深度图、3D几何、角色敏感文本等多源信息的关系见证记录；通过见证验证器将未标注候选分为已验证的正例、可靠负例和不确定样本；采用见证引导的正-无标签学习目标优化模型；引入见证一致性解码和RGB-D缺失关系审计协议。

Result: 在3DSSG/3RScan和ScanNet数据集上的仿真实验表明，该方法在未见关系识别、见证精确率、幻觉减少和冗余关系短语减少方面均优于基线。注：当前结果为规划值，需在正式提交前替换为实测结果。

Conclusion: 通过关系见证者机制，可以在不完整标注条件下有效学习开放词汇的3D场景图关系，减少对完整标注的依赖，提升模型对未见关系的泛化能力。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene graph generation seeks to describe object instances and their relations with flexible natural-language predicates. The central difficulty is not only vocabulary expansion, but supervision reliability: relation annotations in 3D scene graph datasets are selective, and many valid object-pair relations are unannotated. We propose RelWitness, a framework for open-vocabulary 3D scene graph generation from posed RGB-D sequences under incomplete relation supervision. The key concept is a relation witness: a concrete visual-geometric cue that makes a relation observable in the captured scene. Support relations require contact and vertical ordering; containment requires enclosure; proximity requires metric closeness; orientation requires facing direction; and stable relations should persist across views where both objects are visible. RelWitness constructs relation witness records from RGB views, depth maps, reconstructed 3D geometry, role-sensitive text, object-prior null views, and multi-view consistency. A visual-geometric witness verifier assigns unannotated relation candidates to verified missing positives, reliable negatives, or uncertain unlabeled cases. A witness-guided positive-unlabeled objective then learns from incomplete annotations without turning every missing label into a negative. We further introduce witness-consistent decoding and an RGB-D missing-relation audit protocol. Simulated manuscript-planning experiments on 3DSSG/3RScan and ScanNet-derived open-vocabulary splits show the intended behavior: improved unseen-relation recognition, higher witness precision, lower hallucination, and reduced redundant relation phrases. All numerical results are planning values and must be replaced by reproduced measurements before submission

</details>


### [103] [HyDAR-Pano3D: A Hybrid Disentangled Anatomical Recovery Framework for Panoramic-to-3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20827)
*Yaoyao Yue,Jérôme Schmid,Xiaoshuang Li,Eduardo Delamare,Jinman Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出HyDAR-Pano3D，一种两阶段框架，将全景X光片（PR）到锥形束CT（CBCT）的三维重建分解为解剖学解耦问题。第一阶段通过双编码器网络结合放射学特征和SAM语义先验重建标准化的拱形正则化体积；第二阶段通过解剖恢复网络预测形变场，将标准体积映射回原生空间以恢复个体形态变异。实验表明该方法在三个大规模数据集上显著优于基线方法（PSNR 25.76dB, SSIM 85.70%, Dice 83.83%），并能有效支持下游分割任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接将2D全景X光片映射到3D CBCT体积，要求模型同时学习通用解剖结构和患者特异性形态变异，这种耦合式学习导致病态逆问题高度歧义，产生过平滑且边界模糊的重建结果。因此，本文旨在通过解耦解剖结构恢复来解决这一模糊性问题。

Method: 提出HyDAR-Pano3D两阶段框架：第一阶段采用双编码器网络，融合放射学特征与SAM（Segment Anything Model）语义先验，重建拱形正则化的标准解剖体积；第二阶段利用解剖恢复网络，预测受先验约束的结构化形变场，将标准体积映射回患者原生空间，从而恢复个体形态变异。

Result: 在三个大规模数据集上，HyDAR-Pano3D显著优于基线方法（p<0.05），PSNR达25.76dB，SSIM达85.70%，整体解剖Dice达83.83%。下游分割任务中，全牙齿分割Dice为82.4%，下牙槽管分割Dice为72.2%。

Conclusion: 该解耦方法能够有效保留临床相关解剖结构，在CBCT数据不可用时仍可实现鲁棒的解剖感知评估，为PR到CBCT的重建提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Panoramic radiograph (PR) is fundamentally used in routine dental care, but it inherently provides only a two-dimensional (2D) projection of complex three-dimensional (3D) craniofacial anatomy. Most existing learning-based methods attempt to computationally recover this 3D information by directly regressing native cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) volumes from PR. However, this direct mapping requires the model to simultaneously learn common anatomical structures and patient-specific morphological variations. This entangled formulation makes the ill-posed 2D-to-3D inverse problem highly ambiguous, often producing over-smoothed reconstructions with blurred anatomical boundaries. To address this, we propose HyDAR-Pano3D, a two-stage framework that reformulates PR-to-CBCT reconstruction as a disentangled anatomical recovery problem. In Stage 1, a dual-encoder network integrates radiographic features with SAM-derived semantic priors to reconstruct an arch-normalized canonical volume. In Stage 2, an Anatomical Restoration Network predicts a prior-constrained structured deformation field to map this canonical volume back to the native space, restoring individual morphological variations. Experiments on three large-scale datasets show that HyDAR-Pano3D significantly outperforms baseline methods ($p < 0.05$), achieving a 25.76 dB PSNR, 85.70\% SSIM, and an 83.83\% overall anatomical Dice score. The synthesized volumes successfully support downstream segmentation of whole teeth (82.4\% Dice) and the inferior alveolar canal (72.2\% Dice), demonstrating that our disentangled approach preserves clinically relevant structures to enable robust anatomy-aware assessment when CBCT data is unavailable.

</details>


### [104] [Activation-Free Backbones for Image Recognition: Polynomial Alternatives within MetaFormer-Style Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20839)
*Jeffrey Wang,Jonathan Gregory,Grigorios G. Chrysos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文证明在MetaFormer视觉骨干网络中，可以用Hadamard积（多项式函数）替代ReLU、GELU和softmax等传统激活函数，设计出无激活函数的MLP、卷积和注意力模块。所提出的PolyNeXt模型在ImageNet分类、ADE20K语义分割和分布外鲁棒性上匹配或超越带激活函数的模型，且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战视觉骨干网络中激活函数（如ReLU、GELU、softmax）不可或缺的传统认知，探索用多项式替代方案简化网络结构。

Method: 用Hadamard积替代标准非线性激活函数，设计三种核心原语的激活无关多项式替代方案（MLP、卷积、注意力），并将其集成到MetaFormer框架中形成PolyNeXt模型。

Result: PolyNeXt模型在ImageNet分类、ADE20K语义分割和分布外鲁棒性上匹配或超越带激活函数的基线模型，且计算成本更低；显著优于以往的多项式网络。

Conclusion: 标准模块的多项式变体可以击败复杂的定制架构，激活函数在MetaFormer风格骨干中并非必需。

Abstract: Modern vision backbones treat pointwise activations (e.g., ReLU, GELU) and exponential softmax as essential sources of nonlinearity, but we demonstrate they are not required within MetaFormer-style vision backbones. We design activation-free polynomial alternatives for three core primitives (MLPs, convolutions, and attention), where Hadamard products replace standard nonlinearities to yield polynomial functions of the input. These modules integrate seamlessly into existing architectures: instantiated within MetaFormer, a modular framework for vision backbones, our PolyNeXt models match or exceed activation-based counterparts across model scales on ImageNet classification, ADE20K semantic segmentation, and out-of-distribution robustness. We also substantially outperform prior polynomial networks at reduced computational cost, showing that polynomial variants of standard modules beat complex custom architectures.

</details>


### [105] [Map-Mono-Ego: Map-Grounded Global Human Pose Estimation from Monocular Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20889)
*Hiroyuki Deguchi,Ryosuke Hori,Kotaro Amaya,Tsubasa Maruyama,Mitsunori Tada,Hideo Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MapMonoEgo通过利用预扫描的3D点云，仅从单目摄像头实现全局一致的人体姿态估计，解决了单目视觉中的尺度模糊和漂移问题，并引入了AIST-Living数据集验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目自我中心人体姿态估计方法主要关注相对运动，无法获取用户在环境中的绝对位置，且存在尺度模糊导致的平移漂移问题，限制了长期跟踪能力。

Method: 提出MapMonoEgo框架，利用预扫描的3D点云作为环境先验，从单目摄像头视频中实现全局一致的人体姿态估计。

Result: 在AIST-Living数据集上显著优于现有基线方法，证明无需专用硬件即可用于实际监控任务。

Conclusion: MapMonoEgo有效解决了单目自我中心姿态估计中的绝对定位和漂移问题，为实用化监控提供了低成本方案。

Abstract: Monocular egocentric human pose estimation is essential for ubiquitous activity monitoring. However, understanding the user's absolute location within the environment remains a challenge. Existing methods primarily focus on relative motion from an initial position, and tend not to account for the wearer's absolute location within an environment. Furthermore, inherent scale ambiguity in monocular vision leads to severe translational drift, limiting long-term tracking without specialized multi-sensor hardware. To address this, we propose MapMonoEgo, a novel framework achieving globally consistent human pose estimation solely from a monocular camera by leveraging a pre-scanned 3D point cloud. We also introduce AIST-Living dataset, a new dataset pairing egocentric video with ground-truth motion in a scanned environment. Experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline, proving its utility for practical monitoring tasks without specialized hardware.

</details>


### [106] [HDMoE: A Hierarchical Decoupling-Fusion Mixture-of-Experts Framework for Multimodal Cancer Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20891)
*Huayi Wang,Haochao Ying,Yuyang Xu,Qiyao Zheng,jun wang,Cheng Zhang,Ying Sun,Jian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HDMoE框架，通过双层MoE和随机特征重组模块，解决多模态生存预测中特征解耦-融合的冗余信息和细粒度关系建模不足问题，在肝癌和TCGA数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征解耦-融合方法存在两个不足：(1)解耦前未能减少模态特征的冗余信息，影响解耦和融合效果；(2)缺乏对特征细粒度关系的建模能力，无法捕捉模态内和模态间的局部信息交互。

Method: 提出HDMoE框架，包含两级MoE和两个RFR模块。第一级MoE通过共享专家和路由专家去除冗余信息并提取细粒度特定特征；第二级MoE实现细粒度模态间特征解耦。每个MoE后接RFR模块，分别融合模态内和模态间的细粒度特征。

Result: 在私有肝癌（LC）数据集和三个TCGA公开数据集上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 双层MoE结合RFR模块的设计能有效解决多模态生存预测中特征冗余和细粒度交互建模问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal survival prediction, a crucial yet challenging task, demands the integration of multimodal medical data (\eg Whole Slide Images (WSIs) and Genomic Profiles) to achieve accurate prognostic modeling. Given the inherent heterogeneity across modalities, the feature decoupling-fusion paradigm has emerged as a dominant approach. However, these methods have the following shortcomings: (1) fail to reduce the redundant information of modality features before decoupling, which negatively affects the feature decoupling and fusion effect;(2) lack the ability to model the fine-grained relationships of the features and capture the local information interactions between intra- and inter-modality features. To address these issues, we propose a \underline{H}ierarchical \underline{D}ecoupling-Fusion \underline{M}ixture-\underline{o}f-\underline{E}xperts (HDMoE) framework with two levels of MoE and \underline{R}andom \underline{F}eature \underline{R}eorganization (RFR) modules.In the first-level MoE, shared experts and routed experts are employed to remove redundant information and extract fine-grained specific features within each modality, while the second-level MoE facilitates fine-grained inter-modality feature decoupling. Besides, we design two RFR modules following each level of MoE to finely fuse intra- and inter-modality features, which can help the model capture more fine-grained relationships between modalities. Extensive experimental results on our private Liver Cancer (LC) and three TCGA public datasets confirm the effectiveness of our proposed method. Codes are available at https://github.com/ZJUMAI/HDMoE.

</details>


### [107] [FruitEnsemble: MLLM-Guided Arbitration for Heterogeneous ensemble in Fine-Grained Fruit Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20892)
*Enhui Yu,Junhui Li,Ruitong Lu,Jialu Li,Youshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对细粒度水果分类中数据集匮乏和类间相似度高的问题，构建了包含306类、116,233样本的数据集，并提出FruitEnsemble两阶段动态推理框架：第一阶段通过验证校准的加权集成生成Top-3候选，第二阶段在置信度低于0.6时触发多模态大模型结合外部植物学描述进行Chain-of-Thought推理仲裁。最终分类准确率达70.49%，超越现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度水果分类面临两大核心挑战：一是高质量标注数据集严重匮乏，二是不同水果类别间视觉相似度极高，导致传统静态单模型架构的泛化能力不足，难以在实际农业场景中取得令人满意的分类效果。

Method: （1）构建了包含306类、116,233样本的大规模细粒度水果数据集；（2）提出FruitEnsemble两阶段动态推理框架，第一阶段使用验证校准的加权集成多异构骨干网络生成鲁棒的Top-3候选池；（3）引入专家仲裁机制，当集成置信度低于0.6时触发多模态大语言模型，结合外部植物学描述通过Chain-of-Thought推理进行视觉验证；（4）优化训练流程，采用困难样本感知的联合损失函数。

Result: FruitEnsemble在构建的数据集上取得了70.49%的分类准确率，超越了现有最先进的模型，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段动态推理框架为真实农业场景中的视觉分选和品质检测任务提供了一种高效、可部署的解决方案，有效克服了静态单模型架构的泛化局限。

Abstract: Fine-grained fruit classification is a critical yet challenging task in agricultural computer vision, primarily hindered by a severe shortage of high-quality datasets and the high visual similarity between classes. To address these challenges, we first constructed a comprehensive dataset comprising 306 fruit categories with 116,233 samples. Moreover, we propose FruitEnsemble, a practical two-stage dynamic inference framework designed to overcome the generalization limitations of static single-model architectures. In the first stage, FruitEnsemble employs a validation-calibrated weighted ensemble of heterogeneous backbones to generate a robust Top-3 candidate pool. To tackle difficult samples, we introduce an expert arbitration mechanism: when ensemble confidence falls below 0.6, a multimodal large language model (MLLM) is triggered to perform rigorous visual verification by integrating external botanical descriptions using Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. Furthermore, we optimized the training pipeline with a hard sample-aware joint loss. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FruitEnsemble achieves a classification accuracy of 70.49\% and outperforms existing state-of-the-art models. Our framework provides an efficient, deployment-oriented solution for real-world agricultural visual sorting and quality inspection tasks.

</details>


### [108] [JFAA: Technical Report for the EPIC-KITCHENS-100 Action Anticipation Challenge at EgoVis 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20904)
*Qiaohui Chu,Haoyu Zhang,Yisen Feng,Meng Liu,Weili Guan,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JFAA是一个基于JEPA的动作预测方法，在EPIC-KITCHENS-100挑战赛中取得第一名。它使用冻结编码器提取观测特征，训练轻量级注意力探针预测动作，并通过场感知集成提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受V-JEPA 2.1的表示学习和未来预测能力启发，旨在解决EGO型厨房场景下的动作提前预测任务。

Method: 使用冻结的编码器和预测器提取观察上下文特征和近未来隐层令牌，训练轻量级注意力探针预测动词、名词和动作的对数概率；并通过场感知集成机制选择各领域最可靠的预测结果进行集成。

Result: 在EgoVis 2026 EK-100动作预测挑战赛中取得第一名。

Conclusion: JFAA方法在EPIC-KITCHENS-100动作预测任务上达到了领先性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: We propose JFAA, a JEPA-based Future Action Anticipation method for the EPIC-KITCHENS-100 (EK-100) Action Anticipation task. Inspired by the representation learning and future prediction ability of V-JEPA 2.1, JFAA uses a frozen encoder and predictor to extract observed context features and near-future latent tokens. A lightweight attentive probe is then trained to predict verb, noun, and action logits with separate task queries. To improve robustness, we further build a field-aware ensemble over selected epoch-level predictions, allowing each output field to benefit from its most reliable candidates. Experimental results on the official challenge server show that JFAA achieves first place in the EgoVis 2026 EK-100 Action Anticipation Challenge. Our code will be released at https://github.com/CorrineQiu/JFAA.

</details>


### [109] [SynCB: A Synergy Concept-Based Model with Dynamic Routing Between Concepts and Complementary Neural Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20908)
*Tores Julie,Sun Rémy,Sassatelli Lucile,Ancarani Elisa,Wu Hui-Yin,Precioso Frédéric*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SynCB框架，通过可训练路由模块动态选择概念分支或神经分支处理输入，在保持高任务精度的同时提升对人类干预的响应性，在五个数据集上超越全神经基线最高3.9个百分点，干预性能超出最强竞品最高6.43个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念模型解释性强但精度不足，混合模型牺牲了人类干预能力。作者希望同时兼顾任务精度与对人类干预的响应性。

Method: SynCB框架包含一个概念分支（CB）和一个互补神经分支，通过可训练的路由模块动态选择每样本使用哪个分支。两分支共享骨干网络联合学习，并引入测试时干预策略及对应损失函数。

Result: 在五个数据集上，SynCB任务精度超过全神经基线最高3.9个百分点，干预性能超出最强竞品最高6.43个百分点。

Conclusion: SynCB通过保持分支独立并引入动态路由和干预策略，成功兼顾了概念模型的解释性与神经网络的强性能，并显著提升了对人类干预的响应能力。

Abstract: Concept-based (CB) models provide interpretability and support test-time human intervention, while standard neural networks (NN) offer strong task performance but little transparency.
