Prior work has explored hybrid formulations that integrate concepts and additional representations to improve accuracy, often at the cost of human interventions. We introduce the \emph{Synergy Concept-Based Model (SynCB)} framework, that combines a CB branch with a complementary neural branch, and a trainable routing module that dynamically selects which branch to use for each input. Unlike prior models, which fuse residual and concept-based predictions, SynCB keeps the two branches distinct and coordinates them through the routing module. Moreover, both branches are learned jointly, allowing information sharing between the complementary neural branch and CB branches through their common backbone. To improve responsiveness to interventions, we further introduce a test-time intervention policy and a corresponding loss. Across five datasets and CB benchmarks, SynCB consistently achieves higher task accuracy while remaining more responsive to human interventions, surpassing the full neural baseline by up to 3.9 percentage points and exceeding the strongest competitor in intervention performance by up to 6.43 percentage points.

</details>


### [110] [FlowLong: Inference-time Long Video Generation via Manifold-constrained Tweedie Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20910)
*Jangho Park,Geon Yeong Park,Gihyun Kwon,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Tweedie Matching + 随机早期阶段采样的推理时方法，通过重叠滑动窗口实现对长视频的生成，无需额外训练且架构无关，在多个任务上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在生成长序列时，双向模型扩展受限于特定架构且质量退化，自回归模型则因曝光偏差累积漂移误差并产生重复运动模式。需要一种架构无关、无需训练的推理时方法来解决长视频生成的质量问题。

Method: 1) 使用重叠滑动窗口生成长视频；2) 通过Tweedie匹配混合相邻窗口的预测干净样本，施加流形约束和时间一致性；3) 采用随机早期阶段采样，在高噪声阶段注入新噪声以同步各窗口轨迹，随后转为确定性ODE采样以保持视觉保真度。

Result: 生成的视频时长远超原生窗口长度，在时间一致性和视觉质量上优于无训练和自回归基线，并零样本扩展至音视频联合生成和文本到3DGS。

Conclusion: 该方法为长视频生成提供了一种简洁、通用且有效的推理时方案，具有良好的跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Extending the generation horizon of video diffusion models to long sequences remains a long-standing and important challenge. Existing training-free approaches fall into two categories: extensions of bidirectional models, which are tightly coupled to specific architectures and suffer from quality degradation over long horizons, and autoregressive models, which accumulate drift errors due to exposure bias and tend to produce repetitive motion patterns. To address these issues, we propose a novel but simple inference-time approach for long video generation that is architecture-agnostic and requires no additional training. Our method generates long videos via overlapping sliding windows, where predicted clean samples from adjacent windows are blended via \emph{Tweedie matching} to enforce both \textbf{manifold constraint and temporal consistency} across overlap regions. \emph{Stochastic early-phase sampling} then synchronizes per-window trajectories by injecting fresh noise after each Tweedie matching correction in the high-noise phase, before transitioning to deterministic ODE sampling to preserve fine-grained visual fidelity. Applied to various video generation models, our method generates videos several times longer than the native window length while outperforming both training-free and autoregressive baselines in temporal consistency and visual quality, and further extends to audio-video joint generation and text-to-3DGS without any fine-tuning.

</details>


### [111] [RISE: Reliable Improvement in Self-Evolving Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20914)
*Chaoran Xu,Yingmao Miao,Pengfei Zhang,Hao Dou,Lei Sun,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RISE框架，通过细粒度角色交替、质量监督器和技能感知动态平衡三大设计，解决了VLM自进化学习中角色交替延迟、问题质量下降和模式坍塌的问题，在七个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM自进化方法面临三个主要挑战：粗粒度的角色交替导致问题生成与求解器适应之间的交互延迟；生成的问题质量会逐渐下降；问题类型可能坍缩到狭窄的分布，限制了自进化的效率和可靠性。

Method: RISE包含三个互补设计：1) 细粒度角色交替，缩短提问者与求解器之间的反馈循环；2) 质量监督器，提升问题的有效性和伪标签的可靠性；3) 技能感知动态平衡，缓解模式坍塌并保持广泛的技能覆盖。

Result: 在两个VLM骨干网络上、七个基准测试中，RISE一致地提升了基础模型性能，带来了广泛且持续的提升。

Conclusion: RISE通过可靠的自我进化框架，使得VLM能够仅从无标注图像中实现有效的自我提升，解决了现有方法的效率与可靠性瓶颈。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, but further improving them still relies heavily on large-scale human-constructed supervision for post-training. Such supervision is costly to obtain, especially for reasoning-intensive multimodal tasks where questions, answers, and feedback signals must be carefully designed. This motivates self-evolving learning, where a model improves itself through a dual-role closed loop: a questioner autonomously poses questions and a solver learns to solve them. However, we observe that current VLM self-evolving methods still face three major challenges: coarse-grained role alternation delays the interaction between question generation and solver adaptation; generated questions can progressively degrade in quality; and question types may collapse toward a narrow distribution. These issues limit the efficiency and reliability of self-evolution. Thus, we propose \textbf{RISE}, a reliable self-evolving framework for vision-language models. RISE is built on three complementary designs: fine-grained role alternation, which shortens the feedback loop between the questioner and the solver to improve efficiency; a quality supervisor, which improves question validity and pseudo-label reliability; and skill-aware dynamic balancing, which mitigates mode collapse and maintains broad skill coverage during evolution. Together, these components enable more reliable and effective self-evolution from unlabeled images. Experiments on two VLM backbones across seven benchmarks show that RISE consistently improves the base models, yielding broad and sustained gains. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/RISE.

</details>


### [112] [3D Reconstruction and Knowledge Distillation to Improve Multi-View Image Models to Explore Spike Volume Estimation in Wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20940)
*Olivia Zumsteg,Jannis Widmer,Yann Bourdé,Norbert Kirchgessner,Andreas Hund,Lukas Roth,Paraskevi Nousi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合2D-3D知识蒸馏方法，利用3D点云网络指导纯2D Transformer模型进行小麦穗体积估计，在保持高效推理的同时提升了精度（MAE降低至639.93 mm³，相关系数提升至0.82），推理速度从160ms大幅降至1.4ms/穗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小麦穗体积的准确估计对产量分析和抗逆评估至关重要，但现有方法存在诸多局限：主动3D传感（LiDAR、ToF）对植物运动敏感或不适于户外，3D重建计算成本高，而纯2D图像处理虽高效但缺乏几何信息。因此需要一种兼顾几何精度与计算效率的方法。

Method: 提出混合2D-3D知识蒸馏框架：1) 训练刚体不变的点云网络（基于距离直方图特征）获取姿态鲁棒的几何表示；2) 将3D模型与多视图图像Transformer（RT）集成为集成架构；3) 通过特征蒸馏或标签蒸馏将集成知识迁移到纯图像学生模型（RT）。

Result: 蒸馏后的RT模型MAE从654.31 mm³降至639.93 mm³和644.62 mm³，相关系数从0.76提升至0.77和0.82；推理时间从160ms大幅降低至1.4ms/穗；蒸馏还减轻了体积依赖偏差，使图像模型的潜在表征向几何感知形状重塑。

Conclusion: 3D信息指导训练的2D Transformer能够实现可扩展且高效的小麦穗体积估计，适用于高通量田间表型分析，验证了知识蒸馏在几何感知视觉任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of wheat spike volume is important for yield component analysis and stress resilience assessment, yet field-based measurement remains challenging. Active 3D sensing methods such as Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) or time-of-flight (ToF) are sensitive to plant motion or poorly suited to outdoor conditions, while 3D reconstructions are computationally expensive. Direct 2D image processing would offer computational advantages, but image-based models lack explicit geometric information. We therefore propose a hybrid 2D-3D approach with knowledge distillation during training while enabling efficient image-only inference. First, we train a rigid-invariant point cloud network using distance-based histogram features to obtain pose-robust geometric representations. We then combine the 3D model with a proposed multi-view image-based regulated Transformer (RT) in an ensemble architecture. Finally, we distill the ensemble knowledge into a purely image-based student model using either feature-based or label-based distillation. The two distilled RTs reduce the mean absolute error (MAE) from 654.31 mm$^3$ of the non-distilled RT to 639.93 mm$^3$ and 644.62 mm$^3$, and increase correlation from 0.76 to 0.77 and 0.82, respectively. At the same time, inference time is reduced from 160 ms to 1.4 ms per spike. Distillation further mitigates volume-dependent bias and reshapes the latent representation of the image model toward a geometry-aware shape. Our results demonstrate that 3D-informed training of a 2D Transformer allows for scalable and efficient spike volume estimation for high-throughput field phenotyping.

</details>


### [113] [PaintCopilot: Modeling Painting as Autonomous Artistic Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20941)
*Yunge Wen,Yuancheng Shen,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaintCopilot 是一个共创神经绘画助手，将绘画建模为基于画布状态和笔触历史的自回归艺术行为，无需目标图像即可预测未来笔触，支持多种交互式工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经绘画方法将绘画视为对预定义参考图像的像素重建，缺乏真正的创作灵活性和人机共创能力。作者希望建模绘画的内在艺术动态，使AI能够像大语言模型生成文本一样，基于上下文连续生成笔触。

Method: 提出三个互补模型：ViT-based Target Predictor（从局部画布推断艺术家意图）、autoregressive Next Stroke Predictor（通过流匹配生成时序连贯笔触）、VAE-based Region Sampler（合成语义局部化的笔触序列）。采用三种可微分笔刷表示，支持四种交互工作流。

Result: 通过专业艺术家的案例研究，展示PaintCopilot实现了流畅的共创绘画工作流，艺术家和AI能够在创作过程中持续交替控制。

Conclusion: PaintCopilot成功地将绘画从目标驱动的像素重建范式转变为开放式的自回归艺术生成范式，为人机协同创作提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: We present PaintCopilot, a co-creative neural painting assistant that models painting as an open-ended autoregressive artistic behavior conditioned on evolving canvas states and prior brushstroke history, without requiring a target image. Unlike existing neural painting methods that frame painting as pixel reconstruction toward a predefined reference, PaintCopilot predicts future strokes directly from learned artistic dynamics, analogous to how large language models continue text sequences from prior context.
  The framework proposes three complementary models: a ViT-based Target Predictor that infers artist intent from partial canvas observations, an autoregressive Next Stroke Predictor that generates temporally coherent brushstrokes via flow matching, and a VAE-based Region Sampler that synthesizes semantically localized stroke sequences on demand. Built on three differentiable brush representations (Hard Round, Brush Tip, and 2D Gaussian), the system supports four interactive workflows: Optimize History, Stroke Completion, Region Inpainting, and Dynamic Brush. Through case studies with professional artists, we demonstrate that PaintCopilot enables fluid co-creative painting workflows in which artists and AI continuously alternate control throughout the creative process.

</details>


### [114] [Bridging Structure and Language: Graph-Based Visual Reasoning for Autonomous Road Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20942)
*Lena Wild,Katie Z Luo,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Combined Road Substrate (CRS)，一种将几何道路结构与开放词汇语义联合执行的图基框架。仅需20-80个CRS增强场景训练小模型（2-4B参数），即可在结构化道路推理任务上超越大型闭源VLM。核心发现：道路理解的瓶颈在于缺乏结构化监督，而非模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM具备语义灵活性但缺乏几何和关系基础，传统模块化系统（如HD地图）结构精确但语义僵化。两者之间存在鸿沟，亟需一种同时兼具结构精确性与语义灵活性的统一表示。

Method: 提出CRS框架，通过递归图查询自动生成组合复杂且语言多样的QA对，引入"免费基础"机制确保逻辑可追溯性，以及程序化提取的思维链监督轨迹。

Result: SOTA VLM（含大型闭源模型）在结构化道路推理上表现不佳；而仅用20-80个CRS场景训练2-4B参数小模型，即可在组合推理任务上取得稳定提升。

Conclusion: 模型行为分析表明，基线模型失败在关系场景理解，CRS训练后失败降级为属性识别。道路理解的主要瓶颈是缺乏结构化监督，而非模型规模大小。

Abstract: Structured road understanding of lane geometry, topology, and traffic element relationships is foundational to safe autonomous driving. While vision-language models (VLMs) offer promising semantic flexibility, they lack the geometric and relational grounding required for precise road reasoning. Conversely, traditional modular systems, e.g., HD maps and topological road graphs, provide structural precision but remain semantically rigid. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Combined Road Substrate (CRS), a graph-grounded framework that makes geometric road structure and open-vocabulary semantics jointly executable in a single representation. CRS enables the automatic generation of compositionally complex and linguistically varied question-answer pairs via recursive graph queries, augmented with a "grounding for free" mechanism that ensures logical traceability to specific map elements, and procedurally extracted chain-of-thought supervision traces. We demonstrate that state-of-the-art VLMs - including large, closed-source models - struggle significantly with structured road reasoning, yet training a small 2- or 4-billion-parameter model with as few as 20 to 80 CRS-enriched scenes yields stable gains in compositional reasoning tasks of varying depth. Analysis of model behavior via verifiable reasoning traces reveals a systematic shift in failure modes: whereas baseline models fail at relational scene understanding, CRS-trained models reduce failures to attribute recognition, suggesting that the primary bottleneck in road understanding is not model scale, but the absence of structured supervision.

</details>


### [115] [DrawMotion: Generating 3D Human Motions by Freehand Drawing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20955)
*Tao Wang,Lei Jin,Zhihua Wu,Qiaozhi He,Jiaming Chu,Yu Cheng,Junliang Xing,Jian Zhao,Shuicheng Yan,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DrawMotion是一个基于扩散模型的文本到动作生成框架，通过引入手绘草图条件（freehand drawing condition）和多条件融合模块（MCM），让用户能以更直观、高效的方式生成符合意图的3D人体动作，相比纯文本方法减少约46.7%的用户时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户仅通过文本难以精确表达复杂的运动意图，现有方法缺乏直观的空间控制手段。DrawMotion旨在通过引入手绘条件降低用户表达成本，同时提升生成动作与用户意图的对齐度。

Method: 1) 设计自动生成手绘火柴人草图（freehand stickman sketch）的算法，适配不同数据集格式；2) 提出多条件融合模块（Multi-Condition Module, MCM）集成到扩散过程中，支持文本+手绘的任意条件组合，同时降低计算复杂度；3) 利用MCM中间特征的连续空间特性，采用无训练的分类器引导（classifier-guidance）更新特征，保持生成保真度。

Result: 定量实验和用户研究表明，手绘草图条件相比纯文本方法，用户生成符合想象的动作所需时间减少了约46.7%。

Conclusion: DrawMotion证明了手绘条件作为文本补充的可行性与高效性，为多条件、细粒度的文本到动作生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Text-to-motion generation, which translates textual descriptions into human motions, faces the challenge that users often struggle to precisely convey their intended motions through text alone. To address this issue, this paper introduces DrawMotion, an efficient diffusion-based framework designed for multi-condition scenarios. DrawMotion generates motions based on both a conventional text condition and a novel hand-drawing condition, which provide semantic and spatial control over the generated motions, respectively. Specifically, we tackle the fine-grained motion generation task from three perspectives: 1) freehand drawing condition. To accurately capture users' intended motions without requiring tedious textual input, we develop an algorithm to automatically generate hand-drawn stickman sketches across different dataset formats; 2) multi-condition fusion. We propose a Multi-Condition Module (MCM) that is integrated into the diffusion process, enabling the model to exploit all possible condition combinations while reducing computational complexity compared to conventional approaches; and 3) training-free guidance. Notably, the MCM in DrawMotion ensures that its intermediate features lie in a continuous space, allowing classifier-guidance gradients to update the features and thereby aligning the generated motions with user intentions while preserving fidelity. Quantitative experiments and user studies demonstrate that the freehand drawing approach reduces user time by approximately 46.7% when generating motions aligned with their imagination. The code, demos, and relevant data are publicly available at https://github.com/InvertedForest/DrawMotion.

</details>


### [116] [Preserve, Reveal, Expand: Faithful 4D Video Editing with Region-Aware Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20961)
*Zhangchi Hu,Wenzhang Sun,Xiangchen Yin,Jiahui Yuan,Chunfeng Wang,Hao Li,Kun Zhan,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PREX提出了一种区域感知框架，将目标时空体分解为保留、揭示和扩展三种角色，通过区域感知适配器将带校准置信度的观测外观线索注入冻结的视频扩散骨干，解决了4D视频编辑中证据-角色不匹配导致的保存漂移、鬼影和不稳定外推问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D驱动视频扩散模型主要关注合理生成，但在忠实4D编辑中，需要保留源观测区域的同时合成被遮挡或视角外的内容。作者发现了"证据-角色不匹配"问题：可靠的源证据、不可靠的渲染线索和未支持区域被混杂在单一条件信号中，导致保存漂移、鬼影和不稳定外推。

Method: 提出PREX框架，将目标时空体分解为Preserve（保留）、Reveal（揭示）和Expand（扩展）三种角色。构建带校准置信度的观测外观线索，通过区域感知适配器注入冻结的视频扩散骨干，并使用代理任务训练，无需成对编辑视频。同时提出了包含精心编辑、区域角色掩码和人类对齐指标的PREBench基准。

Result: 实验表明PREX减少了区域结构化的失败，同时保持了强大的视觉质量和4D编辑控制能力。

Conclusion: 通过区域感知的角色分解和适配器注入，PREX有效解决了4D视频编辑中的证据-角色不匹配问题，实现了更忠实的4D编辑，为复杂时空编辑任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing 4D-driven video diffusion models primarily target plausible generation, but faithful 4D editing requires preserving source-observed regions while synthesizing disoccluded or out-of-view content. We identify Evidence-Role Mismatch: reliable source-backed evidence, unreliable rendered cues, and unsupported regions are entangled in a single conditioning signal, causing preservation drift, ghosting, and unstable extrapolation. We propose PREX (Preserve, Reveal, Expand), a region-aware framework that decomposes the target spatiotemporal volume into Preserve, Reveal, and Expand roles according to observation support and scene extent. PREX builds observation-backed appearance cues with calibrated confidence and injects them into a frozen video diffusion backbone through a region-aware adapter, trained with proxy tasks without requiring paired edited videos. We further introduce PREBench, a diagnostic benchmark with curated edits, region-role masks, and human-aligned metrics that complement global video-quality and 4D-control evaluations. Experiments show that PREX reduces region-structured failures while maintaining strong visual quality and 4D edit control capability. Project Page: https://ricepastem.github.io/PREX-Open

</details>


### [117] [Towards UAV Detection in the Real World: A New Multispectral Dataset UAVNet-MS and a New Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20963)
*Yihang Luo,Jun Chen,Chao Xiao,Yingqian Wang,Zhaoxu Li,Qiang Ling,Xu He,Nuo Chen,Gaowei Guo,Hongge Li,Miao Li,Longguang Wang,Yulan Guo,Li Liu,Wei An,Zhijie Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于细粒度小型无人机检测的多光谱数据集UAVNet-MS（15,618组RGB-MSI数据），以及双流融合基线模型MFDNet，在极低对比度、小目标（平均18²像素）场景下，相比最优RGB方法获得了+6.2% AP50的提升，证明光谱线索对空间线索具有补充价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB无人机监控系统在小尺度、高类间相似性、目标-杂波模糊和低对比度场景下性能退化严重，而多光谱成像虽能编码物质感知的光谱特征，但缺乏专门的数据集和方法用于细粒度小型无人机检测。

Method: 构建UAVNet-MS多光谱数据集（15,618组RGB-MSI数据立方体，1440×1080分辨率，含边界框标注），并提出MFDNet双流基线模型，用于处理阵列引起的视差问题和空间-光谱特征融合。

Result: 在RGB-only、MSI-only和RGB+MSI三种协议下与20个检测器对比，MFDNet相比最佳RGB方法在AP50上提升了6.2%。

Conclusion: 光谱线索能提供超出空间线索的互补性物质证据，该工作为多光谱无人机监控研究提供了基础数据集、强基线方法和评测基准。

Abstract: The proliferation of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) has created urgent demand for precise UAV monitoring. Existing RGB-based systems rely on spatial cues that degrade at small scales, particularly with high inter-type similarity, target-clutter ambiguity, and low contrast. Multispectral imaging (MSI) encodes material-aware spectral signatures, yet MSI-based fine-grained small-UAV detection remains underexplored due to lack of dedicated datasets. We introduce UAVNet-MS, the first multispectral dataset for fine-grained small-UAV detection, comprising 15,618 temporally synchronized RGB-MSI data cubes (1440x1080) with bounding box annotations. The dataset features challenging small objects (93.7% <= 32^2 pixels, average 18^2 pixels, ~0.02% image area) under low contrast. We propose MFDNet, a dual-stream baseline addressing array-induced parallax and spatial-spectral fusion. Extensive evaluation under RGB-only, MSI-only, and RGB+MSI protocols against 20 detectors shows MFDNet achieves +6.2% AP50 improvement over best RGB-only methods, demonstrating spectral cues provide complementary material evidence beyond spatial cues. This work provides foundational dataset, strong baseline, and benchmark for multispectral UAV monitoring research.

</details>


### [118] [Towards Integrated Rock Support Visualisation in 3D Point Cloud of Underground Mines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20973)
*Dibyayan Patra,Simit Raval,Pasindu Ranasinghe,Bikram Banerjee,Ismet Canbulat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于3D点云的自动化框架，将结构映射、锚杆识别、不连续面拟合和锚杆方向估计整合为一体，实现地下矿山支护效果的综合可视化与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地下矿山中，不连续面特征描述和锚杆识别通常被视为独立任务，缺乏整合分析，限制了对支护效果的综合评估。

Method: 构建了统一工作流：结构映射→锚杆识别→不连续面拟合→锚杆方向估计，生成集成3D可视化，并结合立体图分析不连续面板点与锚杆方向，同时计算锚杆外露长度和偏离顶板法线的安装质量指标。

Result: 在真实地下金属矿山扫描数据上验证，该框架在中尺度点云上能够产生准确的结构映射和锚杆识别结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为自动化、集成化的岩土支持效果评估提供了实用方案，无需人工测量或额外的现场数据采集。

Abstract: The effectiveness of rock support in underground mines depends on the interaction between installed rock bolts and the structural fabric of the surrounding rock mass. However, discontinuity characterisation and rock bolt identification are commonly treated as separate tasks, limiting their value for integrated support assessment. This study presents an automated framework for integrated rock support visualisation using 3D point clouds of underground mine excavations. The framework integrates structure mapping, rock bolt identification, discontinuity plane fitting, and bolt orientation estimation into a unified workflow optimised for accuracy and computational efficiency. The outputs are used to generate an integrated 3D visualisation of fitted discontinuity planes and bolt vectors, enabling direct assessment of their spatial intersections and geometric relationships. A complementary stereographic analysis of discontinuity poles and bolt orientations is also performed to evaluate overall bolting geometric effectiveness relative to the mapped structural fabric. Additionally, bolt-level quality metrics, including exposed protrusion length and deviation from the local roof normal, are visualised to support assessment of installation quality. The proposed framework is demonstrated on real underground metal mine scans, producing accurate structure mapping and rock bolt identification results in medium-scale point clouds. Overall, the study provides a practical step towards automated, integrated geotechnical assessment of rock support effectiveness without requiring manual measurements or additional in-situ data acquisition.

</details>


### [119] [CHOIR: Contact-aware 4D Hand-Object Interaction Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20992)
*Hao Xu,Yilin Liu,Yinqiao Wang,Chi-Wing Fu,Niloy J. Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHOIR是一个从日常单目视频中重建手物交互（手部运动、物体形状与6D姿态、接触时空信息）的框架，通过将接触作为显式耦合信号，利用视觉先验初始化、空间矫正模块和接触感知联合优化，在物体重建、物理合理性和时序一致性上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前从单目视频重建手物交互（HOI）的方法通常假设已知物体或受控场景，且对手部和物体的独立估计在遮挡、杂乱背景和未见物体几何形状下容易产生错位。论文旨在解决这一痛点，实现从日常开放世界单目视频中规模化挖掘真实交互数据。

Method: CHOIR包含三个核心步骤：(1) 利用开放世界视觉先验初始化一个粗粒度的、不依赖接触的4D HOI序列；(2) 引入生成式HOI空间矫正模块，预测射线深度校正并修正手-物体相对位置，然后在矫正后的几何上推导初始逐帧接触对应关系；(3) 进行接触感知的联合优化，动态更新接触约束以强制满足几何、时序和接触一致性。

Result: 在受控和挑战性视频上的实验表明，CHOIR在物体重建质量、物理合理性和时序一致性上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了将接触作为手与物体之间的显式耦合信号，能够有效提升单目视频中手物交互重建的准确性和鲁棒性，为实现大规模真实交互数据挖掘、场景感知合成与规划提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We ask whether everyday open-world monocular videos can be turned into reusable 4D interaction primitives: articulated hand motion, object shape with 6D pose over time, and the when/where of contact. Such a capability would enable scalable mining of real interactions and, beyond reconstruction, support scene-aware synthesis and planning. However, reconstructing hand-object interaction (HOI) from challenging monocular videos remains difficult: methods often assume known objects or curated scenes, and separately estimated hands and objects easily become misaligned under clutter, occlusion, and unseen object geometries. Targeting this setting, we present CHOIR, a Contact-aware HOI Reconstruction framework for a monocular camera, using contact as an explicit coupling signal between hands and objects. CHOIR first initializes a coarse, contact-agnostic 4D HOI sequence from open-world visual priors. It then introduces a generative HOI spatial rectification module to predict ray-depth corrections and rectify hand-object relative placement, then derive initial per-frame contact correspondences on the rectified geometry. Last, a contact-aware joint optimization with dynamically updated contact constraints enforces geometric, temporal, and contact consistency. Experiments on controlled and challenging videos show that CHOIR improves object reconstruction, physical plausibility, and temporal consistency over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [120] [DAMA: Disentangled Body-Anchored Gaussians for Controllable Multi-Layered Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21001)
*Daniel Eskandar,Berna Kabadayi,Garvita Tiwari,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAMA是一种基于身体锚定高斯表示的3D着装人体重建方法，实现了物理上合理的多层服装重建，具备清晰的服装分离、显式堆叠控制和最先进的几何与穿透率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D着装人体重建方法虽视觉保真度高，但忽略了几何结构与物理合理性，存在服装边界模糊、无法控制堆叠顺序和层序问题。

Method: 将高斯锚定到SMPL-X面部网格，使用重心坐标和法线偏移参数化；从多视图图像中基于2D分割提升为身体锚定高斯，通过拓扑引导校正优化层，并联合优化几何与外观。

Result: 在4D-DRESS数据集（82个扫描）上，在几何重建、服装分离、穿透率和穿透深度方面达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: DAMA是首个从多视图图像实现物理合理分层、干净服装分离和显式堆叠控制的高斯人体重建方法，并支持用户自定义服装重排序和快速转换为模拟就绪网格。

Abstract: Existing 3D clothed avatar reconstruction methods achieve high visual fidelity but ignore geometric structure and physical plausibility. They either model clothed humans as a single deformable surface or attempt garment disentanglement without enforcing geometric constraints, resulting in ambiguous garment boundaries and no control over stacking or layer ordering. To address these limitations, we introduce DAMA (Disentangled body-Anchored Gaussians for Controllable Multi-layered Avatars), a 3D avatar reconstruction method that produces physically plausible clothed avatars through a dedicated representation and reconstruction method. At the representation level, we bind Gaussians to SMPL-X faces using barycentric in-plane coordinates and a positive normal offset. Based on this parameterization, the reconstruction method lifts 2D segmentations to body-anchored Gaussians, refines layers using topology-guided correction, and jointly optimizes geometry and appearance. DAMA is the first Gaussian avatar reconstruction method from multi-view images to achieve physically plausible layering, clean garment separation, and explicit stacking control. On the full 4D-DRESS dataset (82 scans), it achieves state-of-the-art performance in geometry reconstruction, garment separation, penetration rate, and penetration depth. The representation further supports user-defined garment reordering and fast conversion of body-conforming garments to simulation-ready meshes. Project Page: https://danieleskandar.github.io/dama/

</details>


### [121] [Towards Physically Consistent 4D Scene Reconstruction for Closed-loop Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21032)
*Bowyn Tan,Yutong Xie,Bai Huang,Fan Luo,Xiao Li,Naizheng Wang,Yang Guan,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出正交投影梯度（OPG）方法，通过解耦时空参数的信用分配问题，实现高保真街道场景重建，同时保持稳定的新视角合成与时序建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS及其4D扩展方法无法同时实现新视角合成与时变信息建模，根本原因在于单源观测中视角与时间的确定性耦合导致时空参数间的信用分配困境。

Method: 提出正交投影梯度（OPG）分层训练方法：先固定空间参数确保其完整性，再将时间更新限制在空间零空间中；同时引入时序正则化策略，基于外观一致演化的物理先验施加平滑约束。

Result: 实验表明该方法在保持稳定NVS能力的同时，在传统观测复现指标上表现优异，间接反映了对时序动态的建模能力。

Conclusion: 通过解耦时空参数实现了高保真街道场景重建，为端到端自动驾驶闭环仿真提供了有效方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity street scene reconstruction is pivotal for end-to-end autonomous driving simulation, where novel-view synthesis (NVS) and time-varying information modeling are two fundamental capabilities to facilitate closed-loop training. However, existing 3DGS methods and their 4D extensions fail to simultaneously achieve both. To bridge this gap, we establish an information-geometric diagnostic framework, revealing that this limitation stems from a credit assignment dilemma between spatial and temporal parameters. Specifically, the deterministic coupling between viewpoint and time in single-source observation creates a low-rank structure that induces massive null-space ambiguity between static view-dependent and dynamic time-varying components. Temporal information overshadows spatial cues, causing the estimation variance of spatial parameters to diverge. To address this issue, we propose Orthogonal Projected Gradient (OPG), a hierarchical training method designed to restore spatial identifiability. OPG prioritizes the integrity of spatial representations by securing them in an initial stage, then restricts temporal updates to the spatial null space, enabling proactive credit assignment. While OPG isolates temporal updates algebraically, Temporal Regularization Strategy is proposed to further refine the temporal solution space by imposing a smoothness constraint based on the physical prior of consistent appearance evolution, ensuring that the reconstructed scene remains physically consistent in closed-loop simulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method not only maintains stable NVS capabilities but also demonstrates superior performance in traditional observation-reproducing metrics, which indirectly reflect the capability of modeling temporal dynamics.

</details>


### [122] [Dynamic Video Generation: Shaping Video Generation Across Time and Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21042)
*Shikang Zheng,Jingkai Huang,Jiacheng Liu,Guantao Chen,Lixuan,Yuqi Lin,Peiliang Cai,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DVG框架，通过联合分配时空计算资源实现视频生成加速，最高达7倍加速（结合蒸馏达18倍），无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型逐时间步的迭代去噪计算量大，现有渐进式分辨率采样方法在视频领域面临时空需求多样、单一维度压缩加速有限或质量下降的问题。

Method: 提出DVG（Dynamic Video Generation）框架，动态联合分配时间与空间计算资源，自动选择内容感知的加速策略，无需手动调参或重新训练。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo和HunyuanVideo-1.5上实现最高7倍加速，结合蒸馏可达18倍加速，且近乎无损质量。

Conclusion: DVG可作为大规模高效视频生成系统的关键组件，实现跨模型、跨任务的近无损加速。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved impressive performance in video generation, but their iterative denoising process remains computationally expensive due to the large number of tokens processed at each timestep. Recently, progressive resolution sampling has emerged as a promising acceleration approach by reducing latent resolution in early stages. However, scaling this idea to video generation remains challenging, as the additional temporal dimension introduces diverse spatio-temporal demands across different videos, and compressing only a single dimension often leads to limited acceleration or degraded quality. Therefore, we propose DVG, a Dynamic Video Generation framework that jointly allocates computation across time and space, automatically selecting content-aware acceleration strategies without manual tuning or retraining. DVG achieves near-lossless acceleration across models and tasks, reaching up to 7 times speedup on HunyuanVideo and HunyuanVideo-1.5, and 18 times when combined with distillation, demonstrating its potential as a key component in today's large-scale efficient video generation systems. Our code is in supplementary material and will be released on Github.

</details>


### [123] [Multimodal LLMs under Pairwise Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21059)
*Yan Li,Yunlong Deng,Yuewen Sun,Gongxu Luo,Kun Zhang,Guangyi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅通过配对多模态数据（而非完整多模态对齐数据）训练多模态大语言模型的方法，包含潜在表示对齐和跨模态重组两个阶段，并在3D点云和触觉模态上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MLLM训练依赖人工构建的多模态对齐数据集，成本高且难以扩展。本文旨在探索仅利用配对模态数据（而非完整多路对齐数据）来训练MLLM，降低数据构建难度。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 潜在表示对齐阶段：通过自模态重建和配对对比学习学习共享潜在空间，并在对比学习中引入部分对齐和最小潜在规范的归纳偏置；2) 跨模态重组阶段：将新模态的编码器与预训练模态的解码器集成，实现跨模态迁移与生成。

Result: 在预训练MLLM中新增3D点云和触觉模态，使用三组模态配对进行实验，模型取得了强跨模态性能。

Conclusion: 仅利用配对模态数据即可有效学习对齐的潜在表示空间，实现跨模态迁移，为MLLM扩展到更多模态提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Despite the impressive results achieved by multimodal large language models (MLLMs), their training typically relies on jointly curated multimodal data, requiring substantial human effort to construct multi-way aligned datasets and thereby limiting scalability across domains. In this work, we explore training MLLMs by only leveraging multiple paired modalities as a surrogate for the full joint multimodal distribution. Specifically, we first provide a theoretical analysis of the conditions under which the representations are identifiable with only observing pairwise modalities. Building on this analysis, we propose a representation learning framework for aligning latent representations across modalities using only pairwise data. The framework consists of two stages: latent representation alignment and cross-modal recomposition. Specifically, in the first stage, we learn the shared latent space across modalities by both self-modal reconstruction and pair-wise contrastive learning. We also incorporate an inductive bias in the contrastive learning process by partially aligning and minimal latent specification. In stage two, we integrate the encoder of newly introduced modalities with the decoders of the pre-trained modalities to facilitate cross-modal transfer and generation. We evaluate our method by newly adding 3D point clouds and tactile modalities into pre-trained MLLMs with three modality pairs and show that, by learning an aligned latent representation space, our model achieves strong cross-modal performance.

</details>


### [124] [Q-ARVD: Quantizing Autoregressive Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21072)
*Siao Tang,Xinyin Ma,Gongfan Fang,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-ARVD框架，针对自回归视频扩散模型（ARVDs）的量化难题，解决了帧间量化敏感度极度不平衡和权重中异常值模式异构两大挑战，实现了高效的模型量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARVDs在流式视频生成中前景广阔，但其推理成本高昂阻碍实际部署。现有量化方法直接应用于ARVDs效果不佳，因为ARVDs存在独特的量化行为，亟需专门的研究。

Method: Q-ARVD包含两个核心策略：(S1) 最终质量感知的帧加权机制，解决帧间量化敏感度不平衡；(S2) 异常值感知的自适应双尺度量化，自动检测并隔离异构异常值通道，保护正常通道。

Result: 大量实验表明Q-ARVD框架在ARVD量化任务上取得了优越性能，显著优于直接应用现有量化方案的方法。

Conclusion: 本文系统分析了ARVDs量化的两大挑战，提出的Q-ARVD有效解决了这些问题，为自回归视频扩散模型的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models (ARVDs) have emerged as a promising architecture for streaming video generation, paving the way for real-time interactive video generation and world modeling. Despite their potential, the substantial inference cost of ARVDs remains a major obstacle to practical deployment, making model quantization a natural direction for improving efficiency. However, quantization for ARVDs remains largely unexplored. Our empirical analysis shows that directly applying existing quantization schemes developed for standard diffusion transformers to ARVDs leads to suboptimal performance, revealing quantization behaviors that differ from those observed in bidirectional diffusion models. In this paper, we identify two critical challenges in quantizing ARVDs: (C1) Highly unbalanced frame-wise quantization sensitivity. Error accumulation during autoregressive generation can induce severely skewed quantization sensitivity across frames, following an exponential-like decay pattern. (C2) Prominent and heterogeneous outlier patterns in weights. Weight distributions exhibit pronounced outlier channels, whose patterns vary substantially across layer types and block depths. To address these issues, we propose Q-ARVD, a novel framework for accurate ARVD quantization. (S1) To tackle the highly unbalanced frame-wise sensitivity, Q-ARVD incorporates a final-quality aware frame-weighting mechanism into the quantization objective. (S2) To prevent heterogeneous outliers from degrading performance, Q-ARVD introduces an outlier-aware adaptive dual-scale quantization, which automatically detects the presence and quantity of outlier channels for an arbitrary layer, and isolates them to protect normal channels. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of Q-ARVD.

</details>


### [125] [SpectralEarth-FM: Bringing Hyperspectral Imagery into Multimodal Earth Observation Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21075)
*Nassim Ait Ali Braham,Aaron Banze,Conrad M. Albrecht,Julien Mairal,Jocelyn Chanussot,Xiao Xiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了 SpectralEarth-FM，一个能够联合处理高光谱影像与多种低光谱遥感数据（Sentinel-2、Landsat-8/9、Sentinel-1 SAR 等）的分层 Transformer 基础模型，并构建了包含约 2M 全球分布位置、25M 地理参考图块、超 40TB 数据的多传感器配对数据集 SpectralEarth-MM，采用 JEPA 风格预训练，在 HSI 下游任务和通用 EO 基准上均达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EO 基础模型多使用多光谱、SAR 等数据，高光谱影像覆盖不足；而现有 HSI 基础模型又仅用 HSI 单独训练，未探索 HSI 与其他遥感数据的联合预训练与融合。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 设计分层 Transformer 架构，包含光谱分词器、各传感器专用编码器、跨传感器融合模块和共享分层编码器；构建 SpectralEarth-MM 多传感器配对数据集（EnMAP/EMIT/DESIS + Sentinel-2/Landsat-8-9/Sentinel-1/LST）；采用 JEPA 自监督预训练，匹配全局视图与单传感器局部视图的表征。

Result: 在 HSI 下游任务和遵循 PANGAEA 协议的通用 EO 基准评估中，均取得最先进（SOTA）结果。

Conclusion: 证明了 HSI 与多传感器数据的联合预训练能够有效提升模型性能，SpectralEarth-FM 为多传感器融合的 EO 基础模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) foundation models (FMs) are increasingly trained on multisensor data, spanning multispectral imagery (MSI), synthetic aperture radar (SAR), and derived geospatial layers, but hyperspectral imagery (HSI) remains underrepresented. Conversely, existing hyperspectral FMs are trained on HSI alone, leaving joint pretraining and fusion of HSI with co-located EO sensors unexplored. We introduce SpectralEarth-FM, a hierarchical transformer for multisensor EO input with heterogeneous spectral dimensionality. The architecture combines spectral tokenization for hyperspectral inputs, sensor-specific encoders, a cross-sensor fusion module, and a shared hierarchical encoder, enabling joint processing of HSI and lower-channel observations. To pretrain SpectralEarth-FM, we curate SpectralEarth-MM, a dataset that co-locates HSI from three spaceborne sensors (EnMAP, EMIT, DESIS) with Sentinel-2, Landsat-8/9 optical imagery, Landsat land surface temperature (LST), and Sentinel-1 SAR, over common geographic footprints. It comprises approximately 2M globally distributed locations, 25M georeferenced patches, and over 40TB of data. Pretraining uses a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA)-style objective that matches representations between global views and single-sensor local views from the same location. We evaluate SpectralEarth-FM on hyperspectral downstream tasks and standard EO benchmarks following the PANGAEA protocol, achieving state-of-the-art results across both evaluation settings.

</details>


### [126] [VDFP: Video Deflickering with Flicker-banding Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21079)
*Zhiyi Zhou,Libo Zhu,Zihan Zhou,Yulun Zhang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对手机拍摄屏幕出现的频闪条纹问题，本文构建了真实数据集DeViD，并提出基于滚动快门机制建模的退化场模型DFM和空间-时间连续先验感知CPP，通过零初始化增强输入层保留预训练生成先验，有效消除复杂频带并保持高保真空间细节和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手机屏幕拍摄时因硬件同步不匹配产生严重频闪条纹，现有视频修复方法难以处理这种结构化周期性亮度波动，且缺乏真实场景数据集。

Method: 1) 构建真实场景数据集DeViD；2) 提出退化场建模(DFM)模拟复杂多频带场景；3) 提出空间-时间连续先验感知(CPP)，配合Flicker-Aware MSE损失；4) 零初始化增强输入层以保留预训练生成先验。

Result: VDFP显著优于现有方法，能消除复杂频带条纹，同时保持高保真空间细节和时间一致性。

Conclusion: VDFP框架有效解决了手机拍摄屏幕的频闪去噪问题，数据集和代码将开源。

Abstract: Capturing digital screens with smartphones frequently induces severe banding due to hardware synchronization mismatches. Existing video restoration methods struggle with these structured, periodic luminance fluctuations, often resulting in residual artifacts or over-smoothed textures. We firstly construct DeViD, a real-world dataset in various scenes to deal with the lack of available datasets.Then we propose VDFP (Video Deflickering with Flicker-banding Priors), a novel perception-guided generation framework. First, we introduce a Degradation Field Modeling Based on Rolling Shutter Mechanism (DFM) capable of synthesizing complex multi-banding scenarios. Second, we present a spatial-temporal continuous prior perception (CPP). Unlike traditional binary segmentation, this module is optimized via a Flicker-Aware Mean Squared Error (FA-MSE) to capture the luminance transitions. By zero-initializing an augmented input layer, our model preserves pre-trained generative priors as well as spatial-temporal prior perception. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VDFP significantly outperforms other methods, eliminating complex banding with high-fidelity spatial details and temporal consistency. Our dataset and code will be released at~ https://github.com/ZhiyiZZhou/VDFP.

</details>


### [127] [TextSculptor: Training and Benchmarking Scene Text Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21090)
*Yiheng Lin,Siyu Jiao,Xiaohan Lan,Wei Zhou,Qi She,Fei Yu,Heyun Chen,Zhengwei Wang,Jinghuan Chen,Moran Li,Yingchen Yu,Zijian Feng,Yao Zhao,Yunchao Wei,Yujie Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TextSculptor框架，包含自动化数据构建流程、大规模数据集TextSculpt-Data（320万样本）和基准TextSculpt-Bench（涵盖四种文字编辑任务），旨在提升开放场景文字编辑性能，缩小与商业模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM和扩散模型在提示驱动的图像编辑上取得进展，但场景文字编辑仍具挑战：当前开源模型落后于闭源系统，主要原因是高质量训练数据稀缺且缺乏标准化评测基准。

Method: (1) 构建自动化数据流水线，结合文字感知图像合成与程序化文字渲染合成；(2) 构建TextSculpt-Data数据集（320万样本），含120万OCR验证的文生图样本和200万成对文字编辑样本；(3) 提出TextSculpt-Bench基准，覆盖文字添加、替换、移除、混合编辑四类任务；(4) 设计评估协议，通过OCR文本对齐、多模态判断和背景区域相似度衡量文字准确度、视觉质量和背景保持度。

Result: 大量实验表明TextSculptor显著提升了开源模型的文字编辑性能，缩小了与商业模型的差距。

Conclusion: TextSculptor通过大规模高质量数据构建和全面评测基准，有效推动了开源场景文字编辑的发展，弥合了与闭源模型间的差距。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and diffusion-based generative models have substantially improved prompt-driven image editing. However, scene text editing remains challenging, as it requires models to precisely modify textual content while preserving visual realism and non-target regions. Current open-source models still lag behind proprietary systems, largely due to the scarcity of high-quality training data and the lack of standardized benchmarks tailored to text editing. To address these challenges, we present TextSculptor, a comprehensive framework for data construction and evaluation of scene text editing. We first develop an automated data construction pipeline that combines text-aware image synthesis with programmatic text rendering and compositing. Based on this pipeline, we build TextSculpt-Data, a large-scale dataset containing 3.2M training samples, including 1.2M OCR-verified text-to-image samples and 2M paired text editing samples with naturally aligned source-target images and strong background consistency. We further introduce TextSculpt-Bench, a benchmark covering four fundamental text editing tasks: text addition, text replacement, text removal, and hybrid editing. To support reliable evaluation, we design a tailored protocol that measures text accuracy, visual quality, and background preservation through OCR-based text alignment, multimodal judgment, and background-region similarity. Extensive experiments show that TextSculptor improves open-source text editing performance and narrows the gap to proprietary models. The data and benchmark are available at https://github.com/linyiheng123/TextSculptor.

</details>


### [128] [R2AoP: Reliable and Robust Angle of Progression Estimation from Intrapartum Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21099)
*Yuanhan Wang,Yifei Chen,Beining Wu,Mingxuan Liu,Xiaotian Hu,Chunbo Jiang,Yijin Li,Changmiao Wang,Feiwei Qin,Qiyuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出R2AoP框架，通过结构增强分割和置信度引导建模，实现产时超声中进展角度（AoP）的稳健估计，在多中心数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AoP准确估计对客观评估产程进展至关重要，但受成像噪声、边界模糊和局部分割误差几何放大的影响，现有方法不够稳健可靠。

Method: 1) 三分支局部结构增强骨干网络，改进耻骨联合(PS)和胎头(FH)分割；2) 置信度加权轮廓拟合，抑制不可靠边界点；3) 轻量级几何可靠测试时自适应策略，无需目标标注即可稳定推理。

Result: 在多中心基准上，与SOTA方法相比，AoP误差和边界指标均有一致降低。

Conclusion: R2AoP通过结构感知分割与置信度引导几何建模，有效提升了AoP估计的鲁棒性和可重复性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of the Angle of Progression (AoP) from intrapartum transperineal ultrasound is critical for objective assessment of labor progression, yet remains highly sensitive to imaging noise, boundary ambiguities, and the geometric amplification of local segmentation errors. We propose R2AoP, a reliable and robust AoP estimation framework that integrates structurally informed segmentation and confidence-guided geometric modeling to achieve stable and reproducible measurements. A three-branch local-structure-enhanced backbone improves the delineation of the pubic symphysis (PS) and fetal head (FH), while confidence-weighted contour fitting explicitly suppresses the influence of unreliable boundary points in AoP computation. To further improve performance under heterogeneous acquisition conditions, we introduce a lightweight geometry-reliable test-time adaptation strategy as an auxiliary component, enabling stable inference without target annotations. Extensive evaluations on multi-center benchmarks demonstrate consistent reductions in AoP error and boundary metrics compared with state-of-the-art AoP methods. Our source code is available at https://github.com/baiyou1234/R2AoP.

</details>


### [129] [RCGDet3D: Rethinking 4D Radar-Camera Fusion-based 3D Object Detection with Enhanced Radar Feature Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21112)
*Weiyi Xiong,Bing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RCGDet3D，通过增强雷达特征提取（而非复杂融合策略）在4D汽车雷达点云稀疏场景下实现高效3D目标检测，性能超越现有方法且保持实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D雷达-相机融合方法依赖复杂融合模块，虽能提升精度但计算开销大、推理速度慢，难以满足实时部署需求；而雷达点云特征提取本身计算量小但未被充分探索，因此本文旨在验证增强雷达特征提取能否替代复杂融合，兼顾精度与速度。

Method: 提出RCGDet3D，核心是改进雷达特征编码并简化多模态融合。具体包括：（1）Ray-centric PGE（R-PGE），在射线对齐坐标系中预测高斯属性再统一投影到BEV空间，解耦坐标变换以提升几何一致性；（2）Semantic Injection（SI）模块，从图像中注入视觉语义线索，丰富雷达特征的几何精度与语义信息。

Result: 在VoD和TJ4DRadSet数据集上，RCGDet3D在检测精度和推理速度上均超越现有最先进方法，树立了实时部署的新标杆。

Conclusion: 本文证明，通过充分挖掘雷达自身特征提取能力，可以在不依赖复杂融合策略的情况下实现更优的3D检测性能，为4D雷达在自动驾驶中的实时应用提供了高效方案。

Abstract: 4D automotive radar is indispensable for autonomous driving due to its low cost and robustness, yet its point cloud sparsity challenges 3D object detection. Existing 4D radar-camera fusion methods focus on complex fusion strategies, trading inference speed for marginal gains. This trade-off hinders real-time deployment due to heavy computation on dense feature maps. In contrast, feature extraction from sparse radar points is less time-consuming but remains under-explored. This work uncovers that simply enhancing radar feature extraction can achieve comparable or even higher performance than elaborate fusion modules, while maintaining real-time performance. Based on this finding, we propose RCGDet3D, which centers on radar feature encoding and simplifies multi-modal fusion. Its encoder inherits from the efficient Gaussian Splatting-based Point Gaussian Encoder (PGE) in RadarGaussianDet3D with two key improvements. First, the Ray-centric PGE (R-PGE) predicts Gaussian attributes in ray-aligned coordinate systems before unifying them to Bird's-Eye View (BEV) space, significantly improving geometric consistency and reducing learning difficulty by decoupling the coordinate transformation from representation learning. Second, a Semantic Injection (SI) module incorporates visual cues from images, producing more geometrically accurate and semantically enriched radar features. Experiments on View-of-Delft (VoD) and TJ4DRadSet show that RCGDet3D outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both accuracy and speed, setting a new benchmark for real-time deployment.

</details>


### [130] [ROAR-3D: Routing Arbitrary Views for High-Fidelity 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21121)
*Hanxiao Sun,Mingxin Yang,Shuhui Yang,Zebin He,Xintong Han,Hongbo Fu,Chunchao Guo,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ROAR-3D，一种轻量级方法，通过解耦方向控制与几何传输，将预训练的单视图3D生成模型升级为支持任意数量未标定视角的多视图模型，实现SOTA效果且仅增加少量参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单视图3D生成存在遮挡区域模糊性；多视图方法要么需要固定视角，要么依赖外部重建模块导致训练成本高、生成质量受限。作者希望复用预训练单视图模型的能力，但发现其条件机制将方向控制与几何传输纠缠在一起，多视图混合时会冲突。

Method: 1) Token-wise View Router：为每个3D隐式token分配最相关视图，隐式建立2D-3D对应关系，无需显式位姿输入。2) Dual-stream Attention：保留预训练主视角行为，辅助视图通过独立路径进行几何增强。3) Orientation Perturbation：确保辅助路径学习与方向无关的几何传输。

Result: 在多项多视图3D生成任务上达到SOTA质量，支持测试时从1到12+个视图的扩展，且性能持续提升。相比单视图基线仅增加极小的推理开销。

Conclusion: 通过分析并解耦单视图模型中的方向控制与几何传输，ROAR-3D以极轻量方式实现了高效的多视图条件3D生成，验证了复用预训练知识的有效性。

Abstract: Single-image-to-3D generative models can now produce high-quality geometry, yet conditioning on a single view inevitably introduces ambiguity about unseen regions. Multi-view conditioning can reduce this ambiguity, but existing methods either require fixed canonical viewpoints or rely on external reconstruction modules that impose heavy training costs and limit generation quality. We observe that pretrained single-view models already possess strong 2D-to-3D grounding that can be reused for multi-view conditioning. However, a closer analysis reveals that their conditioning mechanism entangles orientation control with geometry transfer, two functions that conflict when images from different viewpoints are naively combined. Based on this analysis, we propose ROAR-3D, a lightweight method that upgrades a pretrained single-view model to accept an arbitrary number of unposed images. A token-wise view router assigns each 3D latent token to its most relevant view, implicitly establishing 2D-to-3D correspondences without explicit pose input. A dual-stream attention design preserves the pretrained primary-view behavior while routing auxiliary views through a separate path dedicated to geometric enrichment. An orientation perturbation strategy ensures the auxiliary path learns orientation-independent geometry transfer. These components introduce minimal trainable parameters and add negligible inference overhead relative to the single-view baseline. ROAR-3D achieves state-of-the-art multi-view 3D generation quality and supports test-time view scaling from 1 to 12+ views with consistent improvements.

</details>


### [131] [Linear-DPO: Linear Direct Preference Optimization for Diffusion and Flow-Matching Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21123)
*Kesong Li,Yixuan Xu,Kuo-kun Tseng,Weiyi Lu,Kan Liu,Tao Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Linear-DPO，通过统一扩散模型与流匹配的逆向SDE框架，将标准DPO中的sigmoid效用函数替换为线性效用函数，并引入EMA更新参考模型，有效解决了文本到图像生成中DPO的目标不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法在文本到图像生成中面临两个问题：一是局限于去噪扩散模型而忽略了流匹配模型；二是将离散NLP领域DPO直接应用于回归式生成任务存在目标不匹配。

Method: 通过统一的逆向时间SDE框架推导出覆盖扩散和流匹配的广义DPO目标；从梯度角度分析指出标准DPO的sigmoid效用函数过于激进；提出Linear-DPO，采用持续线性效用函数代替sigmoid，并引入EMA更新的参考模型。

Result: 在扩散模型（SD1.5、SDXL）和流匹配模型（SD3-Medium）上的定性和定量实验均表明Linear-DPO优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: Linear-DPO通过线性效用函数和EMA参考模型，有效解决了文本到图像生成中DPO的目标不匹配问题，并在多种模型架构上展现了优越性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is successful for alignment in LLMs but still faces challenges in text-to-image generation. Existing studies are confined to denoising diffusion models while overlooking flow-matching, and suffer from an objective mismatch when applying discrete NLP-based DPO to regression-based generative tasks.\ In this paper, we derive a generalized DPO objective that covers both diffusion and flow-matching via a unified reverse-time SDE framework, and point out from a gradient perspective that the standard DPO objective is suboptimal for text-to-image generation. Consequently, we propose Linear-DPO, which replaces the aggressive sigmoid-based utility function with a sustained linear utility and incorporates an EMA-updated reference model. Qualitative and quantitative experiments on diffusion models (SD1.5, SDXL) and flow-matching model (SD3-Medium) demonstrate the superiority of our approach over existing baselines.

</details>


### [132] [VersusQ: Pairwise Margin Reasoning for Generalizable Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21130)
*Shibei Meng,Binxin Yang,Yuan Liu,Jiexuan Zhang,Zhengyao Lv,Hubery Yin,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VersusQ框架，通过成对比较而非绝对评分进行视频质量评估，利用Margin-Coupled GRPO优化，在多个 benchmarks 上达到SOTA性能并展现强跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMMs视频质量评估方法采用绝对分数预测，容易混入数据集特定的校准偏差（如标注协议、评分习惯、分数分布），导致跨域泛化能力差。作者认为相对比较可以消除绝对尺度校准偏差，专注于感知差异。

Method: 提出VersusQ成对边际推理框架：1）基于LMM对两个视频进行直接比较；2）推理视觉和时间质量差异；3）预测带符号的连续边际值（表示偏好选择及差异程度）。同时提出Margin-Coupled GRPO，联合优化rollout-based关系推理和连续边际回归。

Result: 在多个公开VQA基准上达到SOTA性能，展现出强跨域泛化能力，以及在不同评测场景下的可靠细粒度排序能力。

Conclusion: 成对比较范式能够有效消除数据集特定的评分校准偏差，提升VQA模型的跨域泛化能力；Margin-Coupled GRPO有效对齐了解释性推理与细粒度数值差异预测。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have shown promise for video quality assessment, but most methods still predict an absolute score for each video. Such pointwise supervision often mixes perceptual quality with dataset-specific calibration, including annotation protocols, rating habits, and score distributions. As a result, the learned scoring rule may work well within a benchmark but transfer poorly across unseen domains. We argue that relative comparisons alleviate the absolute-scale calibration bias by focusing purely on perceptual differences rather than dataset-specific rating habits. Consequently, we propose \textbf{VersusQ}, a pairwise margin reasoning framework driven entirely by direct comparisons. Specifically, VersusQ performs LMM-based comparison between two videos, reasons about their visual and temporal quality differences, and predicts a signed continuous margin that captures both the preferred choice and the degree of difference. Furthermore, to align interpretable comparison rationales with fine-grained numerical differences, we introduce Margin-Coupled GRPO, which jointly optimizes rollout-based relational reasoning and continuous margin regression. Extensive experiments on multiple public VQA benchmarks demonstrate that VersusQ achieves state-of-the-art performance, strong cross-domain generalization, and reliable fine-grained ranking under heterogeneous evaluation scenarios.

</details>


### [133] [UniT: Unified Geometry Learning with Group Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21131)
*Haotian Wang,Yusong Huang,Zhaonian Kuang,Hongliang Lu,Xinhu Zheng,Meng Yang,Gang Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniT是一个基于分组自回归Transformer的统一模型，通过将传感器观测数据分组作为基本自回归单元，以无锚点和尺度自适应方式预测点地图，从而统一在线感知、离线重建、多模态融合、长时程扩展和度量尺度估计等多种几何感知能力，在十个基准测试的七个任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈模型在几何感知方面虽取得进展，但关键能力仍分散在多个不兼容的范式（如在线感知、离线重建、多模态融合、长时程可扩展性和度量尺度估计）中，缺乏统一的框架。

Method: 提出Group Autoregressive Transformer，将传感器观测分组作为基本自回归单元，以无锚点和尺度自适应方式预测点地图；在线/离线模式通过调整组大小统一；引入队列式KV缓存机制实现长时程有界记忆；提出尺度自适应几何损失来改善度量尺度泛化；设计模态注意力模块整合辅助模态信息。

Result: 在十个基准测试的七个代表性任务上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: UniT通过统一框架有效整合了多种几何感知能力，验证了分组自回归建模在统一几何感知中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent feed-forward models have significantly advanced geometry perception for inferring dense 3D structure from sensor observations. However, its essential capabilities remain fragmented across multiple incompatible paradigms, including online perception, offline reconstruction, multi-modal integration, long-horizon scalability, and metric-scale estimation. We present UniT, a unified model built upon a novel Group Autoregressive Transformer, which reformulates these seemingly disparate capabilities within a single framework. The key idea is to treat groups of sensor observations as the basic autoregressive units and predict the corresponding point maps in an anchor-free and scale-adaptive manner. More specifically, diverse view configurations in both online and offline settings are naturally unified within a single group autoregression process. By varying the group size, online mode operates over multiple autoregressive steps with single-frame groups, whereas offline mode aggregates a multi-frame group in a single forward pass. Meanwhile, a queue-style KV caching mechanism ensures bounded autoregressive memory over long horizons. This is enabled by reducing long-range dependencies on early frames through anchor-free relational modeling, thereby allowing outdated memory to be discarded on the fly. To improve metric-scale generalization across scenes, a scale-adaptive geometry loss is further introduced within this framework. It couples relative geometric constraints with a partial absolute scale term, implicitly regularizing global scale and inducing a progressive transition from scale-invariant geometry to metric-scale solutions. Together with a dedicated modal attention module for integrating auxiliary modalities, UniT achieves state-of-the-art performance in unified geometry perception, as validated on ten benchmarks spanning seven representative tasks.

</details>


### [134] [SurgOnAir: Hierarchy-Aware Real-Time Surgical Video Commentary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21132)
*Jingyi He,Yue Zhou,Long Bai,Kun Yuan,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgOnAir，一个流式视觉语言模型，能够逐帧实时生成手术工作流的多层级叙事描述，并显式标记关键状态转换，实现细粒度的帧到令牌生成，从而支持即时响应手术动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术工作流理解方法多为离线或在粗时间尺度上运行，只能在处理完视频片段后生成描述，无法对手术中细微的器械运动和不断变化的组织状态等实时动态做出即时响应，存在感知延迟，可能影响辅助决策或危及手术安全。

Method: 提出SurgOnAir流式视觉语言模型，逐帧顺序处理视频帧（无未来信息访问），随视觉输入到达逐步生成叙事令牌；构建SurgOnAir-11k分层数据集（包含动作、步骤、阶段三级监督）；训练模型生成多层级文本响应，并通过特殊转换令牌显式标记工作流状态变化。

Result: 实验表明，SurgOnAir能够通过单一视觉语言模型统一流式理解手术工作流的多个层级，生成具有层级感知的高质量叙事描述，实现实时理解。

Conclusion: SurgOnAir首次实现了手术工作流的流式实时多层级理解，通过帧到令牌的细粒度生成和显式状态转换标记，为智能手术辅助系统提供了实时感知与响应的基础能力。

Abstract: Understanding surgical workflow in real time is fundamental for intelligent surgical embodiment, where AI systems continuously perceive and respond as surgery proceeds. In the operating room, critical decisions depend on subtle, moment-to-moment changes, such as fine instrument movements and evolving tissue states, where even slight perceptual delays can limit assistance or compromise safety. Yet existing methods remain offline or operate at coarse temporal scales, generating descriptions only after processing clips, preventing immediate reaction. We address this by proposing SurgOnAir, a streaming vision-language model that processes frames sequentially without future access and progressively generates narration tokens as visual input arrives. SurgOnAir achieves fine-grained frame-to-token generation, enabling instant responsiveness to evolving surgical dynamics. Built upon our curated hierarchical dataset SurgOnAir-11k spanning action-, step-, and phase-level supervision, the model is trained to produce multi-level textual responses that reflect the inherent hierarchy of surgical procedures. Furthermore, special transition tokens are generated to explicitly mark state changes, allowing SurgOnAir to capture and signal key workflow transitions as they occur. Experiments show that SurgOnAir enables real-time understanding through a single vision-language model that unifies streaming across multiple hierarchies of the surgical workflow, generating superior and hierarchy-aware narrations. Code and dataset will be public.

</details>


### [135] [Distill to Think, Foresee to Act: Cognitive-Physical Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21139)
*Yang Wu,Qiang Meng,Zhaojiang Liu,Youquan Liu,Jian Yang,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoPhy框架，通过蒸馏VLM知识到BEV编码器实现认知能力（零推理成本），结合自回归BEV世界模型进行预测，利用GRPO和双奖励机制（物理奖励+认知奖励）优化驾驶策略，在NAVSIM基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶受限于行为克隆的上限，强化学习虽好但缺少两个基础设施：理解交通语义和驾驶意图的认知基础，以及能预测候选动作后果的远见物理环境。

Method: (1) 将VLM知识蒸馏到BEV编码器后丢弃VLM，实现零推理成本的认知能力；(2) 构建自回归BEV世界模型，基于候选动作预测未来语义图；(3) 采用GRPO优化策略，设计双奖励机制：物理奖励（BEV rollout保障安全约束）和认知奖励（语言对齐评分器确保意图符合）。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2基准上达到SOTA结果，实现更安全的驾驶（基于认知的场景合规）和灵活的用户语言指令控制。

Conclusion: CoPhy通过认知-物理双基础设施有效突破了行为克隆天花板，兼顾安全性与意图可控性。

Abstract: Current end-to-end autonomous driving models are fundamentally constrained by the behavioral cloning ceiling of imitation learning. While reinforcement learning offers a path to smarter autonomy, it demands two missing pieces of infrastructure: (1) a cognitive foundation that understands traffic semantics and driving intent, and (2) a foresighted physical environment that can anticipate the consequences of candidate actions. To this end, we propose CoPhy, a CognitivePhysical reinforcement learning framework for autonomous driving. To distill to think, we distill VLM knowledge into the BEV encoder and then discard the VLM entirely, retaining cognitive ability at zero inference cost while releasing the cognitive channel as a pluggable interface for optional human language commands. To foresee to act, we build an auto-regressive BEV world model that explicitly predicts future semantic maps conditioned on candidate actions, serving as an interpretable physical sandbox from which safety metrics are directly derived. Built upon this dual infrastructure, we optimize the driving policy via GRPO with a novel dual-reward mechanism: a physical reward derived from BEV rollouts enforces hard safety constraints, while a cognitive reward from a language-aligned scorer ensures intent compliance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoPhy not only achieves state-of-the-art results on NAVSIM v1 and v2 benchmarks, but also enables safer driving via cognitively informed scene compliance and flexible intent control through user-defined language instructions.

</details>


### [136] [FTerViT: Fully Ternary Vision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21171)
*Szymon Ruciński,Pietro Bonazzi,Engin Türetken,Simon Narduzzi,Michele Magno,Nadim Maamari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FTerViT，一种完全三值化的视觉Transformer，所有权重矩阵和归一化参数均被三值化，通过新算子TernaryBitConv2d和TernaryLayerNorm实现，结合知识蒸馏与量化感知恢复训练，在微控制器上首次部署三值ViT，实现15倍压缩且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三值ViT方法仅对编码器层进行三值化，而patch嵌入、LayerNorm参数和分类头仍保留全精度，在资源受限的微控制器上这些全精度组件主导了内存占用，严重限制了部署效率。本文旨在实现完全三值化以解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 1) 提出TernaryBitConv2d（逐通道缩放的三值卷积）用于patch嵌入；2) 提出TernaryLayerNorm用于归一化层三值化；3) 采用知识蒸馏训练，随后进行轻量级量化感知恢复训练。

Result: W2A8 DeiT-III-S在384×384分辨率下ImageNet-1K top-1达82.43%，6.09MB（~15倍压缩，相比FP32仅降2.42个百分点），优于先前方法最多8个百分点。在ESP32-S3微控制器上成功部署FTerViT-Small，79.64% top-1精度。

Conclusion: 首次实现完全三值化ViT并在微控制器上成功部署，证明极低比特量化ViT在资源极度受限设备上的可行性，为边缘AI视觉任务提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Ternary Vision Transformers offer substantial model compression, however state-of-the-art methods only ternarize the encoder layers, leaving patch embeddings, LayerNorm parameters, and classifier heads in full precision. In compact models targeting resource-constrained processors, such as microcontrollers, these remaining full-precision components determine the total memory footprint, severely limiting deployment efficiency and on-device feasibility. In this work, we introduce a fully ternarized Vision Transformer in which \emph{all} weight matrices and normalization parameters are ternarized (FTerViT). To this end, we introduce two novel operators : TernaryBitConv2d with per-channel scaling for patch embedding and TernaryLayerNorm. FTerViT is trained using knowledge distillation, followed by a lightweight quantization-aware recovery phase. Our ternary W2A8 DeiT-III-S at 384$\times$384 resolution achieves 82.43\% ImageNet-1K top-1 at 6.09\,MB (${\sim}$15$\times$ compression, $-$2.42\,pp vs.\ FP32), outperforming prior ternary ViTs methods up to 8 pp. Finally, we demonstrate the first implementation of ternary vision transformers on a dual cores XTensa LX7 microcontroller inside the ESP32-S3 system-on-chip. By deploying FTerViT-Small (based on DeiT-III-Small at 224$\times$224 resolution, 5.81\,MB), we achieve 79.64\% ImageNet-1K top-1 accuracy.

</details>


### [137] [Semantic Granularity Navigation in Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21190)
*Liangsi Lu,Minzhe Guo,Xuhang Chen,Yang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NaviEdit是一种无需训练的推理时控制方法，通过解耦编辑进度与模型尺度遍历，在保持结构保真度的同时提升语义可编辑性，并在多个编辑器和骨架网络上取得正向增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散/流模型的图像编辑方法存在语义可编辑性与结构保真度之间的权衡，根源在于编辑进度与模型尺度隐式耦合——更强的编辑需要访问更高噪声状态，在语义变化定位前就破坏了布局结构。

Method: NaviEdit是一种训练无关的推理时控制方法，通过严格的自我一致性约束（self-consistency contract）将编辑进度与模型尺度遍历解耦。它将尺度作为控制输入，将固定步长预算重新分配给语义响应更敏感的中间尺度，而非破坏性的高噪声区域，在rollout层级操作，不修改底层预训练模型。

Result: 在多个兼容编辑器和流骨架网络上取得正向平均增益，证明解耦策略是一种可移植的推理时控制原则。

Conclusion: NaviEdit通过解耦编辑进度与尺度遍历，有效打破了现有范式中的固有权衡，为图像编辑提供了一种轻量、可迁移的推理时控制方案。

Abstract: Despite the generative capabilities of diffusion and flow models, real-image editing remains constrained by a persistent trade-off between semantic editability and structural fidelity. We trace a primary cause of this limitation to the implicit coupling of edit progress with model scale in existing paradigms. Under this coupling, stronger edits typically require visiting noisier states, which spends computation on destabilizing layout before the semantic change is well localized. We introduce NaviEdit, a training-free inference-time controller that decouples edit progress from model scale traversal through a strict self-consistency contract. NaviEdit operates at the rollout level and leaves the underlying pretrained model unchanged. It treats scale as a control input and reallocates a fixed step budget toward semantically responsive intermediate scales instead of destructive high-noise regimes. Experiments show positive average gains across compatible editors and flow backbones, supporting decoupling as a portable inference-time control principle.

</details>


### [138] [RankE: End-to-End Post-Training for Discrete Text-to-Image Generation with Decoder Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21195)
*Siyong Jian,Siyuan Li,Luyuan Zhang,Zedong Wang,Xin Jin,Ying Li,Cheng Tan,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RankE，首个针对离散自回归文生图模型的端到端后训练框架。现有方法仅优化策略网络而冻结VQ解码器，导致潜在协变量偏移——策略更新使token分布偏离解码器训练时的真实分布，造成奖励提升但图像质量下降。RankE通过交替优化策略和解码器，并引入排序对齐目标和稳定性锚点正则化，打破保真度-对齐度权衡，在多个模型上同时提升了FID和CLIP分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散自回归文生图模型的后训练仅优化策略网络而冻结VQ解码器，导致潜在协变量偏移（Latent Covariate Shift）：策略更新使token分布偏离解码器训练分布，奖励提升但解码图像质量下降。目前缺乏针对离散AR模型的类似扩散模型中REPA-E那样的VAE瓶颈研究。

Method: 提出RankE端到端后训练框架，通过交替优化（alternating optimization）协同演化策略和解码器两个组件：每个模块在优化时最大化基于排序的对齐目标（ranking-based alignment objective），同时引入适合各自参数空间的稳定性锚点正则化（stability-preserving anchor）。

Result: 在LlamaGen-XL (775M)上，标准RL仅提升CLIP但恶化FID，而RankE同时提升两者（MS-COCO 30K上FID 15.21, CLIP 33.76）。在Janus-Pro (1B)上取得一致增益，验证了解码器协同演化能将奖励优化可靠转化为像素空间的质量提升。

Conclusion: 解码器冻结是离散AR模型后训练的性能瓶颈，解码器协同演化（co-evolution）打破了保真度-对齐度之间的权衡，使奖励优化能够有效转化为像素空间的质量提升。

Abstract: Discrete autoregressive (AR) text-to-image (T2I) models pair a VQ tokenizer with an AR policy, and current post-training pipelines optimize only the policy while keeping the VQ decoder frozen. Recent diffusion T2I work, exemplified by REPA-E, has shown that the VAE itself constitutes a key alignment bottleneck, yet no analogous investigation exists for discrete AR models. We show that policy-only optimization induces Latent Covariate Shift: as the policy evolves, the resulting token distribution diverges from the ground-truth distribution on which the decoder was trained, such that reward scores improve while decoded image quality degrades. To address this mismatch, we propose RankE, the first end-to-end post-training framework for discrete T2I generation. Rather than optimizing the policy against a fixed decoder, RankE co-evolves both components through alternating optimization: each module maximizes a ranking-based alignment objective while being regularized by a stability-preserving anchor suited to its parameter space. This co-evolution breaks the fidelity--alignment trade-off that plagues frozen-decoder approaches: on LlamaGen-XL (775M), standard RL improves CLIP but degrades FID, whereas RankE improves both simultaneously (FID 15.21, CLIP 33.76 on MS-COCO 30K). Consistent gains on Janus-Pro (1B) confirm that decoder co-evolution reliably converts reward optimization into pixel-space quality improvements.

</details>


### [139] [PGC: Peak-Guided Calibration for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21207)
*Xiaoyu Zhou,Jianwei Fei,Peipeng Yu,Jingchang Xie,Chong Cheng,Zhihua Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Peak-Guided Calibration (PGC) 框架，通过峰值聚焦机制增强局部判别线索以校准全局决策，提升生成图像检测性能，并在多个基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于全局表征的检测器易被高保真图像内容（如主体）主导，忽略了生成式 AI 产生的细微判别线索，导致检测可靠性受限。

Method: 提出 PGC 框架：采用峰值敏感聚合（peak-focusing mechanism）突出最具判别性的局部线索，利用这些关键信号校准全局决策，恢复被全局上下文淹没的细微模式。

Result: 在 CommGen15 数据集上平均准确率提升 +12.3%；在 GenImage (+2.1%)、AIGI (+3.5%)、UniversalFakeDetect (+1.7%) 等标准基准上均刷新记录。

Conclusion: PGC 通过局部峰值引导校准全局判断的策略有效提升了生成图像检测的鲁棒性，同时 CommGen15 数据集为真实世界威胁模拟提供了更具挑战性的评估基准。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of generative AI, from GANs to modern diffusion models, has resulted in increasingly subtle discriminative clues. These fine-grained signals are often overshadowed by dominant, high-fidelity image content (e.g., the main subject), limiting the reliability of existing detectors that predominantly rely on global representations. To address this challenge, we propose the Peak-Guided Calibration (PGC) framework. PGC introduces a novel strategy that aggregates salient features via a peak-focusing mechanism. Specifically, by employing a peak-sensitive aggregation that accentuates the most discriminative local clues, PGC leverages these critical signals to calibrate the global decision. This approach recovers subtle patterns that would otherwise be submerged in the global context. Furthermore, to better simulate real-world threats, we introduce the CommGen15 dataset, a challenging benchmark comprising samples from 15 commercial models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PGC achieves state-of-the-art performance. Specifically, it improves mean accuracy by +12.3% on our CommGen15 dataset, and sets new records on standard benchmarks, including GenImage (+2.1%), AIGI (+3.5%), and UniversalFakeDetect (+1.7%). Code is available at https://github.com/xiaoyu6868/PGC.

</details>


### [140] [SR-Ground: Image Quality Grounding for Super-Resolved Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21244)
*Artem Borisov,Evgeney Bogatyrev,Khaled Abud,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了SR-Ground大规模数据集，包含63,000张超分图像和6种伪影类型的像素级标注，并通过1,062名参与者验证。该数据集用于训练具备可解释性的图像质量评估模型，可有效检测和减少超分伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超分方法（尤其是扩散模型）产生的新型视觉伪影缺乏细粒度识别手段，而传统IQA方法仅提供整体质量分数，无法解释和区分不同类型的伪影。

Method: 1) 构建SR-Ground数据集：使用多种SOTA超分模型生成图像，进行像素级伪影标注；2) 开展1,062人参与的大规模众包验证；3) 训练具有定位能力的IQA模型；4) 提出微调流程，利用定位模型减少超分输出中的伪影。

Result: SR-Ground数据集包含63,000张图像，覆盖6种伪影类型。基于该数据集训练的IQA模型在下游任务中性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入细粒度伪影分割数据集，可增强IQA模型的可解释性和实用性，并进一步用于优化超分模型输出，减少伪影。

Abstract: Super-Resolution (SR) has advanced rapidly in recent years, with diffusion-based models achieving unprecedented fidelity at the cost of introducing new types of visual artifacts. While existing Image Quality Assessment (IQA) methods provide holistic quality scores, they lack interpretability and fail to distinguish between different artifact types arising from modern SR approaches.
  To address this gap, we introduce SR-Ground, a large-scale dataset specifically designed for fine-grained artifact segmentation in super-resolved images. The dataset comprises images processed by a diverse set of state-of-the-art SR models, with pixel-level annotations for multiple artifact categories. We conduct a large-scale crowdsourcing study involving 1,062 participants to validate and refine automatically generated segmentations, resulting in a high-quality dataset of 63,000 images spanning 6 distinct artifact types.
  We demonstrate that training IQA models with grounding capabilities on SR-Ground significantly improves performance on downstream tasks. Furthermore, we introduce a fine-tuning pipeline that leverages our grounding model to reduce perceptible artifacts in SR outputs, showcasing the practical utility of our dataset.

</details>


### [141] [STiTch: Semantic Transition and Transportation in Collaboration for Training-Free Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21261)
*Miaoge Li,Dongsheng Wang,Zening Sun,Jinsen Zhang,Wenhan Luo,Jingcai Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无训练零样本组合图像检索框架STTC，通过语义过渡向量精炼LLM生成的描述，并将检索任务重构为集合到集合的对齐任务，使用双向传输距离实现细粒度跨模态匹配，在多个CIR任务上取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的无训练零样本CIR方法存在两个问题：1）生成的描述因图像与文本间的语义鸿沟而引入参考图像中的无关特征；2）检索阶段的点对点对齐无法捕获多样化的组合信息。

Method: 1）利用LLM推断组合描述，通过过渡向量在嵌入空间精炼描述，使其更接近目标图像，过滤无关噪声；2）将描述和图像建模为离散分布，将检索任务重构为集合到集合的对齐；3）开发双向传输距离实现跨模态细粒度对齐并计算检索分数。

Result: 在多个CIR任务上的大量实验表明该方法具有通用性、有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 通过语义过渡和集合级双向传输距离，有效解决了无训练零样本CIR中描述精炼和多样对齐两大挑战。

Abstract: Training-free zero-shot composed image retrieval models are recently gaining increasing research interest due to their generalizability and flexibility in unseen multimodal retrieval. Recent LLM-based advances focus on generating the expected target caption by exploring the compositional ability behind the LLMs. Although efficient, we find that 1) the generated captions tend to introduce unexpected features from the reference image due to the semantic gap between the input image and text modification, where the image contains much more details than the text; 2) the point-to-point alignment during the retrieval stage fails to capture diverse compositions. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel Semantic Transition and Transportation in collaboration framework for training-free zero-shot CIR tasks. Specifically, given the composed caption inferred by an LLM, we aim to refine it through a transition vector in the embedding space and make it closer to the target image. Combining LLMs with user instruction, the refined caption concentrates more on the core modification intent and thus filters out unnecessary noise. Moreover, to explore diverse alignment during the retrieval stage, we model the caption and image as discrete distributions and reformulate the retrieval task as a set-to-set alignment task. Finally, a bidirectional transportation distance is developed to consider fine-grained alignments across modalities and calculate the retrieval score. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method can be general, effective, and beneficial for many CIR tasks.

</details>


### [142] [Vision Transformers and Convolutional Neural Networks for Land Use Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21268)
*Arun D. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对比了Vision Transformer (ViT) 和 CNN（如AlexNet）在遥感土地利用场景分类中的表现。实验表明，CNN在训练数据有限且局部纹理特征明显的场景中表现稳健，而ViT在数据充足时能更好地捕捉全局空间关系，但计算资源需求更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CNN虽能有效提取局部空间特征，但在捕捉全局上下文关系方面存在局限。ViT通过自注意力机制建模长距离依赖，可能弥补这一不足。本文旨在系统对比两种架构在遥感土地利用场景分类中的性能差异，为实际应用中的模型选择提供指导。

Method: 选取代表性CNN模型AlexNet与ViT作为对比对象，在UC Merced Land Use和EuroSAT Land Use两个基准数据集上进行实验。评估指标包括分类准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数以及计算复杂度，从而全面比较两种架构的性能。

Result: CNN在训练样本有限、局部纹理特征强的数据集上表现稳健；ViT在训练数据充足时，能更好地捕获复杂场景中的全局空间关系，但需要更大的计算资源和训练数据量才能达到最优性能。

Conclusion: CNN和ViT各有优劣：前者适合小样本和纹理主导的场景，后者适合数据充足且依赖全局上下文的任务。研究结果为遥感土地利用场景分类的模型选型提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Land Use Scene Classification (LUSC) from remote sensing imagery plays a critical role in environmental monitoring, urban planning, and sustainable resource management. In recent years, deep learning methods have significantly advanced the state of the art, with Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) dominating the field because of their strong ability to capture local spatial features. However, the emergence of Vision Transformers (ViTs) has introduced a new paradigm that models long-range dependencies through self-attention mechanisms, potentially enabling improved global context understanding. This paper presents a comparative assessment of Vision Transformers and CNN-based architecture for remote sensing land use scene classification. Representative CNN models, such as AlexNet, is evaluated alongside the Vision Transformer (ViT) using benchmark remote sensing datasets, including the UC Merced Land Use and EuroSAT Land Use datasets. The study examines classification accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and computational complexity to provide a comprehensive performance comparison. Experimental results demonstrate that CNNs perform robustly on datasets with limited training samples and strong local texture characteristics, whereas Vision Transformers exhibit superior performance in capturing global spatial relationships in complex scenes when sufficient training data are available. However, ViTs typically require greater computational resources and larger training datasets to achieve optimal performance. The findings of this study provide insights into the strengths and limitations of both architectures and offer guidance for selecting appropriate models for remote sensing land use scene classification applications.

</details>


### [143] [DriveMA: Rethinking Language Interfaces in Driving VLAs with One-Step Meta-Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21273)
*Weicheng Zheng,Yixin Huang,Qiao Sun,Derun Li,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DriveMA，用一步元动作（meta-actions）替代传统驾驶VLA模型中的自然语言推理链，结合动作监督训练与回合级强化学习，在Waymo端到端驾驶挑战中取得SOTA（2B模型RFS 8.060，4B模型RFS 8.079）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶VLA模型使用自然语言推理作为中间接口，面临三大瓶颈：高质量推理标注难获取、紧凑模型难以处理长推理链、推理延迟显著增加。作者希望设计更高效的语义接口。

Method: 提出DriveMA框架：(1) 用一步元动作（meta-actions）替代冗长的自然语言推理，元动作语义明确且低熵，可从专家轨迹自动推导；(2) 采用动作中心的有监督训练；(3) 引入回合级信用分配强化学习框架，联合优化元动作正确性、轨迹质量和轨迹-元动作一致性。

Result: 2B模型在Waymo端到端驾驶挑战中RFS达8.060，4B模型达8.079，均为SOTA；在NAVSIM上也取得有竞争力表现。消融实验表明一步元动作在表达能力、可预测性和推理效率之间优于自然语言推理和细粒度动作序列。

Conclusion: 一步元动作是驾驶VLA模型中自然语言推理的有效替代方案，在保持语义决策引导的同时，显著降低标注成本、模型负担和推理延迟，为端到端驾驶提供了更实用的语言接口设计范式。

Abstract: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models (Driving VLAs) commonly introduce natural-language reasoning as an intermediate interface for end-to-end planning, but reasoning-centric interfaces face three practical bottlenecks: obtaining high-quality reasoning annotations is difficult, generating and understanding long reasoning chains is challenging for compact models, and inference latency is substantially increased. In this paper, we rethink the design of language interfaces in Driving VLAs and show that concise one-step meta-actions are a simple yet effective alternative to verbose reasoning. Meta-actions provide semantic decision grounding while remaining low-entropy, and being automatically derivable from expert trajectories, enabling scalable supervision and reliable trajectory conditioning. Building on this interface, we propose DriveMA, which combines action-centric supervised training with a turn-level credit-assignment reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes meta-action correctness, trajectory quality, and trajectory--meta-action consistency. Experiments show that DriveMA already achieves a new state of the art on the Waymo End-to-End Driving Challenge with a 2B model, reaching a Rater Feedback Score (RFS) of 8.060, while its 4B version further improves the state of the art to 8.079; DriveMA also obtains competitive performance on NAVSIM. Ablations demonstrate that one-step meta-actions offer a better practical trade-off between expressiveness, predictability, and inference efficiency than natural-language reasoning or finer-grained action sequences. Code, data, and models will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [144] [Let EEG Models Learn EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21280)
*Yifan Wang,Yijia Ma,Wen Li,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JET框架，基于条件流匹配直接对EEG信号连续轨迹建模，避免离散去噪方法，引入谱结构、时间平稳性等约束，在三大基准上实现SOTA，TS-FID降低40%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG生成方法依赖离散去噪目标，无法准确反映神经活动的连续时间动态和频谱结构，导致长程时间依赖保留不足、生成信号的频谱与时间结构不匹配。

Method: 提出Just EEG Transformer (JET)，基于条件流匹配(conditional flow matching)将EEG建模为沿连续轨迹演化的原始序列，通过学习平滑向量场将噪声传输至EEG数据分布，并引入保持频谱结构、时间平稳性和信号级统计量的约束。

Result: 在三个大规模基准数据集上，JET一致达到SOTA性能，TS-FID相比强基线降低超过40%。

Conclusion: JET能够有效捕获神经动力学的关键结构特性，为EEG生成提供了一种可扩展且原则性的方法。

Abstract: High-fidelity EEG generation is critical for alleviating data scarcity and addressing privacy constraints in large-scale neural modeling. Despite recent progress, most existing approaches formulate EEG generation via discrete denoising objectives, which inadequately reflect the inherently continuous temporal dynamics and spectral structure of neural activity. As a result, these methods often struggle to preserve long-range temporal dependencies and exhibit mismatches in the spectral and temporal structure of the generated signals. In this work, we argue that effective EEG generation requires models that operate directly on the continuous evolution of neural signals. We introduce Just EEG Transformer (JET), a generative framework based on conditional flow matching that models EEG as raw sequences evolving along continuous trajectories.
