By learning a smooth vector field that transports noise to the EEG data distribution, JET captures temporal continuity and transient dynamics without relying on discretized denoising schemes or domain-specific representations. To ensure that the learned dynamics remain consistent with key properties of EEG signals, we introduce principled constraints that preserve spectral structure, temporal stationarity, and signal-level statistics. Across three large-scale benchmarks, JET consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, reducing TS-FID by over 40% compared to strong baselines. Extensive analyses show that JET captures key structural properties of neural dynamics, providing a scalable and principled approach to EEG generation. Project page: https://y-research-sbu.github.io/JET/ .

</details>


### [145] [Reducing Object Hallucination in LVLMs via Emphasizing Image-negative Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21300)
*Meng Shen,Minghao Wu,Deepu Rajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现LVLM幻觉源于模型倾向文本生成而忽视图像信息，通过按视觉依赖性将token分为三类并提出加权训练与数据过滤策略，有效减少幻觉且不增加推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LVLM中对象幻觉的可能根源——模型优先处理文本生成而非有效利用图像信息，并据此提出缓解方法。

Method: 1) 根据视觉依赖性将token分为图像正相关、不变和负相关三类；2) 依据视觉依赖程度调整训练权重；3) 剔除可能含有更多幻觉的训练数据。

Result: 在三种LVLM变体上验证，两种方法均有效减少幻觉，不降低响应长度，不增加推理计算成本。

Conclusion: 模型训练过于侧重指令跟随而非图像信息提取是幻觉的重要成因，通过视觉依赖调权与数据过滤可有效缓解。

Abstract: Object hallucination is a significant challenge that hinders the application of large vision-language models (LVLMs) in practice. We hypothesize that one possible origin of hallucination is the model's tendency to prioritize text generation over meaningful interaction with images. To explore this, we examine the generation process and categorize text tokens into three groups: image-positive, invariant, and negative, based on their visual dependence on input image tokens. Our analysis reveals that most generated tokens are minimally influenced by the image information. This suggests that during the model's training stage, more emphasis is placed on learning how to follow textual instructions, rather than extracting information from images. Based on this finding, we propose adjusting the training weights of different tokens depending on their visual dependence to control hallucination. Additionally, we remove a portion of the training data that potentially contains more hallucinations as a data filtering strategy. Both methods achieve a reduction in hallucination without compromising response length or introducing additional computational costs during inference. We validate our methods across three LVLM variants, demonstrating the effectiveness and general applicability.

</details>


### [146] [OcclusionFormer: Arranging Z-Order for Layout-Grounded Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21343)
*Ziye Li,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OcclusionFormer框架，通过显式建模Z轴顺序（遮挡关系）解决布局到图像生成中的物体重叠问题。核心贡献包括：构建大规模SA-Z遮挡数据集、基于volume rendering的Diffusion Transformer架构，以及查询对齐损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布局到图像模型在处理物体间遮挡（边界框重叠）时，缺乏显式遮挡信息，导致重叠区域纹理混乱或物理层叠不一致。需要一种能够显式建模Z-order优先级的方法。

Method: 1）构建SA-Z数据集（含显式遮挡顺序和像素级标注）；2）提出OcclusionFormer框架，通过解耦实例并利用volume rendering合成，在Diffusion Transformer中显式建模Z-order；3）引入查询对齐损失（queried alignment loss）对单个实例进行显式监督，增强语义一致性。

Result: 该方法有效减少重叠区域的歧义，强制执行正确的遮挡依赖关系，保持结构完整性，在多种场景下显著提升生成精度。

Conclusion: 显式建模Z-order是解决布局到图像生成中遮挡问题的有效途径，OcclusionFormer通过数据集构建和网络架构设计双管齐下，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Recent layout-to-image models have achieved remarkable progress in spatial controllability. However, they still struggle with inter-object occlusion. When bounding boxes overlap, most existing methods lack explicit occlusion information, which makes the generation in intersection regions inherently ambiguous and hinders the determination of complex occlusion relationships. As a result, they often produce entangled textures or physically inconsistent layering in the overlapped areas. To address this issue, we first construct SA-Z, a large-scale dataset enriched with explicit occlusion ordering and pixel-level annotations. Building upon our proposed dataset, we introduce OcclusionFormer, a novel occlusion-aware Diffusion Transformer framework that explicitly models Z-order priority by decoupling instances and compositing them via volume rendering. Furthermore, to ensure fine-grained spatial precision, we introduce a queried alignment loss that explicitly supervises individual instances and enhances semantic consistency. The proposed method effectively reduces ambiguity in overlapping regions, enforces correct occlusion dependencies, and preserves structural integrity, leading to substantial accuracy gains across diverse scenes.

</details>


### [147] [A Non-Reference Diffusion-Based Restoration Framework for Landsat 7 ETM+ SLC-off Imagery in Antarctica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21371)
*Leyue Tang,Jonathan Louis Bamber,Gang Qiao,Yuanhang Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对南极地区Landsat 7 ETM+因2003年扫描线校正器故障导致约22%像素缺失的问题，本文提出了一种无需参考影像的扩散模型框架DiffGF，通过两阶段设计（潜在空间扩散+像素空间细化）修复缺失像素，并构建了专用南极数据集SLCANT，实验证明该方法能高保真恢复影像，在冰裂隙分割等下游任务中具有实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南极地区极夜和云层覆盖导致光学影像获取困难，Landsat虽是最重要的遥感数据源，但2003年SLC故障导致Landsat 7 ETM+影像约22%像素缺失。由于南极地表变化剧烈且快速，难以获取可靠的参考影像，传统基于参考的缺口填充方法不适用，因此需要一种无需参考影像的修复方法。

Method: 提出DiffGF框架，采用两阶段设计：第一阶段在潜在空间进行扩散过程学习数据分布，第二阶段在像素空间进行细化修复。同时构建了专用南极数据集SLCANT用于训练和评估。

Result: 定量和定性结果均表明DiffGF能够高保真地修复南极SLC-off影像，在下游的冰裂隙分割应用中验证了其实用价值。

Conclusion: DiffGF为利用南极地区Landsat 7 SLC-off存档数据提供了有效方法，能够从历史记录中提取有价值信息，支持相关南极研究。

Abstract: Acquiring usable optical imagery in Antarctica is inherently challenging due to prolonged polar nights and frequent cloud cover. Landsat provides the longest and most continuous optical observations and constitutes one of the most important remote sensing data sources for Antarctic studies. However, the scan-line corrector (SLC) failure in 2003 resulted in approximately 22% missing pixels in Landsat 7 ETM+ SLC-off imagery, severely limiting its usability. Unlike many non-polar environments, Antarctic surfaces undergo rapid and substantial changes, which makes it difficult to obtain reliable reference imagery and reduces the applicability of conventional reference-based gap-filling methods. To address this challenge, we propose DiffGF, a non-reference diffusion-based framework for restoring Landsat 7 SLC-off imagery without requiring any external reference data. DiffGF adopts a two-stage design consisting of a latent-space diffusion process and a pixel-space refinement. A dedicated Antarctic dataset, SLCANT, is constructed for training and evaluation. Quantitative and qualitative results demonstrate that DiffGF restores Antarctic SLC-off imagery with high fidelity. Its practical value is further examined through a downstream crevasse segmentation application. The results suggest that DiffGF provides a useful approach for exploiting Landsat 7 SLC-off archives in Antarctica, enabling the extraction of valuable information from historical records and supporting related Antarctic studies.

</details>


### [148] [Disentangling Generation and Regression in Stochastic Interpolants for Controllable Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21381)
*Yi Liu,Jia Ma,Wengen Li,Jihong Guan,Shuigeng Zhou,Yichao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了DiSI框架，通过解耦随机插值过程中的生成与回归组件，实现从纯回归到纯生成的连续可控过渡，在多种图像恢复任务中以少步推理高效取得竞争性结果，并支持推理时灵活控制失真-感知权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式IR方法（扩散模型、流匹配）能合成逼真纹理但推理步骤多、像素保真度低；回归式IR方法单步高效且像素重建保真度高。为了弥合两者差距，作者希望构建一个统一框架，兼具两者的优势。

Method: 提出DiSI，核心是解耦随机插值过程中的生成与回归组件。具体包括：(1) 两种特定采样轨迹及统一采样器实现任意轨迹上的少步高质量推理；(2) 像素空间的双分支U-Net风格Transformer网络，其中专用分支增强条件引导并保持高吞吐量。

Result: 在多种图像恢复任务上高效取得具有竞争力的结果，并且能够在单一模型内，在推理时灵活控制失真-感知权衡。

Conclusion: DiSI成功弥合了回归式IR与生成式IR之间的鸿沟，提供了一种兼具单步效率、高像素保真度和生成真实纹理能力的统一方案，同时支持推理时按需调节失真-感知平衡。

Abstract: Recent advances in Image Restoration (IR) have been largely driven by generative methods such as Diffusion Models and Flow Matching, which excel in synthesizing realistic textures while suffering from slow multi-step inference and compromised pixel fidelity. In contrast, classical regression-based IR methods excel precisely in these aspects, offering single-step efficiency and high pixel-level reconstruction fidelity. To bridge this gap, we propose DiSI, a unified framework that Disentangles the underlying Stochastic Interpolant process into independent generation and regression components. This decoupling endows DiSI with remarkable versatility, enabling a continuous and controllable transition from a pure regression process to a fully generative one. Technically, we instantiate this framework with two specific sampling trajectories, accompanied by a unified sampler for high-quality, few-step inference on arbitrary trajectories. Furthermore, we design a dual-branch U-Net style transformer network in pixel space, using a dedicated branch to enhance conditional guidance while ensuring high throughput. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DiSI efficiently achieves competitive results on various IR tasks, while uniquely offering the inference-time flexibility to control the distortion-perception trade-off within a single model.

</details>


### [149] [RoadTones: Tone Controllable Text Generation from Road Event Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21411)
*Chirag Parikh,Siddhi Pravin Lipare,Ravi Kiran Sarvadevabhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RoadTones-51K数据集、RoadTones-VL-CoT可控视频描述模型和RoadTones-Eval评估套件，实现了对道路视频字幕的语气控制（如语气、紧急程度、风格），并通过人工验证和用户研究证明了其在事实一致性与语气控制方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频语言模型虽能生成事实性道路事件描述，但无法控制表达的语气、紧急程度或风格，限制了其在需要兼顾内容与表达方式的通信场景中的应用。

Method: 1) 构建人类验证的数据生成管线，扩展道路视频语料库并标注多样化语气，形成RoadTones-51K数据集；2) 提出RoadTones-VL-CoT模型，实现可控制的视频到文本生成，并生成基于语气的思维链中间草稿以增强可解释性；3) 提出RoadTones-Eval评估套件，联合衡量事实一致性与语气遵从度。

Result: 用户研究验证了生成字幕的质量、语气控制能力和事实一致性。RoadTones-Eval套件能够有效评估模型在事实准确性和语气符合度上的表现。

Conclusion: 该工作为上下文敏感的可控语气视频描述奠定了基础，弥补了现有模型在表达方式控制上的空白。

Abstract: Existing video-language models can generate factual descriptions of road events but lack control over how these events are expressed: their tone, urgency, or style. This limits deployment in communication-critical settings where the effectiveness of a message depends on both content and presentation, not just factual accuracy. To mitigate this, we introduce a comprehensive dataset-model-evaluation suite for tone-controllable road video captioning. Our human-validated data generation pipeline expands road-video corpora with diverse tonal annotations and multi-tone captions, yielding the RoadTones-51K dataset. We propose RoadTones-VL-CoT, a controllable video-to-text model that also generates tone-conditioned Chain-of-Thought intermediate drafts for interpretability. We also introduce RoadTones-Eval, a new evaluation suite that jointly measures factual consistency and tone adherence. In addition, we conducted a user study whose results validate caption quality, tone control, and factual consistency. Together, these contributions lay the foundation for context-sensitive tone-controllable video captioning.

</details>


### [150] [AIGaitor: Privacy-preserving and cloud-free motion analysis for everyone, using edge computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21421)
*Lauhitya Reddy,Trisha M. Kesar,Hyeokhyen Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AIGaitor是一个在消费级智能手机上运行的隐私保护、无需云端的动作分析系统，实现了从单目视频到运动捕捉和下游深度学习分析的全流程端上处理。通过对74名康复临床医生的调查，系统针对成本、隐私和培训等主要障碍进行了优化，在iPhone 14上处理10秒4K 60fps视频仅需77秒，性能可匹敌甚至超越包含网络传输时间的云端方案（H200服务器）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床运动分析的金标准——运动捕捉系统成本高、技术复杂且存在隐私问题。研究者对74名康复临床医生调查发现，92%愿意采用准确、经济、易用的AI步态分析工具，但79.7%认为运营成本、68.9%认为培训不足、64.9%认为隐私问题是主要障碍。因此需要一种低成本、易用、保护隐私的运动分析方案。

Method: 在消费级智能手机上优化并实现单目运动捕捉管线的各个组件，包括2D/3D姿态估计、姿态优化、基于骨架的深度学习分析和视觉语言模型。采用Time-Priority端到端管线，利用手机上的神经加速器进行推理，完全无需云端参与。对比实验包括本地处理与云端（NVIDIA H200）处理的总时间（含网络传输）。

Result: 在iPhone 14上处理10秒4K 60fps视频片段需77秒；相同管线在H200云端服务器上，若考虑全球平均移动网络上传速度（94秒）或发达地区Wi-Fi速度（66秒），端上方案时间介于两者之间，整体具有竞争力。轻量模型ViTPose-s可实现实时关键点提取，基于骨架的动作识别模型可在亚毫秒级完成步态分类。

Conclusion: AIGaitor是首个实现端到端端上运动捕捉和下游深度学习分析的单目系统，证明了在消费级智能手机上进行临床可行的运动分析是可能的，且具备低成本、隐私保护和易用性优势，有望推动运动分析在临床中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Motion capture is the gold standard for measuring human movement, but clinical use remains limited by cost, technical complexity, and privacy concerns. AIGaitor is a privacy-preserving, cloud-free motion analysis system that runs markerless monocular motion-capture pipelines and downstream deep-learning analysis entirely on a consumer smartphone using on-device neural accelerators. To motivate its design, we surveyed 74 rehabilitation clinicians: 92 percent said they would adopt an accurate, cost-effective, easy-to-use AI gait analysis tool, while 79.7 percent cited operating cost, 68.9 percent insufficient training, and 64.9 percent privacy concerns as leading barriers. We then optimized and benchmarked mobile iOS implementations of current monocular pipeline components, including 2D and 3D pose estimation, pose optimization, skeleton-based deep-learning analysis, and a vision-language model. A Time-Priority end-to-end on-device pipeline processes a 10 s 4K 60 fps video clip in 77 s on an iPhone 14, matching or beating the same pipeline on a high-end NVIDIA H200 cloud server when network transfer is included: 94 s at global mobile-average uplink and 66 s at developed-world Wi-Fi. Lightweight models such as ViTPose-s achieve real-time keypoint extraction, and skeleton-based action-recognition models provide sub-millisecond gait classification on the same clip. To our knowledge, AIGaitor is the first monocular system to demonstrate end-to-end on-device motion capture and downstream deep-learning analysis, supporting clinically applicable movement analysis that is low-cost, private, and accessible to smartphone users.

</details>


### [151] [iTryOn: Mastering Interactive Video Virtual Try-On with Spatial-Semantic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21431)
*Jun Zheng,Zhengze Xu,Mengting Chen,Jing Wang,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Kaifu Zhang,Bo Zheng,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Interactive Video Virtual Try-On (Interactive VVT)新任务，并设计iTryOn框架，通过多层级交互注入机制（空间级3D手部先验+语义级动作描述与A-RoPE）解决视频中主动人衣交互的语义模糊与复杂形变问题，在传统VVT和新任务上均达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VVT方法局限于非交互场景（模特仅展示衣服），忽略了真实世界中人与服装的主动交互（如拉扯、整理等），缺乏对此类动态场景的形式化定义与解决方案。

Method: 基于大规模视频扩散Transformer的iTryOn框架，包含：(1) 空间级：引入与衣物无关的3D手部先验，精确指导手-衣接触；(2) 语义级：全局描述+时间戳动作描述，通过Action-aware Rotational Position Embedding (A-RoPE)同步对齐。

Result: 在传统VVT基准上达到SOTA，在Interactive VVT新设定下大幅领先现有方法。

Conclusion: iTryOn成功推动了VVT从静态展示向动态可控交互的转变，是更真实、更可控的虚拟试穿体验的重要里程碑。

Abstract: Video Virtual Try-On (VVT) aims to seamlessly replace a garment on a person in a video with a new one. While existing methods have made significant strides in maintaining temporal consistency, they are predominantly confined to non-interactive scenarios where models merely showcase garments. This limitation overlooks a crucial aspect of real-world apparel presentation: active human-garment interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce and formalize a new challenging task: Interactive Video Virtual Try-On (Interactive VVT), where subjects in the video actively engage with their clothing. This task introduces unique challenges beyond simple texture preservation, including: (1) resolving the semantic ambiguity of interactions from standard pose information, and (2) learning complex garment deformations from video where interactive moments are sparse and brief. To address these challenges, we propose iTryOn, a novel framework built upon a large-scale video diffusion Transformer. iTryOn pioneers a multi-level interaction injection mechanism to guide the generation of complex dynamics. At the spatial level, we introduce a garment-agnostic 3D hand prior to provide fine-grained guidance for precise hand-garment contact, effectively resolving spatial ambiguity. At the semantic level, iTryOn leverages global captions for overall context and time-stamped action captions for localized interactions, synchronized via our novel Action-aware Rotational Position Embedding (A-RoPE). Extensive experiments demonstrate that iTryOn not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on traditional VVT benchmarks but also establishes a commanding lead in the new interactive setting, marking a significant step towards more dynamic and controllable virtual try-on experiences.

</details>


### [152] [ReMATF: Recurrent Motion-Adaptive Multi-scale Turbulence Mitigation for Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21440)
*Zhiming Liu,Zhicheng Zou,Nantheera Anantrasirichai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级循环框架ReMATF，仅用两帧输入即可高效去除大气湍流导致的视频失真，在保证时空一致性的同时大幅降低计算开销，适合资源受限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer/3D架构的多帧方法计算量和内存消耗大，难以实现实时部署，尤其在资源受限场景下。需要一种轻量级、高效且保持时空一致性的视频湍流复原方法。

Method: 提出ReMATF循环框架：(1)多尺度编码器-解码器；(2)时间扭曲（temporal warping）；(3)运动自适应时间融合模块（motion-adaptive temporal fusion），对前一输出与当前预测进行逐像素融合，无需扩大时间窗口即可增强时序连贯性。

Result: 在合成和真实湍流数据集上，PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS均有提升，推理速度显著快于多帧Transformer基线方法。

Conclusion: ReMATF是一种轻量高效、适合资源受限场景的实时视频湍流复原方案，能够在仅两帧输入下保持空间细节和时序稳定性。

Abstract: Atmospheric turbulence severely degrades video quality by introducing distortions such as geometric warping, blur, and temporal flickering, posing significant challenges to both visual clarity and temporal consistency. Current state-of-the-art methods are based on transformer, 3D architectures and require multi-frame input, but their large computational cost and memory usage limit real-time deployment, especially in resource-constrained scenarios. In this work, we propose ReMATF, a lightweight recurrent framework that restores videos using only two frames at a time while preserving spatial detail and temporal stability. ReMATF combines a multi-scale encoder-decoder with temporal warping and a motion-adaptive temporal fusion module that performs per-pixel fusion between the warped previous output and the current prediction to enhance coherence without enlarging the temporal window. This design reduces flicker, sharpens details, and remains efficient. Experiments on synthetic and real turbulence datasets show consistent improvements in PSNR/SSIM and perceptual quality (LPIPS), along with substantially faster inference than multi-frame transformer baselines, making ReMATF suitable turbulence mitigation in resource-constrained scenarios.

</details>


### [153] [ProtoPathway: Biologically Structured Prototype-Pathway Fusion for Multimodal Cancer Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21454)
*Amaya Gallagher-Syed,Costantino Pitzalis,Myles J. Lewis,Michael R. Barnes,Gregory Slabaugh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoPathway是一个可解释的多模态癌症生存预测框架，通过形态学原型和通路的双向交叉注意力机制，统一了病理图像与转录组数据，在多个TCGA数据集上取得竞争性性能并大幅提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有癌症生存预测的多模态方法缺乏生物学层面的可解释性，难以在推理时提供从基因到组织形态的完整归因路径，且计算成本高。

Method: 1) 病理端：K个可学习的形态学原型通过软分配将可变长度补丁压缩为固定长度的任务自适应token；2) 基因组端：基于Reactome通路层次的双向图神经网络编码基因表达，生成通路嵌入；3) 跨模态注意力在原型×通路矩阵上操作，原型查询通路，模拟分子程序驱动组织形态的生物学方向。

Result: 在五个TCGA癌症队列上取得竞争性或更优的生存预测性能，显著提升生物学可解释性并降低计算成本，通过分层秩次群体分析验证了可解释性。

Conclusion: ProtoPathway通过设计即解释的方式，实现了从基因到通路、原型再到空间组织图谱的完整生物学层次归因，为可解释多模态生存预测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We introduce ProtoPathway, an interpretable-by-design multimodal framework for cancer survival prediction that unifies whole slide imaging and transcriptomics through encoders producing biologically grounded representations on both sides of the fusion. On the histopathology side, $K$ learnable morphological prototypes, trained end-to-end with the survival objective, serve as the slide representation itself: patches flow into prototype tokens via soft assignment, compressing variable-length patch sets into fixed task-adaptive tokens. On the genomic side, a bipartite graph neural network encodes gene expression within the Reactome pathway hierarchy, producing pathway embeddings that reflect both constituent genes and their broader biological context through bidirectional message passing over a shared gene--pathway graph. Cross-modal attention then operates over a compact prototype $\times$ pathway matrix in which prototypes query pathways, modeling the biological direction in which molecular programs give rise to tissue morphology. Because both axes carry stable task-learned identity, the attention matrix is itself an interpretability output, yielding native inference-time attribution across the full biological hierarchy, from genes through pathways and prototypes to spatial tissue maps. We evaluate on five TCGA cancer cohorts, demonstrating competitive or superior survival prediction with substantially improved biological interpretability and reduced computational cost, with interpretability claims validated through fold-stratified rank-based population-level analysis. Our source code, model weights, and Reactome pathways, together with a unified codebase reimplementing all multimodal survival baselines under identical preprocessing and evaluation, are available at: https://github.com/AmayaGS/ProtoPathway.

</details>


### [154] [StreamGVE: Training-Free Video Editing via Few-Step Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21466)
*Guanlong Jiao,Chenyangguang Zhang,Jia Jun Cheng Xian,Zewei Zhang,Renjie Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamGVE，一种基于流式生成模型的视频编辑方法，通过噪声到数据范式、双分支快速采样、自注意力桥接和交叉注意力增强等技术，在少步采样下实现高质量视频编辑，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法采用数据到数据范式，与生成模型不兼容，导致迭代成本高且编辑质量不佳。作者希望将视频编辑从数据到数据范式转向噪声到数据范式，以更好地适配现代生成模型。

Method: 基于预训练的流式生成模型，提出双分支快速采样架构：1) 自注意力桥接连接源视频与目标分支；2) 交叉注意力定位/增强注入条件；3) 源导向引导提升生成质量；4) 视觉提示策略增强编辑灵活性与实用性。

Result: 在多种视频编辑任务上，StreamGVE在少步采样设置下一致优于现有方法，且时间成本显著降低。

Conclusion: StreamGVE通过噪声到数据视角重新审视视频编辑，有效解决了数据到数据范式的局限性，具备高效、鲁棒、可泛化的特点。

Abstract: Although existing video editing methods are generally feasible, they often require many costly iterations and still struggle to deliver high-quality yet satisfying editing results. We attribute this limitation to the prevalent data-to-data paradigm, which is less compatible with modern generative models than noise-to-data generation. To address this gap, we revisit video editing from a noise-to-data perspective and propose Streaming-Generation-based Video Editing (StreamGVE), which preserves few-step sampling while seamlessly injecting source-video conditions. Built on pre-trained streaming generation models, StreamGVE introduces dual-branch fast sampling with a self-attention bridge and cross-attention grounding/boosting to satisfy both sampling and conditioning requirements. We further propose source-oriented guidance to improve target-generation quality, and a visual prompting strategy to enhance editing flexibility and practicality. The method is effective, robust, and generalizable across different models. Extensive experiments on diverse video editing tasks show that StreamGVE consistently outperforms existing approaches, even in few-step settings with minimal time cost.

</details>


### [155] [Stream3D: Sequential Multi-View 3D Generation via Evidential Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21472)
*Kaichen Zhou,Zeyang Bai,Xinhai Chang,Mengyu Wang,Paul Liang,Fangneng Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream3D提出了一种无需训练的流式机制，能将冻结的视图条件3D生成器扩展为支持连续单目视频流的流式生成器，通过紧凑的证据记忆（evidential memory）选择性地缓存最信息量的历史帧，在保持时序一致性的同时避免内存线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视图条件3D生成器（如SAM 3D、TRELLIS、Hunyuan3D）能高质量地从单视图重建3D对象，但真实世界中的视觉观测通常是连续的单目视频流。直接将生成器独立应用于每一帧会导致生成结果严重缺乏时序一致性，因此需要一种流式机制来保证时序稳定的3D生成。

Method: Stream3D是一种无需训练（training-free）的流式机制，核心在于维护一个紧凑的**证据记忆（evidential memory）**。该记忆基于提出的**证据分数（evidence score）**机制，选择性地缓存信息量最高的历史帧。随着视频流推进，记忆动态更新，始终保留固定数量的信息帧，从而避免内存随序列长度线性增长，同时保持底层生成器完全冻结，无需重训练、架构修改或辅助损失。

Result: 在真实和合成流式基准测试上，Stream3D在光度指标和几何指标上均优于潜在传输基线方法（包括KV-cache复用和基于流的特征编辑）。

Conclusion: Stream3D证明了无需任何训练或模型修改，仅通过精心设计的记忆管理机制即可将现有3D生成器扩展为流式版本，有效解决单目视频流中的时序一致性问题，且内存开销恒定。

Abstract: View-conditioned 3D generators such as SAM 3D, TRELLIS and Hunyuan3D produce high-quality object reconstructions from a single view, but real-world visual observation often arrives as long monocular streams. Naively applying these generators to each streaming frame independently leads to severe temporal inconsistency in the generated results. To address this problem, we propose Stream3D, the first training-free streaming mechanism that turns a frozen view-conditioned 3D generator into a streaming generator with constant cross-chunk memory. Stream3D achieves this by maintaining a compact evidential memory, which selectively caches the most informative historical frames based on a proposed evidence score mechanism. As the stream progresses, the memory dynamically updates to retain a fixed number of informative frames, preventing the memory footprint from growing linearly with sequence length. This also prevents degradation over long sequences and keeps the underlying generator completely unchanged without retraining, architectural modifications, or auxiliary losses. Evaluated on both realistic and synthetic streaming benchmarks, Stream3D outperforms latent-transport baselines, including KV-cache reuse and flow-based feature editing, across both photometric and geometric metrics. More details can be found at: https://anonymous-submission-20.github.io/streaming3D.github.io/.

</details>


### [156] [Latent Dynamics for Full Body Avatar Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21478)
*Shichong Peng,Chengxiang Yin,Fei Jiang,Zhongshi Jiang,Lingchen Yang,Qingyang Tan,Amin Jourabloo,Jason Saragih,Ke Li,Christian Häne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D高斯表示的全身动画方法，通过引入动力学残差潜变量和Transformer解码器，利用短历史姿态和上一帧潜状态预测动态变形，从而生成时间连贯、历史依赖的高质量动画，且在松散衣物等复杂动态场景下优于现有数据驱动基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于姿态驱动的神经渲染全身模型无法捕捉松散衣物等动态元素的惯性、接触和历史依赖变形；显式模拟或分层服装方法需要专用模板或高成本物理仿真；数据驱动方法虽避免了显式服装层，但仅在推理时固定或回归潜变量，未显式建模其动力学演化，同时现有架构在捕捉精细细节上表现不佳，导致模糊和时序伪影。

Method: 在姿态条件3D高斯化身基础上，增加Transformer解码器和动力学残差潜变量。推理时，使用学习的潜动力学模型从短姿态历史和上一潜状态演化残差潜变量，将每次更新分解为驱动力、恢复力和耗散力三种力分量，实现时间相干、历史依赖的 rollout，且计算成本极低。

Result: 在9个日常运动序列（含多种松散衣物）上，定量指标和感知用户研究均表明，所提方法在动画质量上优于近期数据驱动基线。

Conclusion: 该方法无需显式服装模板或测试时物理模拟，即可生成历史依赖、时间连贯的动态变形，且支持不同初始条件产生多样合理轨迹，通过力分解提供刚度等可解释控制。

Abstract: Pose-driven full-body avatars built on neural rendering produce high-quality novel views of a captured subject. Yet loose clothing and other dynamic elements deform in ways pose alone cannot explain: the same pose can correspond to many different states, because their motion depends on history, inertia, and contact. Explicit simulation and layered-garment methods can model such dynamics, but they require either a dedicated garment template, which raw multi-view capture does not naturally provide, or a test-time physics simulator with non-trivial runtime cost. A parallel line of work learns data-driven clothing avatars that avoid explicit garment layers. These methods add an auxiliary latent for variation beyond pose; at inference, they fix it, regress it from pose, or retrieve it from training data, without explicitly modeling how the latent evolves with its own dynamics. Additionally, even in everyday motion with loose clothing, existing architectures often struggle to capture fine-grained detail, producing blurry renderings and temporal artifacts. We augment a pose-conditioned 3D Gaussian avatar with a transformer-based decoder and a dynamics residual latent that captures temporal appearance and geometry variation beyond the driving signals. At inference, a learned latent dynamics model evolves the residual latent from a short pose history and the previous latent state. The model decomposes each update into driving, restoring, and dissipative forces, producing temporally coherent, history-dependent rollouts with negligible added cost. Different initial conditions yield diverse yet plausible motion trajectories, and the force decomposition exposes controls such as stiffness. Across nine captured sequences of everyday motion with diverse loose garments, quantitative metrics and a perceptual user study show improved animation quality over recent data-driven baselines.

</details>


### [157] [One-Step Distillation of Discrete Diffusion Image Generators via Fixed-Point Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21484)
*Chaoyang Wang,Yunhai Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出固定点蒸馏（FPD），一种端到端框架，通过局部校正目标和多带宽漂移损失，实现离散扩散模型的单步蒸馏，在保持视觉质量的同时显著加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型虽然视觉合成效果优异，但依赖缓慢的迭代解码过程。现有单步蒸馏方法要么需要训练辅助评分网络导致计算量翻倍，要么引入特殊参数化和多阶段流水线使优化碎片化，迫切需要一种端到端的高效蒸馏方法。

Method: FPD通过部分破坏学生模型单步生成的草稿，再用单步教师模型精炼来构建局部校正目标；将离散token提升到连续特征空间，应用多带宽漂移损失迭代累积校正；使用直通估计器反向传播，同时结合可选的对抗目标增强感知真实性。

Result: 在类别条件和文本条件生成任务上，FPD在单步推理中实现了与多步教师模型相近的视觉保真度和结构对齐，并优于现有的离散蒸馏基线方法。

Conclusion: FPD有效地缩小了单步模型与多步教师模型之间的差距，为离散扩散模型的高效推理提供了一种简洁且有效的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models excel at visual synthesis but rely on slow, iterative decoding. Existing single-step distillation methods attempt to bypass this bottleneck, either by training auxiliary score networks that effectively double compute, or by introducing specialized parameterizations and multi-stage pipelines that fragment optimization. In this paper, we introduce Fixed-Point Distillation (FPD), an end-to-end framework that constructs local correction targets by partially corrupting the student's one-step draft and refining it with a single teacher step. To compute the training objective in a semantically meaningful space, we lift discrete tokens into continuous features and apply a multi-bandwidth drift loss that iteratively accumulates these corrections. To backpropagate through the discrete bottleneck, we employ a straight-through estimator that feeds exact hard-sampled tokens to the teacher and decoder during the forward pass, ensuring that training and inference operate on the same codebook manifold, while routing continuous gradients back to the student logits. This fully differentiable pathway additionally accommodates an optional unconditional adversarial objective to enhance perceptual realism. Evaluations on both class- and text-conditional generation validate the effectiveness of our framework. FPD achieves competitive visual fidelity and structural alignment within a single inference step, narrowing the gap to multi-step teachers while outperforming existing discrete distillation baselines.

</details>


### [158] [Uni-Edit: Intelligent Editing Is A General Task For Unified Model Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21487)
*Dian Zheng,Manyuan Zhang,Hongyu Li,Hongbo Liu,Kai Zou,Kaituo Feng,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Uni-Edit，一种智能图像编辑任务，通过单一任务、单一训练阶段和单一数据集，同时提升统一多模态模型的图像理解、生成和编辑能力，打破了传统多任务训练的性能权衡困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMMs）通过混合多任务训练来增强图像理解、生成和编辑能力，但存在任务冲突，需要复杂的多阶段流水线和数据平衡技巧，最终只能实现性能折衷而非真正的相互增强。

Method: 1. 将图像编辑识别为天然需要视觉理解和生成能力的通用任务；2. 提出自动化可扩展的数据合成流水线，将多样化VQA数据转换为包含嵌入问题和嵌套逻辑的复杂编辑指令；3. 构建Uni-Edit-148k数据集。

Result: 在BAGEL和Janus-Pro上的大量实验表明，仅使用Uni-Edit进行微调，无需任何辅助操作，即可在图像理解、生成和编辑三种能力上实现全面增强。

Conclusion: Uni-Edit打破了传统多任务训练的范式，验证了智能图像编辑任务可以作为统一多模态模型微调的首选通用任务，实现真正的性能相互增强。

Abstract: Currently, enhancing Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) with image understanding, generation, and editing capabilities mainly relies on mixed multi-task training. Due to inherent task conflicts, such strategy requires complex multi-stage pipelines, massive data mixing, and balancing tricks, merely resulting in a performance trade-off rather than true mutual reinforcement. To break this paradigm, we propose Uni-Edit, an intelligent image editing task that serves as the first general task for UMM tuning. Unlike complex mixed pipelines, Uni-Edit improves performance across all three abilities at once using only one task, one training stage, and one dataset. Specifically, we first identify image editing as an inherently ideal general task, as it naturally demands both visual understanding and generation. However, existing editing data relies on simplistic instructions that severely underutilize a model's understanding capacity. To address this, we introduce the first automated and scalable data synthesis pipeline for intelligent editing, transforming diverse VQA data into complex and effective editing instructions with embedded questions and nested logic. This yields Uni-Edit-148k, pairing diverse reasoning-intensive instructions with high-quality edited images. Extensive experiments on BAGEL and Janus-Pro demonstrate that tuning solely on Uni-Edit achieves comprehensive enhancements across all three capabilities without any auxiliary operations.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Adaptive Human-Robot Collaboration for Masonry Construction Under Material and Assembly Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20264)
*Jutang Gao,Arash Adel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应人机协作砌筑工作流，通过末端投影仪提供实时定位引导和激光扫描反馈校正，解决施工中通信有限和公差累积问题，实验证明能提高砂浆涂抹一致性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑中人机协作面临机器人-人类通信受限以及材料和装配不确定性导致的公差累积两大挑战，需开发自适应方法应对。

Method: 1) 末端执行器安装投影仪，提供空间配准的实时投影引导人工涂抹粘合剂；2) 激光扫描实现反馈驱动的抓取和放置位姿校正。

Result: 全尺寸实验表明：投影引导提高了粘合剂涂抹一致性并缩短时间；激光校正保持水平层高，避免了开环执行的碰撞故障。

Conclusion: 集成空间投影与反馈驱动自适应（基于材料和实体传感）可缓解公差累积，提升人机协作施工的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration in construction is often challenged by limited robot-to-human communication and the need to adapt to tolerance accumulation arising from material and assembly uncertainties. We present an adaptive human-robot collaborative workflow for masonry construction that addresses communication limitations and tolerance accumulation, demonstrated through a brickwork case study in which a robot places bricks while a human applies adhesive. This workflow is enabled by two complementary mechanisms: 1) an end-effector-mounted projector that provides spatially registered, just-in-time projection guidance for manual adhesive application, and 2) laser scanning for feedback-driven grasping and placement pose correction. Together, these mechanisms enable adjustment of human and robotic actions in response to material variability and accumulated assembly tolerances. Full-scale experiments across conventional running-bond and nonstandard configurations demonstrate that projection guidance improves adhesive application consistency and reduces application time, while laser-based correction maintains level courses and avoids collision-prone failures associated with open-loop execution. These results indicate that integrating spatial projection with feedback-driven adaptation, enabled by material and as-built sensing, can mitigate tolerance accumulation and improve precision and robustness in human-robot collaborative construction.

</details>


### [160] [Terrestrial Soft Mobile Robots: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20304)
*Dimuthu D. K. Arachchige*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了无轮式软体移动机器人的研究现状，涵盖运动策略、驱动方法、建模与控制，并指出了未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体移动机器人在搜救、服务、侦查、探索和制造等领域具有重要应用前景，但缺乏系统性的综述来总结其发展现状与关键挑战。

Method: 对软体移动机器人（特别是无轮式陆地 locomotion 系统）的运动策略、驱动方式、建模方法和控制系统进行了全面文献回顾与分类总结。

Result: 系统梳理了软体移动机器人在运动、驱动、建模和控制方面的现有成果，并识别出阻碍其广泛应用的关键研究难题。

Conclusion: 本文为软体机器人领域的研究者和从业者提供了有价值的参考，指出了未来需要攻克的核心挑战以实现软体移动机器人的大规模应用。

Abstract: Soft mobile robots have emerged as a promising area of research with potential applications in various disciplines including but not limited to search-and-rescue, service, surveillance, explorations, and manufacturing. In this article, we provide a comprehensive review of the current state of soft mobile robot research, focusing on wheelless terrestrial locomotive systems. We include past and present developments in locomotion strategies, actuation methods, modeling approaches, and control systems. Further, we identify key research challenges that must be overcome to enable the widespread adoption of soft mobile robots in various applications. Overall, this article provides a valuable resource for researchers and practitioners interested in the field of soft mobile robots and soft robotics.

</details>


### [161] [SUGAR: A Scalable Human-Video-Driven Generalizable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20373)
*Tianshu Wu,Xiangqi Kong,Yue Chen,Qize Yu,Hang Ye,Jia Li,Yizhou Wang,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SUGAR框架，通过三阶段流程（提取运动先验、物理精炼、分层策略蒸馏）将人类视频转化为可部署的人形机器人全身移动操作技能，无需任务特定奖励工程或参考运动条件，在仿真和真实人形机器人上验证了六项任务，性能优于基线且支持零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖繁琐的任务特定奖励设计、僵化的参考运动复现或昂贵的遥操作，缺乏可扩展性。人类视频虽蕴含丰富行为信息，但直接用于策略学习存在遮挡、接触伪影和重定向误差等缺陷。因此需要一种可扩展的数据驱动框架，从人类视频中提取可部署的人形机器人移动操作技能。

Method: 三阶段框架：1) 自动化管道从非结构化人类视频中提取运动学交互先验（人-物运动轨迹和接触标签）；2) 特权物理精炼器使用统一模仿奖励和渐进状态池将不完美先验转化为物理可行的技能；3) 精炼技能蒸馏为分层自主策略（命令生成器+命令跟踪器）。

Result: 在六项代表性移动操作任务中，SUGAR显著优于参考跟踪基线；性能随人类视频数据量增加而明显提升；实现零样本真实世界迁移，具备可靠的闭环执行、自主故障恢复和稳定的长周期性能。

Conclusion: SUGAR提供了一种可扩展的方案，将海量人类视频转化为实用的人形机器人技能，无需手工奖励设计或遥操作数据，为通用全身移动操作提供了新范式。

Abstract: Building humanoid robots capable of generalizable whole-body loco-manipulation in the real world remains a fundamental challenge. Existing methods either rely on laborious task-specific reward engineering, rigidly replay reference motions that fail to generalize, or depend on costly teleoperation that limits scalability. While human videos capture diverse human behaviors, motion priors inferred from them are inherently imperfect, suffering from occlusion, contact artifacts, and retargeting errors that render them unsuitable for direct policy learning. To address this, we present SUGAR, a scalable data-driven framework that converts diverse human videos into deployable humanoid loco-manipulation skills, without any task-specific reward engineering or reference-motion conditioning at inference. SUGAR proceeds in three stages. First, a fully automated pipeline extracts kinematic interaction priors including human-object motion trajectories and contact labels from unstructured human videos. Second, a privileged physics-based refiner uses a unified mimic reward and progressive state pool to transform imperfect priors into physically feasible, high-fidelity skills. Third, refined skills are distilled into a hierarchical autonomous policy consisting of a command generator and a command tracker. We evaluate SUGAR on six representative loco-manipulation tasks in simulation and real-world humanoid hardware. Our method substantially outperforms reference-tracking baselines, and performance scales clearly with the amount of human video data. It also achieves zero-shot real-world transfer with reliable closed-loop execution, autonomous failure recovery, and stable long-horizon performance under external perturbations. Project Page: https://tianshuwu.github.io/sugar-humanoid/

</details>


### [162] [VBT-MPC: Vision-Based Tactile MPC for Contour Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20392)
*Edison Velasco-Sanchez,Luis F. Recalde,Guanrui Li,Pablo Gil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉触觉模型预测控制（VBT-MPC）框架，用于机器人轮廓跟踪任务。该控制器直接在轮廓特征空间操作，无需单独的位姿估计模块或复杂的力控制架构，并在仿真和真实实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉感知在机器人操作中至关重要，特别是在表面检查等任务中。成功执行需要在保持接触的同时准确跟踪物体轮廓，现有方法通常需要复杂的位姿估计或力控制架构。

Method: 提出VBT-MPC框架，使用眼在手配置的视觉触觉传感器（VBTS），控制器直接在轮廓特征空间操作，避免了单独的位姿估计模块或复杂力控制架构。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，对具有不同几何形状和材料的物体进行了轮廓跟踪评估，并与适应触觉特征的视觉伺服策略进行了比较。

Conclusion: VBT-MPC框架在机器人轮廓跟踪任务中有效，能够处理不同几何形状和材料的物体，且不需要复杂的位姿估计或力控制模块。

Abstract: Tactile sensing plays a key role in robotic manipulation, particularly in tasks like surface inspection. Successful execution requires maintaining contact while accurately tracking object contours. In this work, we propose a Vision-Based Tactile Model Predictive Control (VBT-MPC) framework for robotic contour following using a Vision-Based Tactile Sensor (VBTS) mounted in an eye-in-hand configuration. The proposed controller operates directly in contour features space, thereby avoiding the need for separate pose-estimation modules or complex force-control architectures. We further compare our VBT-MPC with visual-servoing strategies adapted to tactile features, and evaluate contour tracking on objects with diverse geometries and materials in both simulation and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [163] [Scalable Multi-robot Motion Planning via Hierarchical Subproblem Expansion and Workspace Decomposition Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20395)
*Isaac Ngui,Courtney McBeth,James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多机器人运动规划方法，通过利用工作空间分解的离散搜索来协调机器人，将规划时间提升一个数量级，并迭代细化工作空间表示以搜索更小的解耦配置空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人运动规划的核心挑战是：既要充分协调以避免机器人间冲突，又要避免联合配置空间搜索带来的巨大计算开销。

Method: 利用工作空间分解的离散搜索来提供规划期间的机器人协调；相比于先前基于工作空间拓扑决定何时需要协调再组合机器人进入联合配置空间的方法，本文进一步迭代细化工作空间表示，使得规划器能够搜索更小、解耦的配置空间。

Result: 规划时间提升达一个数量级（约10倍）。

Conclusion: 通过工作空间分解的离散搜索和迭代细化表示，可以有效平衡多机器人协调与计算效率，显著提升规划速度。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in multi-robot motion planning is achieving sufficient coordination to avoid inter-robot conflicts without incurring the large computational expense of searching the joint configuration space of the robot group. In this work, we present a method for multiple mobile robot motion planning that achieves an improvement in planning time up to an order of magnitude by leveraging the insight that we can use discrete search over a workspace decomposition to provide coordination between robots during planning. While prior work uses workspace topology to inform when coordination between robots is needed and then composes robots into their joint configuration space, we take a step further by iteratively refining our workspace representation to allow our planner to search smaller, decoupled configuration spaces.

</details>


### [164] [Spacetime Optimal-Transport Attention for Visuo-Haptic Imitation Learning of Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20433)
*Yue Feng,Weicheng Huang,I-Ming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SO-TA，一种基于最优传输的三模态融合注意力机制，用于高精度接触式操作任务。通过将力-位姿子查询与视觉补丁进行熵正则化最优传输对齐，替代传统softmax注意力，在紧配合装配、连接器插入和曲面擦拭等任务中显著优于基线方法，并具备可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触式操作任务面临间断接触动力学、部分可观测性和严格安全约束的挑战，单一传感模态（视觉、力/力矩、本体位姿）均不足以应对。现有方法大多使用单/双模态信号，少数三模态融合方法采用通用注意力模块，缺乏对注意力分布应如何根据任务相关区域进行分配的先验知识。

Method: 提出Spacetime Optimal-Transport Attention (SO-TA)，用熵正则化最优传输（OT）对齐替代softmax归一化的补丁注意力，在力-位姿导出的子查询与视觉补丁之间建立带显式边际约束的对齐，形成结构化的归纳偏置。搭配基于扩散的序列策略，将观测窗口映射为位姿-动作块。

Result: 在三个真实机器人任务（紧配合销孔装配、BCM接线连接器插入、曲面擦除）中，约200次实验/条件：紧配合任务100% vs 交叉注意力93%；光照、干扰物、部分遮挡扰动下82.5% vs 拼接基线43.5%。OT导出的补丁热图和留一法模态影响比提供了可解释的阶段相关诊断。

Conclusion: SO-TA通过最优传输的结构化归纳偏置有效提升了三模态融合在接触式操作任务中的性能和鲁棒性，同时提供了可解释性诊断工具。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation tasks such as tight-clearance insertion, connector mating, polishing, and surface-conforming wiping remain difficult for data-driven controllers because they couple discontinuous contact dynamics, partial observability, and strict safety constraints. No single sensing modality suffices: vision supplies global context before contact, force/torque (F/T) feedback governs interaction after contact, and proprioceptive pose provides a consistent kinematic backbone. Most prior imitation-learning policies for contact-rich tasks operate on uni- or bi-modal signals, and the few that fuse three modalities typically adopt off-the-shelf attention modules with no explicit prior on how attention mass should be distributed across task-relevant regions. We present Spacetime Optimal-Transport Attention (SO-TA), a tri-modal fusion backbone that replaces softmax-normalized patch attention by an entropy-regularized Optimal Transport (OT) alignment between force-pose-derived sub-queries and visual patches. Explicit marginal constraints act as a structured inductive bias for contact-rich tasks, encouraging conditioning-aware spatial selection that is stable across illumination, distractors, and partial occlusion. SO-TA is paired with a diffusion-based sequence policy mapping observation windows to pose-action chunks. We evaluate SO-TA on three real-robot tasks: tight peg-in-hole assembly, BCM wiring-connector insertion, and curved-surface mark erasing. With ~200 rollouts per condition, SO-TA reaches 100% success on tight peg-in-hole versus 93% for cross-attention at matched capacity, and retains 82.5% success under illumination, distractor, and partial-occlusion perturbations where a concatenation baseline drops to 43.5%. OT-derived patch heatmaps and leave-one-out modality-influence ratios provide interpretable, phase-dependent diagnostics.

</details>


### [165] [Enhancing Graph-Based SLAM in GNSS-Denied environments by leveraging leg odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20484)
*Léon Perruchot-Triboulet,Luc Jaulin,Kai Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因子图架构，在LIO-SAM框架中融合腿部里程计，将四足机器人在GNSS拒止环境中的高程漂移从30米以上降至30厘米以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人依赖LiDAR等外部传感器进行自主导航时，在几何稀疏或重复场景中易发生高程漂移，而GNSS信号又不可用，亟需轻量级的高程约束方案。

Method: 在LIO-SAM因子图框架中增设一条并行运动学支路，利用腿部本体里程计（proprioceptive leg odometry）提供高程信息，通过带选择性噪声模型的恒等相对位姿约束与主LiDAR-惯性支路耦合。

Result: 在Linxai D50四足平台上，两个户外环路总计超1公里的实验中，高程漂移从30米以上降至30厘米以下，且在基线方法完全失败的场景中实现了收敛。

Conclusion: 本体感知数据（已用于步态控制）在GNSS拒止环境中是一种轻量且有效的高程锚点，可显著提升SLAM系统的垂直精度。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in GNSS-denied environments remains a core challenge for legged robots, where exteroceptive sensors such as LiDAR are prone to elevation drift in geometrically sparse or repetitive scenes. We present a factor graph architecture that augments the LIO-SAM framework with a parallel kinematic lane driven by proprioceptive leg odometry, coupled to the main LiDAR-inertial lane via an identity relative pose constraint with a selective noise model. Applied to a Linxai D50 quadruped platform across two outdoor loops totaling over one kilometer, our approach reduces elevation drift from over 30m to under 30cm and enables convergence in a scene where the baseline pipeline fails entirely. These results suggest that proprioceptive data, already computed onboard for gait control, constitutes a lightweight and effective vertical anchor for SLAM in GNSS-denied settings.

</details>


### [166] [The Yes-Man Syndrome: Benchmarking Abstention in Embodied Robotic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20544)
*Doguhan Yeke,Elif Su Temirel,Ananth Shreekumar,Brandon Lee,Dongyan Xu,Z Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboAbstention，一个用于评估视觉-语言模型在具身机器人场景中"弃权"（即识别何时不应执行指令）能力的基准。通过对5个机器人数据集的图像生成6069条指令，发现前沿VLM模型弃权率最高仅39.0%，而通过防御性提示和上下文学习可提升至93.6%，但仍未完全解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM在纯文本场景中的弃权能力，但忽略了具身机器人环境中指令的感知基础（如视觉观察）和物理约束（如可行性）。机器人需要在指令模糊、物理上不可行、基于错误前提或感知信息不足时主动弃权，现有基准无法覆盖这些场景。

Method: 提出一套分类法（taxonomy）来定义具身机器人中的弃权场景。构建RoboAbstention框架，采用三阶段流水线：(1)结构化视觉锚定，(2)确定性约束推导，(3)通过类别特定模板生成受控指令。从五个机器人数据集中收集图像并生成包含可验证弃权条件的多样化数据集（共6069条指令）。

Result: 多个前沿VLM在弃权任务上表现显著薄弱：最佳模型Gemini 2.5 Flash弃权率仅39.0%，新发布具身规划器Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview仅16.5%。通过防御性提示和上下文学习可大幅提升性能，Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview达93.6%，GPT 5.4 Mini达88.6%，但无一模型完全解决该问题。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在具身机器人场景下缺乏可靠的弃权能力，即使具备高级推理能力的模型也是如此。现有干预方法虽能改善但不够完善，该问题仍需进一步研究。数据集和框架已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are used as high-level planners for embodied agents, translating natural language instructions and visual observations into action plans. While prior work has studied abstention in LLMs, existing benchmarks are largely text-only and do not capture the perceptual grounding and physical constraints inherent to embodied robotics environments. In such settings, abstention requires recognizing when instructions are ambiguous, physically infeasible, based on false premises, or otherwise unresolvable given the available sensory modalities and context. To address this gap, we introduce a taxonomy to categorize abstention in the context of embodied robotics and present RoboAbstention, a scalable and auditable framework for generating abstention instructions grounded in images gathered from five robotics datasets. RoboAbstention instantiates the taxonomy through a three-phase pipeline: (1) structured visual grounding, (2) deterministic constraint derivation, and (3) controlled instruction generation via category-specific templates. This enables the construction of a diverse dataset with verifiable abstention conditions. We evaluate several frontier VLMs and find that all models exhibit significant weaknesses in abstention, including those with advanced reasoning capabilities. The best-performing model, Gemini 2.5 Flash, abstains on only 39.0% of our 6,069 benchmark instructions, while the embodied planner Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview abstains on just 16.5%. We further explore methods for improving abstention in VLM planners, such as defensive prompting and in-context learning, and find that these interventions substantially improve performance, reaching 93.6% abstention rate for Gemini Robotics ER 1.6 Preview and 88.6% for GPT 5.4 Mini, yet no approach fully solves the problem. We open-source RoboAbstention at https://purseclab.github.io/RoboAbstention/.

</details>


### [167] [Fault-Tolerant, Rigidity-Preserving Control of Inflatable Truss Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20561)
*James Wade,Isaac Weaver,Mihai Stanciu,Nathan Usevitch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对可充气等周桁架机器人的电机故障问题，提出了一种容错控制框架，通过扩展运动学优化处理任意电机故障组合、引入离散时间控制屏障函数保证结构刚性，并实现闭环位置控制提升精度。在2D试验台上验证了单电机故障下保留超69%工作空间，闭环控制跟踪精度提升超25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等周桁架机器人具有高强度比、可大幅变形和重构等优势，但工作环境中的电机故障会严重限制其操作能力，现有方法缺乏对任意电机故障组合的系统性容错控制方案。

Method: 1) 扩展运动学优化，通过等式约束禁止使用故障电机处理任意故障组合；2) 引入离散时间控制屏障函数(DTCBF)约束，数学上保证结构刚性并最大化工作空间利用；3) 实现基于编码器反馈和前向运动学状态估计器的闭环位置控制。

Result: 在2D六执行器配置中，单电机故障下工作空间保留超69%；闭环控制相比开环控制跟踪精度提升超25%。

Conclusion: 所提框架为在降级驱动条件下运行更鲁棒、更具弹性的等周桁架机器人奠定了基础。

Abstract: Isoperimetric robotic trusses can adapt to different tasks and environments because they have a high strength-to-weight ratio, can change their own shape dramatically, and can be reconfigured into a variety of different shapes. However, motor failures in operational environments can severely limit operational capabilities if not properly addressed. This paper presents a fault-tolerant control framework for an inflatable robotic truss that maintains functionality despite motor failures, shown through three key contributions. First, we extend the kinematic optimization to handle arbitrary combinations of motor failures by imposing equality constraints to ensure failed actuators are not used. Second, we introduce discrete-time control barrier function (DTCBF) constraints that mathematically guarantee structural rigidity while maximizing workspace utilization, a critical requirement for reliable operation of truss robots under discrete-time control. Third, we implement closed-loop position control using onboard encoder feedback and a forward kinematics-based state estimator, improving positional accuracy in the presence of disturbances. We validate our approach through simulation and hardware experiments on a 2D isoperimetric truss testbed. For a 2D configuration with 6 actuators, we demonstrate >69% workspace preservation under single-motor failures and a >25% improvement in tracking accuracy with closed-loop control. These results establish a foundation for more robust and resilient isoperimetric truss robots operating under degraded actuation.

</details>


### [168] [Conflict-Aware Active Perception and Control in 3D Gaussian Splatting Fields via Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20566)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Vivek Pandey,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种冲突感知的主动感知与控制框架，利用3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)表示环境，通过控制障碍函数(CBF)保障安全，同时引入风险感知的期望信息增益(EIG)和感知障碍函数优化信息采集，最终通过统一二次规划(QP)协调安全与感知的冲突目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在不确定环境中进行主动感知时，安全导航与获取信息观测这两个目标存在天然冲突——信息量大的视角通常靠近碰撞风险高的不确定区域。现有方法难以有效处理这一矛盾。

Method: 1) 使用3DGS表示环境；2) 基于AV@R碰撞风险指标推导CBF保证安全前向不变性；3) 提出风险感知EIG选择下一个最佳视角；4) 引入感知障碍函数使相机朝向对齐局部信息上升方向；5) 构建安全关键-感知感知二次规划(QP)，将安全作为硬约束，感知作为软约束（引入松弛变量）。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与现有基于3DGS的方法相比，该方法在安全性和信息获取方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过将安全约束作为硬约束、感知约束作为软约束的统一QP框架，有效协调了主动感知中安全与信息获取的冲突，在保证安全的同时提升了信息采集效率。

Abstract: Active perception in uncertain environments requires robots to navigate safely while acquiring informative observations to reduce map uncertainty. These objectives inherently conflict, as informative viewpoints often lie near uncertain regions with higher collision risk. To address this challenge, we develop a conflict-aware active perception and control framework for robotic systems operating in environments represented by 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Safety is enforced using a Control Barrier Function (CBF) derived from an Average Value-at-Risk AV@R collision-risk metric that accounts for geometric uncertainty and guarantees forward invariance of a safe set. To improve perception, we propose a risk-aware Expected Information Gain (EIG) formulation for selecting the next-best-view and introduce perception barrier functions that align the camera orientation with the local information-ascent direction. To obtain a tractable formulation for these conflicting safety and perception objectives, we propose a unified safety-critical, perception-aware quadratic program that enforces safety as a hard constraint while relaxing perception constraints through slack variables. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method improves both safety and information acquisition compared to existing 3DGS-based approaches.

</details>


### [169] [Intent-First Aerial V2V for Tactical Coordination and Separation: Protocol and Performance Under Density and Disturbance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20595)
*Mehrnaz Sabet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了首个控制器耦合的全空中、侧链级、意图优先的V2V战术邻域通信协议栈，用于密集无人机系统交通管理（UTM）。该协议栈在低中密度场景下有效减少信念分歧、保持可观性、抑制错误推理并协调资源，但在高密度、高复杂度场景下需回退至保护性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机交通管理存在空白：预飞行路线协调无法应对空中突发扰动，而碰撞避免为时已晚且破坏性过大。战术间隔（tactical separation）虽被视为中间层，但缺乏可大规模部署的邻域通信机制来提供实时、可信的本地协调信息。

Method: 提出一个全空中V2V战术邻域通信协议栈，采用侧链级C-V2X模块和认证新鲜度检查。结合刷新状态/意图信标（用于感知、协作感知、降级评估）与事件触发消息（让行、排序、释放、应急协调），并通过实时场测基础设施支撑的高压力场景驱动测试进行验证。

Result: V2V协议减少了信念分歧、通过协作感知保持可观性、拒斥无效战术消息、抑制错误的本地推理、结构化共享资源协调。在低中密度场景下可行，但随密度、干扰和复杂度增加则需转向保护性回退模式。

Conclusion: 意图优先的空中V2V可作为有界使能器，拓展受扰动驱动城市空域中战术协调的规模化能力，但存在适用边界，需在高密度复杂场景下辅以保护性机制。

Abstract: Dense low-altitude aerial operations require more than pre-flight route coordination and last-resort collision avoidance. Once aircraft are airborne, disturbances can emerge on timescales shorter than strategic reauthorization can absorb, while collision avoidance is too late and disruptive to serve as routine traffic management. Although tactical separation is recognized as the intermediate layer, realizing it at scale requires a deployable neighborhood communication mechanism that provides fresh, trusted information for local coordination. This paper presents what is, to our knowledge, the first controller-coupled characterization of an all-airborne, sidelink-class, intent-first vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) tactical neighborhood exchange stack for dense Unmanned Aircraft System Traffic Management (UTM) operations. Unlike awareness-only broadcast, the proposed exchange combines refreshed state and intent beacons for local awareness, cooperative perception, and degraded-mode assessment with event-triggered messages for yielding, sequencing, release, and contingency coordination. We implement and evaluate this model on an all-airborne V2V stack using sidelink-class C-V2X modules with authenticated freshness checks. Evaluation uses a scenario-driven, high-volume stress campaign supported by real-time, field-anchored infrastructure. Results show that V2V reduces stale-belief divergence, preserves observability through cooperative perception, rejects invalid tactical messages, suppresses false local inference, and structures shared-resource coordination. The implemented stack provides a viable communication layer for tactical separation in lower-to-moderate regimes, but transitions toward guarded fallback as density, impairment, and complexity increase. These findings position intent-first aerial V2V as a bounded enabler for scaling tactical coordination in disturbance-driven urban airspace.

</details>


### [170] [Jointly Learning Predicates and Actions Enables Zero-Shot Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20648)
*Benedict Quartey,Sebastian Castro,Eric Rosen,Wil Thomason,George Konidaris,Stefanie Tellex*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PACTS技能模型，通过联合生成动作轨迹和谓词信念轨迹，实现机器人技能在新组合上的零样本泛化，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD方法无法泛化到已知技能的新组合，生成式策略仅建模动作轨迹，无法推理符号化结果，导致组合泛化能力差。

Method: 提出Predicate Action Skills (PACTS)，一种闭环视觉运动策略，将技能建模为动作和谓词信念轨迹的联合生成过程，利用在线谓词预测作为符号接口进行规划与执行监控。

Result: PACTS的内部表征同时提升了动作生成和谓词分类性能，并实现了学习技能的零样本组合。

Conclusion: 联合建模动作与符号化结果（谓词）是提升机器人技能组合泛化能力的关键方向。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration (LfD) enables robots to learn complex behaviors from expert examples, yet existing approaches often fail to generalize to new compositions of known skills without retraining. Modern generative policies model distributions over action trajectories alone, thus are unable to reason about the symbolic outcomes required for robust composition. We propose that skills should jointly model action trajectories and the symbolic outcomes they induce. To address this gap, we introduce Predicate Action Skills (PACTS), a class of closed-loop visuomotor policies that model skills as a joint generative process over action and predicate belief trajectories, producing coherent action-outcome rollouts within a single model. Jointly generating actions and predicates enables PACTS to learn internal representations that improve both action generation and predicate classification. Furthermore, we demonstrate zero-shot composition of learned skills via planning by leveraging online predicate predictions from PACTS as a symbolic interface for sequencing and monitoring execution. Project website: https://planpacts.github.io/

</details>


### [171] [A Semantic and Occlusion-Aware GM-PHD Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20666)
*Jovan Menezes,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合深度学习的语义信息和遮挡感知机制的出生模型（S-OA），用于高斯混合概率假设密度滤波器，在复杂驾驶场景中显著提升了目标跟踪的初始化和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有出生模型依赖简单或均匀假设，未充分利用环境语义信息和遮挡区域先验，导致在复杂高密度驾驶场景中跟踪性能受限。

Method: 提出语义-遮挡感知（S-OA）出生模型，利用深度学习提取语义信息，显式考虑遮挡区域来定义初始化项，并将其集成到GM-PHD滤波框架中。

Result: 在KITTI数据集和蒙特卡洛仿真上评估，S-OA模型在约70%的案例中匹配或超越最强基线，有效降低了遮挡密集场景下的初始化延迟，在平均绝对基数误差和OSPA指标上表现更优。

Conclusion: 将遮挡推理和语义先验融入贝叶斯跟踪框架，对于提升自动驾驶场景下的跟踪性能具有显著优势。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new birth model including semantic information derived from deep learning to create an occlusion-aware Gaussian Mixture Probability Hypothesis Density (GM-PHD) filter. Unlike prior approaches that rely on simplistic or uniform assumptions, the proposed Semantic-Occlusion Aware (S-OA) birth model defines initialization terms by explicitly considering regions of occlusion and by leveraging semantic information about the environment. This enables the filter to accurately represent where new objects are more likely to appear, thereby improving tracking performance in complex and high-density driving scenarios. The method is evaluated through Monte Carlo simulations and experiments on the KITTI dataset. Performance is assessed by measuring the latency between first detection and track initiation, along with the mean absolute cardinality error and the Optimal Subpattern Assignment (OSPA) metric. Results demonstrate that the S-OA birth model reduces initialization delay in occlusion-heavy settings, matching or outperforming the strongest baseline in approximately 70% of cases. A sensitivity analysis of birth model weights is also provided. Overall, the findings underscore the benefits of integrating occlusion reasoning and semantic priors into Bayesian tracking frameworks for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [172] [GaussianDream: A Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20752)
*Zijian Zhang,Yuqing Jiang,Qian Cheng,Si Liu,Ding Zhao,Ping Luo,Weitao Zhou,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianDream提出一种前馈式3D高斯世界模型插件，通过将机器人轨迹转化为结构化的时空监督信号（当前高斯重建+未来高斯预测），在不增加测试时渲染开销的条件下，提升VLA策略在3D几何、密集视觉结构和短时环境演化方面的物理操作精度。在LIBERO、RoboCasa Human-50和真实机器人任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准动作模仿训练对3D几何、密集视觉结构和短时环境演化的显式监督不足，限制了VLA策略在需要精确物理操作任务中的表现。

Method: 将高斯重建与基于视界的未来高斯预测耦合，训练时迫使紧凑的时空前缀可解码为可渲染的3D高斯状态，从而获得密集RGB渲染、深度和伪3D场景流监督；推理时丢弃辅助解码头，仅保留学习到的前缀用于条件动作生成。

Result: LIBERO上平均成功率98.4%，RoboCasa Human-50上52.6%，真实世界评估50.0%。

Conclusion: GaussianDream通过引入3D高斯世界模型作为训练插件，在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升了VLA策略在3D物理操作任务中的性能，验证了结构化时空监督的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have advanced language-conditioned robotic manipulation by transferring semantic priors from pretrained vision-language models to action generation. Yet, standard action-imitation training often provides limited explicit supervision for 3D geometry, dense visual structure, and short-horizon environment evolution, which are critical for physically precise manipulation. We introduce \textbf{GaussianDream}, a feed-forward 3D Gaussian world-model plug-in that turns robot trajectories into structured spatial-temporal supervision. The key idea is to couple current Gaussian reconstruction with horizon-conditioned future Gaussian prediction during training, forcing a compact spatio-temporal prefix to be decodable into renderable 3D Gaussian states. This enables dense RGB rendering, depth, and pseudo 3D scene-flow supervision without requiring test-time Gaussian decoding. At inference, GaussianDream discards all auxiliary decoding heads and retains only the learned prefix to condition action generation, avoiding rendering, video rollout, or additional planning during closed-loop control. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboCasa Human-50, and real-robot tasks demonstrate strong and highly competitive performance, achieving \textbf{98.4\%} average success on LIBERO, \textbf{52.6\%} on RoboCasa Human-50, and \textbf{50.0\%} in real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [173] [VLA-REPLICA: A Low-Cost, Reproducible Benchmark for Real-World Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20774)
*Alex S. Huang,Jiahui Zhang,Shiqing Tang,Yu Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-REPLICA是一个低成本、易复现的机器人操作基准测试平台，用于评估视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，包含多样化任务集和评估协议，实验结果在不同实验室间具有良好的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真基准无法捕捉真实世界的复杂性，而真实世界基准要么硬件成本高、要么需要集中评估、要么任务多样性有限，缺乏一个低成本、可复现、一致的真实世界评估平台。

Method: 基于现成组件构建的低成本硬件系统，可快速组装和复现；包含多样化操作任务套件和小规模演示数据集；设计了分布内和分布外两种真实世界评估协议。

Result: 在模仿学习和最先进的VLA模型上进行了实验，揭示了模型各自的优势和局限性；并且在独立搭建的多个平台上取得一致结果，验证了基准的可复现性。

Conclusion: VLA-REPLICA为VLA模型提供了一个低成本、可复现的真实世界评估基准，填补了现有基准在可访问性和一致性方面的空白。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong promise for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but their real-world evaluation remains limited by a lack of accessible, reproducible, and consistent benchmarks. Simulation benchmarks fail to capture real-world complexity, while existing real-world benchmarks often require expensive hardware, centralized evaluation, or are limited in task diversity. We introduce VLA-REPLICA, a low-cost, easily reproducible real-world benchmark for evaluating VLA models. Built from off-the-shelf components, our system can be quickly assembled and replicated across laboratories, providing a consistent environment for policy evaluation anywhere in the world. VLA-REPLICA includes a diverse suite of manipulation tasks and a small-scale demonstration dataset for target-domain adaptation, with real-world evaluation protocols for both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Experiments with imitation learning and state-of-the-art VLA models reveal model strengths and limitations, while consistent results across independently constructed setups demonstrate the reproducibility of our benchmark.

</details>


### [174] [CMC-Opt: Constraint Manifold with Corners for Inequality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20796)
*Yetong Zhang,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流形的框架，将带等式和不等式约束的机器人优化问题转化为约束状态空间上的无约束优化问题，通过引入"带角落的约束流形"并扩展流形优化算法，在大规模动力学规划问题中展现了优于标准方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人优化方法在处理混合等式和不等式约束时面临挑战，标准方法可能失败，需要一种更鲁棒和通用的优化框架。

Method: 提出"带角落的约束流形"（constraint manifolds with corners）表示满足混合约束的状态空间，并将流形优化算法扩展到该新拓扑结构上，将原问题转化为约束状态空间上的无约束优化问题。

Result: 在大规模动力学规划问题中成功生成动态可行的轨迹，而标准方法在该任务中失败。

Conclusion: 基于流形的框架能够有效地处理混合等式/不等式约束的机器人优化问题，具有更强的鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: We introduce a manifold-based framework for addressing optimization problems with equality and inequality constraints found in robotics. Our approach transforms the original problem into an unconstrained optimization problem directly on the constrained state space. To achieve this, we introduce ``constraint manifolds with corners" to represent the state space satisfying mixed nonlinear equality and inequality constraints. We further extend manifold optimization algorithms to operate on this new topological structure. We demonstrate the power and robustness of our framework in the context of a large-scale kinodynamic planning problem, successfully generating dynamically feasible trajectories where standard methods fail.

</details>


### [175] [Q-SpiRL: Quantum Spiking Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20801)
*Mohamed Khair Altrabulsi,Nouhaila Innan,Alberto Marchisio,Muhammad Kashif,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-SpiRL框架，将量子计算与脉冲神经网络结合用于机器人导航，在动态障碍物环境中实现了高达99%的成功率与高效路径规划，并在IBM量子硬件上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人导航方法在动态环境中难以兼顾目标可达性、轨迹效率和运动平稳性，需要一种更强大的学习框架来应对复杂环境中的避障与路径优化问题。

Method: 提出了Q-SpiRL框架，开发了五种智能体（Q-learning、MLP、SNN、QMLP、QSNN），其中核心架构QSNN结合了脉冲神经网络的时间处理能力与变分量子特征变换，在三种不同尺寸的网格环境中进行训练和测试。

Result: QSNN在所有性能指标上表现最佳，在最具挑战性的40x40动态障碍物环境中达到99%的成功率，同时保持高路径效率和运动平滑度，并在IBM量子硬件上成功部署。

Conclusion: 量子增强脉冲神经网络（QSNN）在复杂动态导航任务中优于传统方法，实现了任务完成度、轨迹效率与运动平稳性的最佳平衡，且具备在真实量子设备上部署的可行性。

Abstract: Adaptive robot navigation in dynamic environments requires policies that can reach the target reliably while producing efficient and stable trajectories. This paper presents Q-SpiRL, a quantum spiking reinforcement learning framework for obstacle-aware robot navigation. The framework develops and evaluates five agent families: tabular Q-learning, classical MLP, classical SNN, quantum-enhanced MLP (QMLP), and quantum-enhanced spiking neural network (QSNN). While all models are implemented under a unified training and evaluation pipeline, the QSNN is the central architecture of interest, as it combines spike-based temporal processing with variational quantum feature transformation. Experiments are conducted across three grid-world environments of increasing size, namely 20x20, 30x30, and 40x40, with both static and dynamic obstacles. Performance is assessed using success rate, success-weighted path length, path length, and turn rate under deterministic inference. Results show that QSNN achieves the strongest overall trade-off between task completion, trajectory efficiency, and motion smoothness, reaching up to 99% success rate while maintaining high path efficiency in the most challenging setting. Execution on IBM quantum hardware further demonstrates the feasibility of deploying the proposed hybrid policy under real-device conditions.

</details>


### [176] [Demo-JEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for One-shot Cross-Embodiment Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20811)
*Jingyang He,Guangrun Li,Jieyu Zhang,Chengkai Hou,Zhengping Che,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Demo-JEPA是一个跨本体模仿学习框架，通过将演示解耦为未来目标状态而非具体动作，利用JEPA世界模型在不同形态的本体间实现视觉演示到潜空间轨迹的迁移，并通过规划实现灵活模仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习过于关注动作级别的复现，但动作与具体本体形态紧密耦合，跨本体模仿需要共享动作空间或大规模多本体协同训练，效率低下且泛化能力差。

Method: 提出Demo-JEPA框架，基于JEPA世界模型将源本体的视觉演示转化为目标本体可兼容的未来潜空间轨迹（而非动作序列），目标本体利用自身学习的正向动力学模型规划实现这些潜目标。

Result: 在RLBench和真实世界操作任务上，Demo-JEPA匹配了专门的域内规划器，并在未见过的任务和本体配置上泛化能力优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 跨本体模仿学习应关注演示意图（目标状态）而非执行方式（具体动作），Demo-JEPA通过潜空间目标规划有效解耦了意图与执行，实现了灵活鲁棒的跨本体模仿。

Abstract: Robotic imitation learning is often treated as reproducing demonstrated actions, but actions are inherently embodiment-specific. When demonstrations come from humans or robots with different morphology, kinematics, or action spaces, this action-centric view requires shared action spaces, heuristic retargeting, or large-scale multi-embodiment co-training. We instead view demonstrations as implicit specifications of future goals: the target agent should infer what state the demonstrator is trying to realize, rather than how the demonstrator executes it. We propose Demo-JEPA, a cross-embodiment imitation framework that decouples demonstration intent from embodiment-specific execution. Built on a JEPA-based world model, Demo-JEPA translates source visual demonstrations into target-compatible future latent trajectories in a shared predictive representation space. The target agent then uses these latent trajectories as subgoals and realizes them through planning under its own learned forward dynamics. Because Demo-JEPA avoids action-level correspondence and requires only visual demonstrations plus the target agent's own interaction experience, it supports flexible imitation across heterogeneous embodiments. Experiments on RLBench and real-world manipulation tasks show that Demo-JEPA matches specialized in-domain planners and generalizes to unseen tasks and embodiment configurations where prior methods fail.

</details>


### [177] [SmoCap: Unified Scale-Pose Canonicalization with Proxy-Mapped Trust-Region QP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20850)
*Shihao Li,Naohiko Sugita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmoCap是一个泄漏抵抗的规范化框架，通过稀疏控制子空间中的局部置信域二次规划（QP）联合估计形态和姿态，解决了分阶段工作流中形态-姿态补偿导致的解剖不一致问题，在膝关节屈曲、人体测量误差和脊柱运动表达上均取得优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分阶段的模型缩放和逆运动学工作流会导致形态-姿态补偿，尤其在弱观测方向上产生解剖不一致但数值上可接受的解，因此需要一种联合估计形态和姿态的框架来避免泄漏问题。

Method: 提出SmoCap框架，在稀疏控制子空间内求解带约束的置信域二次规划（QP），使用解析代理映射的姿态和尺度雅可比矩阵，低维代理映射稳定弱观测方向并驱动协调结构，可选的预求解为困难配置提供热启动。

Result: 膝关节屈曲RMSE为2.9度（与透视对照），人体测量端点误差约3%。泄漏审计中降低了标记RMSE、FE误差和人体测量端点误差。代理耦合在瑜伽消融实验中仅增加边际拟合误差（+0.14 mm, +0.6%）。中位标记RMSE约20 mm，中位运行时间0.204-0.332 ms/帧，仅需2-3次迭代。

Conclusion: SmoCap提供了一个外部验证的统一耦合感知尺度-姿态框架，使得在数据集规模上实现外部一致的运动规范化成为可能。

Abstract: Objective: Stage-wise workflows that separate model scaling and inverse kinematics can induce morphology-posture compensation, resulting in anatomically inconsistent yet numerically acceptable solutions, especially in weakly observed directions. We present SmoCap, a leakage-resistant canonicalization framework that estimates morphology and posture jointly in each local trust-region quadratic program (QP) within a sparse control subspace. Methods: SmoCap solves a constrained trust-region QP with analytical proxy-mapped pose and scale Jacobians. The low dimensional proxy map stabilizes weakly observed directions and drives coordinated structures. An optional pre-solve provides warm starts in difficult configurations. The framework is evaluated using cohort fluoroscopy knee motion, anthropometric ground truth, and extreme yoga sequences. Results: SmoCap achieved 2.9 degree knee flexion RMSE against fluoroscopy, and a pooled anthropometric endpoint error around 3%. In the leakage audit against segment wise scaling, SmoCap also reduced marker RMSE, FE error, and anthropometric endpoint error. Proxy coupling preserved expressive and coordinated spine motion with marginal fitting error increase (+0.14 mm, +0.6%) against baseline models in yoga ablation. Median marker RMSE was around 20 mm, and median runtime was 0.204-0.332 ms/frame, achieved with consistently 2-3 iterations. Conclusion: SmoCap provides an externally validated unified coupling-aware scale-pose framework, making externally consistent motion canonicalization practical at dataset scale.

</details>


### [178] [Mobile UMI: Cross-View Diffusion Policy with Decoupled Kinematics for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20894)
*Haoran Huang,Haonan Dong,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mobile UMI是一种无需硬件的移动模仿学习框架，通过双摄像头系统、ChArUco空间锚定和非同步滚动时域执行器，解决了便携式演示界面上的动作标签污染和推理延迟两大瓶颈，在四项长时域家务任务中平均成功率达到83.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动模仿学习面临两个耦合瓶颈：一是人类行走动作污染了手部动作标签，二是生成式策略的推理延迟导致机器人基座在预测执行前已经移动，造成动作拼接时的反向修正。需要一种无需额外硬件的解决方案来解耦这些干扰。

Method: 提出Mobile UMI框架，包含三个组件：1) 双摄像头采集系统（胸部全局视角+手腕局部视角）；2) 基于ChArUco的一步空间锚定，统一胸部与手部视觉-惯性坐标系，将手部姿态相对于胸部重新表达，提取解耦的SE(3)操作轨迹和SE(2)基座轨迹；3) 异步滚动时域执行器，在线状态匹配，丢弃已过期的路径点后再执行。

Result: 在四项长时域家务任务上评估，每项任务100次试验，平均成功率83.8%。对照实验表明，胸部相对标签和在线状态匹配分别填补了主要和剩余的性能差距。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，显式运动学分解结合状态级延迟对齐为移动模仿学习提供了有效解决方案，无需改变底层策略类的架构。

Abstract: Mobile imitation learning on portable demonstration interfaces faces two coupled bottlenecks: locomotion-contaminated action labels and inference-induced execution latency on a continuously moving base. Recent wrist-mounted interfaces lower the cost of tabletop data collection, yet a single wrist view does not capture the global context required for base navigation. Adding a body-mounted camera entangles human walking with hand motion. Meanwhile, generative policies introduce hundreds of milliseconds of inference latency, during which the base advances past predicted waypoints, forcing backward corrections at action splices. This paper presents Mobile UMI, a hardware-free demonstration framework that addresses both gaps through three components. First, a dual-camera capture system records chest-centric global context and wrist-centric local interaction without any robot present. Second, a one-shot ChArUco-based spatial anchor unifies the chest and hand visual-inertial frames; the hand pose is then re-expressed relative to the chest to extract decoupled SE(3) manipulation and SE(2) base trajectories. Third, an asynchronous receding-horizon executor performs online state matching: each generated action chunk is realigned with the current physical pose so that expired waypoints are discarded before execution. The full system is evaluated on four long-horizon household tasks, achieving an average success rate of 83.8% over 100 trials per task. Controlled comparisons against ACT and Diffusion Policy show that the chest-relative label alone closes much of the gap; online state matching closes the remainder. These results indicate that, for mobile imitation learning under the tested conditions, explicit kinematic factorization combined with state-level latency alignment provides an effective solution without requiring architectural changes to the underlying policy class.

</details>


### [179] [SubTGraph: Large-Scale Subterranean Environment Synthesis with Controllable Topological Variability for Robotic Autonomy Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20917)
*F. Labra Caso,A. Saradagi,S. Fredriksson,S. Nordström,A. Koval,G. Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SubTGraph框架，用于快速生成高变异性的多层地下环境（如矿井、天然洞穴、熔岩管），以支持机器人自主系统的严格统计验证。该框架结合用户指定的拓扑、维度、纹理等约束，利用Dijkstra算法和DARPA世界生成器的拓扑度量瓦片程序化生成环境。通过三个机器人案例（结构语义分割、多智能体路径规划、LIO SLAM）验证了其有效性，并开源了代码和150个高变异性地下世界数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下环境（如矿井、火星熔岩管）是自主机器人研究的前沿，但由于真实地下环境难以进入，亟需高保真仿真环境进行严格的自主系统验证。现有研究缺乏大规模、高变异性的仿真基准基础设施，导致验证结果往往仅基于少数环境，统计意义不足。

Method: SubTGraph框架：根据用户指定的结构约束（拓扑、维度、纹理等）构建代价矩阵，指导经典Dijkstra算法，利用DARPA World Generator的拓扑度量瓦片程序化生成多样的地下世界。支持多种环境类型（矿井、洞穴、熔岩管），并可控制生成环境的变异程度。

Result: 通过三个机器人案例验证：(1) 结构语义分割验证了与拓扑度量真值的一致性；(2) 多智能体路径规划测试揭示了算法行为模式与趋势；(3) LIO SLAM在挑战性地下区域进行压力测试，发现了失败案例。开源了包含150个高变异性地下世界的数据库。

Conclusion: SubTGraph填补了地下机器人自主系统大规模仿真基准测试的空白，能够生成高度可变的环境用于严格的统计评估，有助于推动地下机器人自主系统的可靠部署。

Abstract: Subterranean (SubT) environments have been a frontier for autonomous robotics, driven by the push for automation of mining operations and the interest in planetary exploration (Martian Lava Tubes). Due to the challenges involved in accessing real SubT environments, rigorous hardening of autonomy stacks in realistic simulation environments is critical. This article fills a well-known gap, which relates to the unavailability of a large-scale simulation-based benchmarking infrastructure for rigorous statistical evaluation of robotic autonomy, due to which it is common for SubT research articles to present validation results in a few environments at best.
  This article presents SubTGraph, a novel framework for rapid synthesis of multi-level SubT environments with high variability, incorporating user specifications related to topology, dimensionality, textures, etc., to generate distinct environments such as operational mines, natural caves and lava tubes. SubTGraph builds a cost matrix from user-specified structural constraints to guide the classical Dijkstra algorithm to procedurally generate SubT worlds utilizing topometric tiles from the DARPA World Generator. Three robotics case-studies are investigated to demonstrate the utility of SubTGraph for rigorous validation of different layers in the robotic autonomy stack. Structural semantic segmentation is validated against topometric ground truths, multi-agent path planning is widely tested for identification of patterns and trends in the algorithm behavior and LIO SLAM is stress-tested in challenging subterranean sections to identify failure cases. The SubTGraph world creation codebase is open-sourced (https://github.com/LTU-RAI/SubTGraph.git) along with a database consisting of 150 highly variable underground worlds.

</details>


### [180] [STEAM: A Training-Free Congestion-Aware Enhancement Framework for Decentralized Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20929)
*Mingyang Feng,Mengnuo Zhang,Shaoyuan Li,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STEAM是一种无需训练的测试时增强框架，通过注入拥塞感知引导来提升基于学习的分散式多智能体路径规划性能，在成功率、完工时间和成本上均有显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的分散式MAPF方法在面临拥塞时性能下降，而集中式规划器虽然效果好但扩展性差。作者希望在不重新训练、不改架构的情况下，提升预训练分散式策略在拥塞环境中的表现。

Method: STEAM包含三部分：1) 利用成本图生成最短路径以预测未来拥塞热点；2) 空间可避免拥塞通过更新智能体专属成本图缓解；3) 空间不可避免的瓶颈通过时序logit校正处理；4) 涌现性局部拥塞通过基于邻居智能体成本图的密度感知logit校正减轻。

Result: 在多个代表性学习型分散式MAPF算法上，STEAM持续提升了成功率、完工时间和路径成本，成功率提升高达60%，仅增加少量计算开销。

Conclusion: STEAM是一种轻量级、无需重新训练的测试时增强框架，能有效提升分散式MAPF算法的拥塞感知能力，具有较好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: We propose STEAM (Spatial, Temporal, and Emergent congestion Awareness for MAPF), a training-free test-time enhancement framework for learning-based decentralized Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) in discrete environments. Given a pretrained decentralized policy, STEAM requires no retraining, architectural modification, or replacement by a centralized planner. Instead, it injects lightweight congestion-aware guidance into the original policy execution. STEAM first rolls out the shortest paths induced by the current cost-to-go maps to identify potential future congestion hotspots. Spatially avoidable congestion is mitigated by updating agent-specific cost-to-go information, while spatially unavoidable bottlenecks are handled through temporal logit correction. In addition, emergent local congestion is reduced by a density-aware logit correction based on neighboring agents' corrected cost-to-go maps. Extensive experiments on representative learning-based decentralized MAPF algorithms show that STEAM consistently improves success rate, makespan, and solution cost, with success-rate gains of up to 60% and only minor computational overhead. The implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/STEAM-MAPF-7A62.

</details>


### [181] [WiXus: A Wheeled-Legged Robot with Wire-Driven Environmental Utilizing to Integrate Mobility and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20932)
*Shintaro Inoue,Kento Kawaharazuka,Temma Suzuki,Sota Yuzaki,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并开发了新型轮腿混合机器人WiXus，通过结合轮腿驱动与钢丝驱动机制，使机器人的腿部不仅能用于 locomotion，还能在身体被钢丝悬挂时充当手臂进行物体操作和工具使用，展示了扩展轮腿机器人操作域的新设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
