Motivation: 现有轮腿机器人仅专注于 locomotion，腿部无法在无需行走时重新用于其他任务（如物体操作或工具使用）。本研究旨在通过外部身体支撑释放腿部的 locomotion 角色，挖掘其潜在的任务执行能力。

Method: 提出并开发WiXus机器人，融合轮腿机构与利用外部环境的钢丝驱动机构；通过协调钢丝驱动与轮腿驱动实现三维移动（如攀爬悬崖）；通过钢丝悬挂身体，使腿部被解放并用作手臂执行操作任务。

Result: WiXus不仅实现了平面轮腿移动，还展示了三维移动能力（如悬崖攀爬）；在身体被钢丝悬挂后，成功将腿部作为手臂执行物体操作（如营救玩具狗）和工具使用（如用修枝剪采摘苹果模型）。

Conclusion: 利用钢丝驱动借助外部环境的方法是一种新的设计原则，能够扩展轮腿机器人的操作域，赋予其超越 locomotion 的多任务能力。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots, which have wheels at their feet and achieve high mobility by coordinating wheel drive and leg drive, have been developed. These robots have been developed purely as platforms specialized for locomotion. Therefore, they do not have a means to repurpose their legs for roles other than locomotion, such as object manipulation or tool utilization. In this paper, we address the problem of how to draw out the potential task-execution capability of the legs by freeing them from the roles of locomotion through external body support. To this end, we propose and develop a new robot, WiXus, which fuses a wheeled-legged mechanism with a wire-driven mechanism that utilizes the external environment. The developed WiXus demonstrates not only planar locomotion with wheeled-legged drive, but also three-dimensional mobility such as cliff climbing by coordinating wire-driven and wheeled-legged actuation. Furthermore, by suspending the body with wire-driven actuation, WiXus successfully repurpose its legs as arms to perform object manipulation, (e.g., rescuing a dog (stuffed animal)), and tool utilization (e.g., harvesting an apple (mockup) with loppers). This study demonstrates that the approach of utilizing the environment with wire-driven actuation is a new design principle that extends the operational domain of wheeled-legged robots.

</details>


### [182] [Component Influence-Driven Fastener Reduction for Robotic Disassemblability-Aware Design Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21026)
*Takuya Kiyokawa,Tomoki Ishikura,Shingo Hamada,Genichiro Matsuda,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分析框架，通过将机器人拆解序列规划结果转化为零件影响分数并投影到CAD模型上生成3D热力图，帮助设计师识别冗余紧固件以减少结构约束、工具切换和机器人移动距离，从而优化产品设计以加速自动化再制造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计师缺乏定量反馈来判断哪些结构要素会阻碍机器人操作，因此需要一种能够提供可操作的重新设计指导的分析框架。

Method: 基于CAD模型自动生成接触-连接-约束（CCC）图，将机器人拆解序列规划结果转化为零件影响分数，投影为3D热力图，并模拟移除高影响紧固件，评估结构稳定性后输出预期改进。

Result: 在七种家用电器上实验，移除推荐紧固件后消除了8到132个结构约束，减少了不必要的工具更换操作，并缩短了165到1675毫米的机器人移动距离。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效定位冗余紧固件，在保证结构安全的前提下显著提升机器人拆解效率，为面向机器人拆解的设计提供了定量化、可视化的决策支持。

Abstract: To accelerate automated remanufacturing, robotic disassembly must be considered during the product design phase. However, designers currently lack quantitative feedback to identify which structural elements hinder robotic operations. To address this, this study proposes an analytical framework that provides actionable redesign guidance focused on fastener reduction, as fasteners are numerous and ubiquitous components found in almost all manufactured products. Using a Computer-Aided Design (CAD) model and its automatically generated Contact-Connection-Constraint (CCC) graph, the framework translates robotic disassembly sequence planning outcomes into component influence scores. These scores reflect how often a component causes structural constraint violations or evaluation objective deteriorations in the robotic disassembly sequence. To visually highlight structural hindrances, the framework projects these scores onto the CAD geometry as 3D heatmaps. The system then analytically simulates the removal of highly influential fasteners. It reports the expected reductions in structural constraints, tool changes, and robot travel distances, while preventing structurally unsafe modifications by evaluating geometric stability metrics. Experiments on seven household appliances demonstrate that the framework successfully targets redundant fasteners. Removing the recommended fasteners simplified the structural dependencies by eliminating between 8 and 132 structural constraints on the graph depending on each product's structural configuration. Furthermore, it improved robotic operational efficiency by eliminating unnecessary tool change operations and shortening travel distances by 165 to 1675 millimeters wherever structurally permissible.

</details>


### [183] [Modeling and Control of a Pneumatic Morphing Soft Quadrotor based on the SOFA Framework for Dynamic Soft Robotic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21031)
*F. Labra Caso,V. Sumathy,P. Ferrentino,V. Vanderborght,J. Haluska,G. Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SOFA的有限元方法，用于气动变形软体四旋翼飞行器的软体建模、动态仿真与控制。该方法在保留传统四旋翼物理可解释性和控制结构的同时，捕捉了气动软臂的复杂时变行为，并通过比例积分控制器实现了软臂的目标位置控制，仿真验证了模型与控制器的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四旋翼动力学模型难以捕捉气动软臂的复杂时变行为，缺乏兼具物理可解释性与软体变形能力的建模框架。

Method: 基于SOFA平台，采用四面体网格离散化软臂，应用弹性材料定律产生内应力；在内部空腔施加周期性和误差驱动控制信号实现气动驱动；设计比例积分（PI）控制器控制软臂达到目标位置。

Result: 仿真结果表明所提出的建模框架和控制器设计在气动变形软臂的动态行为控制与形态变化方面具有有效性。

Conclusion: 该SOFA基有限元方法成功平衡了传统四旋翼控制结构与软体变形复杂性，为气动变形软体飞行器的建模与控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This article presents a novel SOFA based finite element method for the soft body modeling and the corresponding dynamic simulation and control of a pneumatic morphing soft quadrotor. The proposed modeling preserves the physical interpretability and control structure of traditional quadrotor dynamics, while capturing the complex, time-varying behavior of pneumatically actuated soft arms. In SOFA, the soft pneumatically actuated arms are discretized as a tetrahedral mesh following an elastic material law that produces internal forces adequate to the real dynamic behavior of the body. Pneumatic actuation governed by both periodic and error-based control signals is applied within the internal cavities to analyze the morphing capability. Finally, a proportional-integral controller is proposed to study the controlled dynamic behavior and morphing capabilities of the pneumatic arm, wherein the pneumatic actuation to the soft arm is controlled to achieve the desired target position. The simulation results show the effectiveness of the proposed novel modeling framework and the related controller design.

</details>


### [184] [Perception of Social Robots as Communication Partners in Healthcare for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21053)
*Hana Yamamoto,Carlotta Julia Mayer,Charlotte Raithel,Theresa Buchner,Christian Werner,Yasuhisa Hirata,Monika Eckstein,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了社交机器人能否作为老年人的有效互动伙伴，以及“积极提示”是否能增强互动。通过对35名70岁以上参与者的多模态分析（面部表情、心率变异性、主观问卷），发现人机互动与人人互动在整体压力水平上无显著差异，且机器互动时参与者心率略低，表明更放松状态。社交机器人可作为有效互动伙伴，减轻照护者负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对全球照护者短缺问题，需深入理解社交辅助机器人在人机交互中对老年人的心理和生理影响，探究社交机器人能否作为有效互动伙伴替代人类。

Method: 开展比较研究，招募35名70岁以上老年人，通过多模态分析（面部表情数据、心率变异性、主观问卷）综合评估人机互动与人人互动中老年人的压力和接受度。

Result: 人机互动与人人互动在整体压力水平上无显著差异；面部表情分析表明机器人被接受为有效互动伙伴；生理数据显示机器人互动时心率略低，提示更放松状态。

Conclusion: 社交机器人能够在不对老年人造成心理压力的情况下与之互动，并通过执行结构化任务（如健康感知调查）减轻照护者负担；未来需解决机器人设计中“外观-内容不匹配”问题以实现更自然有效的交互。

Abstract: Addressing the global caregiver shortage through socially assistive robots necessitates a deep understanding of their psychological and physiological impacts on older adults during human-robot interaction (HRI). This study addresses whether social robots can serve as effective interaction partners compared to humans, and if "positive prompts" can similarly enhance these interactions. We conducted a comparative study with 35 participants (aged 70+). Our multi-modal analysis, integrating facial expression data, heart rate variability, and subjective questionnaires, revealed no significant differences in overall stress levels between human and robot interactions. Facial expression analysis confirmed that the robot was accepted as a valid interaction partner, while physiological data showed slightly lower heart rates during robot interactions, suggesting a more relaxed state compared to human-led sessions. These findings indicate that social robots can engage older adults without inducing psychological strain and are capable of alleviating caregiver burden by performing structured tasks, such as health-sensing surveys. Future work should address the identified "appearance-content mismatch" in robot design to facilitate even more natural and effective interactions.

</details>


### [185] [Anomaly-Informed Confidence Calibration for Vision-Based Safety Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21109)
*Zhenjiang Mao,Jiawen Wu,Gabriel Wagner,Zhongzheng Zhang,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需重新训练模型的异常感知在线校准方法，通过融合世界模型中的感知分数（重建误差）和动力学分数（认知不确定性和控制流统计），利用测试时增强进行轻量级温度缩放校准，在真实的DonkeyCar平台上将平均期望校准误差从0.184降至0.116，提升37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常信号存在感知-动力学鸿沟：传统分数（如自编码器重建误差）仅捕捉视觉异常，但无法检测动力学异常（如执行偏差、延迟），而此时图像看似正常但轨迹已退化，导致预测器过度自信。

Method: 提出异常感知在线校准（Anomaly-Informed Online Calibration）：从世界模型中提取两种互补异常分数——感知分数（重建误差）和动力学分数（认知不确定性和控制流统计）；融合后通过测试时增强进行轻量级温度缩放校准，选择性降低分布偏移下的过度自信，同时保持名义条件性能。

Result: 在物理DonkeyCar平台上的四种真实世界异常协议（黑暗、模糊、执行偏差、处理延迟）下，平均期望校准误差从0.184降至0.116，相较于最佳基线提升37%。

Conclusion: 融合感知与动力学信息的异常感知校准方法有效弥补了现有异常检测的感知-动力学鸿沟，显著提升了自动驾驶赛车中基于视觉的控制器的安全部署可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable confidence estimates are important for safely deploying vision-based controllers in autonomous racing, where safety predictions must be derived from camera images, yet modern predictors become dangerously overconfident under test-time distribution shifts. We identify a critical perception-dynamics gap in existing anomaly signals: widely used scores, such as autoencoder reconstruction error, capture visual corruptions but miss dynamics anomalies (e.g., actuation bias, latency), where images remain plausible while the trajectory degrades. To address this, we propose an Anomaly-Informed Online Calibration approach that, without retraining any model component, fuses two complementary anomaly scores extracted from a world model: a perceptual score from reconstruction error and a dynamics score from epistemic uncertainty and control-stream statistics. Based on these fused scores, a lightweight temperature-scaling calibrator leverages test-time augmentation to selectively reduce overconfidence under shift while preserving nominal-condition performance. Experiments on a physical DonkeyCar under four real-world anomaly protocols unseen during training (darkness, blur, actuation bias, processing latency) reduce average expected calibration error from 0.184 to 0.116, a 37% improvement over the best baseline, without modifying the base safety predictor.

</details>


### [186] [Benchmarking Empirical and Learning-Based Approaches for Feedforward Steering Control in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21111)
*Georg Jank,Mattia Piccinini,Sebastian Wenk,Phillip Pitschi,Johannes Betz,Boris Lohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统对比了四种前馈转向控制器（两种基于学习、两种基于经验公式）在自动驾驶赛车中的应用，提出一种基于多项式曲面拟合的新EHD公式。结果表明：学习型控制器在开环预测误差最小，但闭环性能不及所提出的EHD方法，后者在鲁棒性和圈速上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈转向控制是分层控制架构减少反馈修正的关键，但现有方法缺乏系统性基准对比，特别是学习型方法与经验方法在真实闭环场景下的表现差异尚不明确。

Method: 提出一种基于多项式曲面拟合的新型EHD公式（有效捕获速度相关的非线性转向行为），并在阿布扎比自动驾驶赛车联盟的高保真双轨车辆动力学仿真环境中，对两种学习型与两种经验型前馈控制器进行开环和闭环系统评估。

Result: 开环测试中学习型控制器预测误差最低；但闭环测试中该优势并未转化为更好的路径跟踪或圈速表现。所提EHD方法在闭环鲁棒性和圈速上均最优。

Conclusion: 前馈转向策略必须在完整规划与控制软件栈中评估，单纯的开环预测精度不足以衡量实际性能；经验型EHD方法在综合表现上优于复杂的学习型方法。

Abstract: Feedforward steering control is a key component of hierarchical control architectures for autonomous racing. The goal is to reduce steering corrections from the feedback controllers by predicting the vehicle's inverse lateral dynamics. This paper presents a systematic benchmark of two learning-based and two empirical (analytical) feedforward steering controllers. We introduce a new \acf{ehd} formulation based on a polynomial surface fit that captures velocity-dependent nonlinear steering behavior with minimal parametrization. We test the feedforward controllers in a high-fidelity simulation framework based on the real-world Abu Dhabi Autonomous Racing League competition, using a high-fidelity double-track vehicle dynamics simulator. Open-loop evaluation shows that the learning-based controllers achieve the lowest prediction errors; however, closed-loop testing reveals that this improved accuracy does not translate into superior path tracking performance or lap times, even after iterative fine-tuning. In contrast, the proposed EHD approach achieves the best overall closed-loop robustness and lap time, highlighting the necessity of evaluating feedforward strategies within the complete trajectory planning and control software stack. Our code is available at https://github.com/TUMRT/steering_ff_control.

</details>


### [187] [Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation via Active Spatial Brain and Generalizable Action Cerebellum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21133)
*Zhizhao Liang,Yi-Lin Wei,Xuhang Chen,Mu Lin,Yi-Xiang He,Zhexi Luo,Jun-Hui Liu,Kun-Yu Lin,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体大模型的空间感知人形全身操作框架，包含主动空间感知与决策的"Active Spatial Brain"和无需真机数据即可生成可执行动作的"Generalizable Action Cerebellum"，在多个空间操作任务上展现出强大性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形全身操作任务面临两大挑战：1) 复杂3D环境中空间理解困难；2) 真机数据昂贵且稀缺，限制了数据驱动模型的泛化能力。现有方法难以同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出双组件框架：1) Active Spatial Brain（主动空间脑）——负责主动感知空间场景、任务规划与子任务分解；2) Generalizable Action Cerebellum（泛化动作小脑）——基于决策模块输出，无需特定任务真机数据即可生成可执行机器人动作。

Result: 在空间感知理解评估和真实机器人任务性能评估两个维度的实验中，该框架在多样化任务和环境中均表现出强性能。

Conclusion: 多智能体大模型可以有效结合空间感知与动作生成，解决人形全身操作中数据稀缺与泛化难题，验证了框架在复杂3D空间操作任务中的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore spatial-aware humanoid whole-body manipulation task. Compared with tabletop settings, this task poses two key challenges: 1) Spatial understanding is challenging in complex 3D environments with diverse spatial relations. 2) Action generation is difficult to generalize, as limited and costly real-robot data restricts data-driven models generalization. To address these challenges, we propose a generalizable humanoid loco-manipulation framework that leverages the spatial perception and action generation capabilities of multi-agent large models. Specifically, our framework includes two components: Active Spatial Brain for active spatial perception and decision-making, and Generalizable Action Cerebellum for executable robot action generation. The first component actively perceives the spatial scene and makes decisions on task planning and subtask decomposition. The second component generate executable robot actions based on the decisions made by the first module without needs of task-specific real robot data. To benchmark our framework, we design a set of spatial manipulation tasks from two perspectives: evaluating spatial perception and understanding, and assessing real-robot task performance. The results demonstrate strong performance on both aspects across diverse tasks and environments.

</details>


### [188] [Safety-Critical Control for Smoothed Implicit Contact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21138)
*Haegu Lee,Yitaek Kim,Christoffer Sloth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在平滑隐式接触动力学中通过边界聚焦滚动和鲁棒控制障碍函数来约束接触力、保障安全性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平滑隐式接触动力学虽有利于梯度规划，但安全关键控制困难，因为平滑参数κ会松弛接触互补约束，影响接触力精度，导致安全性难以保证。

Method: 提出边界聚焦滚动机制筛选κ，结合一阶泰勒近似的离散时间控制障碍函数框架，并添加鲁棒裕度补偿接触力低估问题。

Result: 在四个接触丰富系统的仿真中，该方法消除了标准CBF下观察到的接触力违反现象。

Conclusion: 本文方法有效解决了平滑接触动力学中因松弛约束导致的安全性问题，能够在不牺牲平滑性的前提下保障接触力界限。

Abstract: Smoothed implicit contact dynamics enables gradient-based planning and control for contact-rich tasks without predefined mode sequences. However, safety-critical control remains challenging because implicit contact dynamics makes safety-filter design nontrivial. The smoothing parameter $κ$ relaxes contact complementarity constraints, which makes the dynamics smooth but affects the contact force. This paper provides a method for bounding the actual contact force despite the use of relaxed complementarity constraints. We show that constraint violations can be non-monotonic in $κ$. Smaller $κ$ reduces force-approximation error, but it does not necessarily improve safety performance. To address this issue, we introduce boundary-focused rollouts to screen $κ$ by comparing the safety margin with the approximation error. We then develop a discrete-time control barrier function (CBF) framework based on a first-order Taylor approximation of the implicitly defined contact force. To account for possible force under-prediction, we augment the resulting safety constraint with a fixed robust margin. Simulations on four contact-rich systems show that the proposed method eliminates force violations observed under a standard CBF.

</details>


### [189] [EllipseLIO: Adaptive LiDAR Inertial Odometry with an Ellipsoid Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21150)
*Rowan Border,Margarita Chli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EllipseLIO，一种无需场景调参即可适应不同环境和LiDAR传感器的实时激光惯性里程计方法，在五个数据集上平均比第二名方法低38%的里程计误差，且从未发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LIO方法在特定场景下经调参表现良好，但难以在异构环境和传感器间保持鲁棒性，需要一种无需人工干预、能泛化到不同场景的LIO方法。

Method: 提出EllipseLIO，通过自适应LiDAR扫描滤波和配准方法，使其能根据传感器能力和环境特性自动调整，无需针对特定场景进行参数调优。

Result: 在五个多样化且具挑战性的数据集上实验，EllipseLIO整体表现最佳，平均里程计误差比第二名方法低38%，且是唯一在所有实验中均未发散的方法。

Conclusion: EllipseLIO通过自适应滤波和配准机制，实现了跨异构环境和传感器的高泛化能力，为无需人工调参的鲁棒LIO提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LiDAR Inertial Odometry (LIO) is a critical component for many mobile robots that need to navigate without relying on external positioning (e.g., GPS). Platforms that operate autonomously in different environments and with heterogeneous LiDAR sensors require a LIO approach that can adapt to these different scenarios without human intervention.
  Existing LIO approaches can typically provide reliable and accurate odometry in scenarios with similar environments and sensors when suitably tuned. However, many approaches struggle to retain robust odometry across heterogeneous environments and sensors while using a consistent configuration.
  This paper presents EllipseLIO, a real-time LIO approach that generalises between scenarios by using methods for LiDAR scan filtering and registration that adapt to the sensor capabilities and environment without requiring scenario-specific tuning. Experiments with EllipseLIO and state-of-the-art LIO approaches on five datasets with diverse and challenging scenarios demonstrate that EllipseLIO is the best-performing approach overall. It achieves a 38% lower odometry error on average than the second-best approach and is the only approach that does not diverge in any experiment. An open-source version of EllipseLIO will be available at github.com/v4rl-ucy/ellipselio.

</details>


### [190] [A Terrain-Adaptive epsilon-Constraint MPC for Uneven Terrain Kinodynamic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21188)
*Otobong Jerome,Geesara Kalathunga,Tiago Nascimento*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应epsilon约束MPC框架，结合半参数车辆-地形动力学模型（分析动力学+SGP），在越野地形上实现车辆的运动规划，相比基线方法导航成功率94%，最大姿态偏差降低24%，多目标权衡质量提升23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平坦地形上进行类车运动规划时，需要同时优化路径效率与姿态稳定性等相互竞争的目标，现有方法难以在实时性下有效探索Pareto前沿。

Method: 提出自适应epsilon-constraint方法集成于MPC框架中，epsilon边界根据地形容器动态调整以实时探索Pareto前沿；同时构建半参数模型，将分析车辆动力学与基于同一地形容器训练的稀疏高斯过程（SGP）相结合来刻画车辆-地形交互动力学。

Result: 与MPPI和GAKD基线相比，该方法导航成功率达94%，最大方向偏差降低24%，多目标权衡质量提升23%。

Conclusion: 自适应epsilon-MPC能有效平衡越野场景下路径效率与姿态稳定性，半参数模型为复杂地形动力学建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Kinodynamic planning for car-like vehicles on uneven terrain requires simultaneously optimizing competing objectives such as path efficiency and pose stability. This work presents an adaptive epsilon-constraint method integrated into a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework, where the epsilon bounds are dynamically adjusted based on terrain descriptors to explore the Pareto front in real time. To capture vehicle-terrain dynamics, we develop a semi-parametric model combining analytical vehicle dynamics with a Sparse Gaussian Process (SGP) trained on the same terrain descriptors. The proposed epsilon-MPC is evaluated against MPPI and GAKD baselines, achieving a 94% navigation success rate while reducing maximum orientation deviation by 24% and improving multi-objective trade-off quality by 23%.

</details>


### [191] [To Select or not to Select, that is the Question: Distilling Robot Skill Prediction into a Small Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21242)
*Haechan Mark Bong,Simon Roy,Euhid Aman,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对异构机器人集群的任务分配问题，提出一种任务到技能的预测方法。通过LLM生成合成数据集，训练了一个约1.33亿参数的小模型（mpnet+MiniLM集成），在任务-技能匹配任务上达到83.5%准确率，显著优于Kimi K2（72.0%）等大型通用大模型。结果表明，针对固定机器人技能分类，小规模专用模型结合合成数据可超越大型通用LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人集群日益异构化（包括人形机器人、轮式车、四足机器人、无人机等），如何为任务选择最合适的机器人成为一个核心系统问题。然而，缺乏将自然语言任务描述映射到机器人物理能力的标注数据。

Method: 1) 利用LLM辅助生成和标签审计构建合成任务-技能数据集；2) 训练约1.33亿参数的双编码器集成模型（mpnet + MiniLM）进行任务到技能的匹配预测。

Result: 在200个任务的测试集上，小模型达到83.5%的匹配准确率，显著优于Kimi K2（72.0%）、GPT-OSS-120B（71.5%）和Llama-4-Scout-17B（69.0%）等大型通用LLM在零样本提示下的表现。

Conclusion: 对于固定的机器人技能分类体系，基于合成数据训练的小规模专用模型在集群级任务路由任务中可以超越大型通用LLM，表明"小而专"的策略更具实际优势。

Abstract: As robot fleets become more heterogeneous, including humanoids, rovers, quadrupeds, and drones, selecting the right robot for a task becomes a core systems problem. We study robot skill prediction: mapping a natural-language task description to the physical capabilities required to execute it, such as fly, wheels, legs, surface water, under water and hands. Since labelled data that maps natural-language task descriptions to robot's physical capabilities does not exist, we construct a synthetic task-to-skill dataset using LLM-assisted generation and targeted label auditing. Trained on this data, a ~133M-parameter ensemble of two fine-tuned sentence encoders (mpnet + MiniLM) reaches 83.5% task-to-skill matching on a stratified 200 task dataset, outperforming Kimi K2 (1T MoE) at 72.0%, GPT-OSS-120B at 71.5%, and Llama-4-Scout-17B at 69.0% under the same zero-shot prompt. These results suggest that, for fixed robot skill taxonomies, small specialized models trained on synthetic data can outperform much larger general-purpose LLMs for fleet-level task routing.

</details>


### [192] [Reinforcement Learning for Risk Adaptation via Differentiable CVaR Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21257)
*Xinyi Wang,Taekyung Kim,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端风险自适应框架，结合强化学习和基于CVaR障碍函数的可微二次规划安全层，用于在障碍物运动不确定（高斯混合模型建模）的拥挤环境中实现安全高效的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在随机交互的拥挤环境中，要么过于保守，要么效率低下，难以同时兼顾安全与效率。

Method: 采用强化学习联合学习名义控制输入、风险水平与安全裕度，并通过基于条件风险价值（CVaR）障碍函数的可微二次规划安全层施加显式概率安全约束，实现端到端的风险自适应框架。

Result: 在动态、不确定、拥挤的环境中，在不同障碍物密度和机器人模型下进行广泛评估，并在三种分布外泛化测试中，相比基于优化、强化学习及两者集成的方法，该方法在安全性、效率和泛化能力上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 该方法通过上下文感知的风险自适应机制，仅在必要时才采取谨慎策略，从而在不确定性下实现了安全、高效且具泛化能力的拥挤环境导航。

Abstract: Planning through crowded environments under uncertain obstacle motions remains difficult, as stochastic interactions often induce overly conservative behavior or reduced efficiency. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end risk adaptation framework for crowd navigation under obstacle-motion uncertainty modeled by a Gaussian mixture model. The framework combines reinforcement learning~(RL) with a differentiable quadratic-program safety layer based on Conditional Value-at-Risk~(CVaR) barrier functions, jointly learning nominal control input, risk level, and safety margin and enforcing explicit probabilistic safety constraints. This design enables context-aware adaptation, promoting efficient behavior while invoking caution only when necessary. We conduct extensive evaluations in dynamic, uncertain, and crowded environments across varying obstacle densities and robot models, and further assess generalization under three out-of-distribution cases. Comparisons across optimization-based, RL-based, and integrated RL and optimization methods are provided, and the proposed method is shown to deliver the strongest overall performance in safety, efficiency, and generalization under uncertainty.

</details>


### [193] [Learning Structural Latent Points for Efficient Visual Representations in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21258)
*Yicheng Jiang,Jiaxu Wang,Junhao He,Zesen Gan,Junhao Li,Qiang Zhang,Jingkai Sun,Jiahang Cao,Mingyuan Sun,Xiangyu Yue,Qiming Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合表示（结构潜在点）的3D预训练框架，结合隐式表达的丰富性和显式几何的结构先验，通过点云变分自编码器和轻量级3DGS渲染管线，在多个仿真和真实机器人任务上提升了任务成功率、样本效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D感知预训练方法要么是全隐式神经场（缺乏显式结构线索），要么是全显式几何基元（分辨率有限、泛化弱），两者各有局限。作者希望结合二者的优势，兼顾表达力和结构性。

Method: 提出混合表示：在点云自编码器的潜空间中插入逐点变分自编码器，联合正则化点特征和坐标到高斯先验，得到紧凑的结构潜在点（保留粗粒度结构和语义信息）。同时设计了基于3DGS的轻量级渲染管线，将更多表示能力留给前端潜模块。

Result: 在RLBench、ManiSkill2和真实机器人平台上，相比强基线方法，在任务成功率、样本效率、视角和场景鲁棒性上取得一致提升。消融实验验证了每个组件的必要性。

Conclusion: 混合表示方法成功融合了隐式和显式表示的优势，轻量渲染管线的设计也验证了前端潜模块可以承担更多表示负担，为3D感知预训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Current 3D-aware pretraining methods for embodied perception and manipulation are largely built on differentiable rendering frameworks, producing either fully implicit neural fields or fully explicit geometric primitives. Implicit representations, while expressive, lack explicit structural cues, whereas explicit ones preserve geometry but suffer from resolution limits and weak generalization. To address these limitations, we propose a novel pretraining framework that learns a hybrid representation-structural latent points. Specifically, we insert a point-wise latent variational autoencoder into the latent space of a point-cloud autoencoder, jointly regularizing point-wise features and coordinates toward a Gaussian prior. The resulting compact latent preserves coarse structural tendencies, which do not encode precise geometry but capture richer rough shape and semantic information, effectively combining the expressiveness of implicit representations with the structural priors of explicit ones. In addition, informed by shared design choices in prior work, we develop a streamlined, efficient 3DGS-based rendering pipeline that is deliberately kept lightweight, improving efficiency while leaving greater representational capacity to the front-end latent module. Extensive evaluations on RLBench, ManiSkill2, and a real-robot platform demonstrate consistent gains in task success, sample efficiency, and robustness to viewpoint and scene variations over strong baselines. Ablation studies further confirm that each component of our framework is critical to overall performance.

</details>


### [194] [Learning Robust Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation from Joint Sensors with Proprioceptive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21330)
*Senlan Yao,Chenyu Yang,Jaehoon Kim,Aristotelis Sympetheros,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Proprioceptive Transformer (PT)，仅利用关节传感（位置和速度历史）实现肌腱驱动灵巧手的连续立方体旋转，无需外部视觉或触觉感知。通过教师-学生强化学习框架，PT从关节时序模式中提取隐式物体状态信息，在真实ORCA手上实现比基线快3.1倍的旋转速度，且立方体位置估计RMSE比MLP基线低23.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作研究过度依赖视觉或触觉感知来追踪物体状态，而关节传感——任何机械手都具备的最易获取的模态——被严重忽视，尤其是在肌腱驱动手上。作者试图探索仅靠关节传感能达到何种操作性能。

Method: 采用教师-学生强化学习框架：教师策略通过强化学习利用特权物体信息（如立方体姿态）进行训练；学生策略PT仅依赖关节位置和速度历史序列，通过Transformer架构从时序模式中提取隐式物体状态信息，实现无外部感知的连续物体旋转控制。

Result: 在真实ORCA肌腱驱动手上，PT实现比基线高3.1倍的旋转速度；在立方体位置估计任务中，PT的RMSE比MLP基线低23.4%，证明其从本体感觉中提取外部物体信息的能力显著优于MLP。

Conclusion: 关节传感单独即可实现具有竞争力的真实世界灵巧操作性能，Transformer架构能够有效从关节时序信号中提取隐式环境信息，为无需外部感知的低成本灵巧操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In-hand object manipulation is a fundamental yet challenging capability for dexterous robots. Despite significant progress in dexterous manipulation, existing approaches rely heavily on vision or tactile sensing to track object states, while joint sensing -- the most readily available modality on any robotic hand -- remains largely overlooked, particularly for tendon-driven hands. In this paper, we study how far joint sensing alone can go by asking: (i) whether motor encoders or direct joint sensing provides better proprioceptive feedback, (ii) how to extract environment information from joint measurements, and (iii) whether joint-only control can achieve competitive real-world performance without external perception. We present the Proprioceptive Transformer (PT), an exteroceptive-free approach for continuous cube rotation on a tendon-driven dexterous hand that uses only joint sensing feedback. A teacher policy is first trained via reinforcement learning with privileged object information, then distilled into PT, which operates solely on joint position and velocity histories. The Transformer architecture effectively extracts implicit object state information from temporal patterns in joint sensor readings. Experiments on the real ORCA hand show that our approach achieves 3.1x higher rotation speed than baselines. We also demonstrate that our PT achieves a 23.4% lower RMSE for cube position estimation than the MLP baseline, indicating superior extraction of exteroceptive information from proprioceptive sources.

</details>


### [195] [From swept contact to pose: Probe-aware registration via complementary-shape docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21398)
*Chen Chen,Yunwen Li,Yifan Xu,Xiangjie Yan,Chang Shu,Jianxia Hou,Shiji Song,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标定的接触配准方法，通过将接触配准重构为互补形状对接问题，结合3D FFT相关性与李代数连续优化，在口腔预备机器人上实现了优于光学跟踪器的配准精度（0.42 mm/3.75°）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学配准方法存在标定链长、视线受限和制造误差等问题，亟需一种无需外部传感器、不受视线约束的替代方案。

Method: 将接触配准转化为物体与探针扫掠体间的互补形状对接问题；采用全局到局部的搜索策略：基于低差异SO(3)采样的3D FFT相关性搜索，再通过李代数更新和接触灵敏度分析进行连续SE(3)精化。

Result: 仿真中自由形态网格配准精度达0.04 mm/0.4°以下；在口腔预备机器人实物实验中达到0.42 mm/3.75°精度，超越光学跟踪器。

Conclusion: 该无标定接触配准方法为手术机器人和工业机器人提供了一种实用且精确的配准策略，无需外部传感器即可实现高精度。

Abstract: Accurate registration between a prior model and the real scene is essential for high-precision robotic manipulation, yet optical methods suffer from long calibration chains, line-of-sight constraints, and fabrication errors. We propose a calibration-free alternative that reformulates contact registration as complementary-shape docking between the object and the probe's swept volume, explicitly accounting for probe geometry and leveraging both contact and non-contact evidence. Our solver integrates a global-to-local search via 3D FFT correlation over low-discrepancy SO(3) samples, then followed by continuous SE(3) refinement using Lie-algebra updates and analytic contact sensitivities. This pipeline yields efficient exploration and metric-grade convergence without fragile point correspondences. Simulation across free-form meshes achieved sub-0.04 mm and sub-0.4° accuracy and robustness to pose noise and contact loss. On a tooth-preparation robot, our method attained 0.42 mm and 3.75°, outperforming an optical tracker registration while requiring no external sensors. These results demonstrate a practical and precise registration strategy for surgical and industrial robots.

</details>


### [196] [MC-Risk: Multi-Component Risk Fields for Risk Identification and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21406)
*Maximilian Link,Yingjie Xu,Yingbai Hu,Yinlong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MC-Risk：一个面向规划器、多组分的鸟瞰图风险场，能够实现早期、校准且类别感知的风险定位，在RiskBench碰撞子集上取得了最优风险定位和最早危险指示，并可作为MPC成本密度实现无需额外训练的风险感知轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险场方法缺乏标准化定量评估，且未能同时满足早期风险定位、校准性、类别感知以及可解释性的需求。

Method: 将三个可解释模块线性组合：1）基于多模态轨迹预测器与高斯环形解析结构的机动化Agent场；2）基于前向偏置各向异性核的VRU风险场；3）利用高精地图拓扑的路面惩罚场。

Result: 在RiskBench碰撞子集上进行了首个标准化定量评估，取得了最优整体风险定位和最早危险指示。

Conclusion: MC-Risk可作为即插即用的规划接口，以MPC成本密度形式实现无需额外训练的风险感知轨迹生成。

Abstract: We present MC-Risk, a planner-aligned, multi-component risk field on a bird's-eye-view grid that yields early, calibrated, and class-aware risk localization. MC-Risk linearly composes three interpretable modules: (i) a motorized-agent field that fuses a black-box multimodal trajectory predictor with an analytic Gaussian-torus construction whose lateral width grows with speed/curvature and whose height attenuates with look-ahead; (ii) a VRU risk field that replaces isotropic pedestrian blobs with a forward-biased anisotropic kernel aligned to heading and speed; and (iii) a road penalty field that exploits full HD-map topology, imposing an off-road penalty and lane-aware risk exposure for same/opposite directions. We conduct, to our knowledge, the first standardized quantitative evaluation of a risk-field formulation on RiskBench's collision subset. MC-Risk attains the best overall risk localization and the earliest hazard indication. Finally, we demonstrate a plug-and-play planning interface by using the field as an MPC cost density, enabling risk-aware trajectory generation without additional training.

</details>


### [197] [PointACT: Vision-Language-Action Models with Multi-Scale Point-Action Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21414)
*Shizhe Chen,Paul Pacaud,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PointACT，一种融合分层3D点云表示的3D感知VLA策略，通过在动作解码中引入多尺度点-动作交互机制，在LIBERO和RLBench基准上显著提升机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖2D视觉表示，缺乏对精细几何和三维空间推理的能力，限制了在3D环境中的精确鲁棒操作。

Method: 提出PointACT双系统3D感知VLA策略，包含多尺度点-动作交互机制和高效瓶颈窗口自注意力，将分层3D点云表示直接集成到动作解码过程。

Result: 在RLBench-10Tasks上较现有SOTA预训练VLA提升10%成功率，在冻结视觉语言骨干时效果更显著。

Conclusion: 紧密耦合分层3D几何与预训练2D语义表示对鲁棒的空间接地机器人控制至关重要，预训练3D表示在3D感知VLA策略中具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation by leveraging large pretrained vision-language backbones. However, most existing VLAs rely primarily on 2D visual representations, which limit their ability to reason about fine-grained geometry and spatial grounding - capabilities that are essential for precise and robust manipulation in 3D environments. In this paper, we propose PointACT, a dual-system 3D-aware VLA policy that integrates hierarchical 3D point cloud representations directly into the action decoding process. PointACT employs a multi-scale point-action interaction mechanism with efficient bottleneck window self-attention, enabling evolving action tokens to densely attend to both local geometric detail and global scene structure. We evaluate PointACT on the LIBERO and RLBench benchmarks and systematically compare it against monolithic and dual-system VLA baselines, including variants augmented with point cloud inputs. PointACT achieves consistent improvements across both benchmarks, increasing success rates by 10% on the challenging RLBench-10Tasks suite over state-of-the-art pretrained VLAs, with even larger gains when the vision-language backbone is frozen and the action expert is trained from scratch. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate that tightly coupling hierarchical 3D geometry with pretrained 2D semantic representations is critical for robust and spatially grounded robot control. Our results also highlight the promise of pretrained 3D representations for 3D-aware VLA policies.

</details>


### [198] [roto 2.0: The Robot Tactile Olympiad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21429)
*Elle Miller,Jayaram Reddy,Ayush Deshmukh,Trevor McInroe,David Abel,Oisin Mac Aodha,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Robot Tactile Olympiad v2 (roto 2.0)，一个基于GPU并行化的触觉强化学习基准测试平台，该平台在四种不同机器人形态上实现了仅依靠本体感觉和触觉的“盲操作”，在Baoding球旋转任务上达到了比现有最先进水平快一个数量级的速度（10秒13圈）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前触觉强化学习研究存在碎片化严重、过度关注过饱和的定向任务等问题，缺乏标准化的基准测试平台来推动该领域发展。

Method: 提出了roto 2.0基准测试平台，采用GPU并行化架构，支持四种不同自由度（16-DOF到24-DOF）的机器人形态；采用端到端的“盲操作”方法，仅使用本体感觉和触觉感知，不依赖状态信息或知识蒸馏。

Result: 盲操作智能体在10秒内完成13次Baoding球旋转，比现有最先进技术水平快一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过开源环境和完善的调优基线，降低了触觉强化学习的研究门槛，使研究者能专注于核心算法挑战而非繁琐的RL调参工作。

Abstract: Tactile-based reinforcement learning (RL) is currently hindered by fragmented research and a focus on over-saturated orientation tasks. We introduce v2 of the Robot Tactile Olympiad (\texttt{roto 2.0}), a GPU-parallelised benchmark designed to standardise tactile-based RL across four distinct robotic morphologies (16-DOF to 24-DOF). Unlike prior benchmarks, roto focuses on end-to-end "blind" manipulation, utilising only proprioception and tactile sensing without state information or distillation. We demonstrate a significant performance leap, with our blind agents achieving 13 Baoding ball rotations in 10 seconds, an order of magnitude faster than current state-of-the-art speeds. By open-sourcing our environments and robustly tuned baselines, we reduce the barrier to entry and enable researchers to prioritise fundamental algorithmic challenges over tedious RL tuning. Website: https://elle-miller.github.io/roto/

</details>


### [199] [Lost in Fog: Sensor Perturbations Expose Reasoning Fragility in Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21446)
*Abhinaw Priyadershi,Jelena Frtunikj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶VLA模型在传感器退化下的鲁棒性，发现因果链推理一致性可作为轨迹可靠性的量化代理指标。（CoC变化时轨迹偏差放大5.3倍，r=0.99）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释自动驾驶规划器不仅需要生成解释，还需确保这些解释在真实世界的传感器退化条件下保持可靠。现有研究缺乏对VLA模型在传感器扰动下鲁棒性的系统评估。

Method: 对Alpamayo R1（10B参数）模型进行受控扰动研究，在1,996个场景中施加8种传感器扰动（高斯噪声4种强度、2种光照极端、2种雾度），共约18,000次推理试验，分析CoC推理一致性、轨迹偏差等指标。

Result: CoC变化时轨迹偏差激增5.3倍（21.8m vs 4.1m），跨攻击类型相关性r=0.99，每样本r_pb=0.53（Cohen's d=1.12）；CoC生成使轨迹精度平均提升11.8%（p<0.0001）；退化呈近似线性（R²=0.957），标准预处理防御效果有限。

Conclusion: CoC一致性可作为规划安全的定量代理指标，为基于推理的运行时监控提供依据，助力VLA模型更安全部署。

Abstract: Interpretable autonomous driving planners depend not only on generating explanations, but also on those explanations remaining reliable under real-world sensor degradation. In this paper we present a controlled perturbation study of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) robustness in autonomous driving, evaluating Alpamayo R1 (10B parameters) across 1,996 scenarios under eight sensor perturbations (Gaussian noise at four intensities, two lighting extremes, and two fog levels; ${\sim}18{,}000$ inference trials). We find that reasoning consistency is a high-fidelity indicator of trajectory reliability: when Chain-of-Causation (CoC) explanations change after perturbation, trajectory deviation spikes $5.3{\times}$ (21.8m vs 4.1m), with $r\!=\!0.99$ across attack types and $r_{pb}\!=\!0.53$ per-sample (Cohen's $d\!=\!1.12$). A controlled ablation provides evidence that enabling CoC generation is associated with improved trajectory accuracy (11.8% on average across conditions; $p < 0.0001$) under matched inference settings. Over the tested noise range ($σ\in \{10, 30, 50, 70\}$), degradation is approximately linear ($R^2\!=\!0.957$), while standard input preprocessing defenses provide only marginal relief. Together, these results establish CoC consistency as a quantitative proxy for planning safety and motivate reasoning-based runtime monitoring for safer VLA deployment.

</details>


### [200] [HITL-D: Human In The Loop Diffusion Assisted Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21460)
*Riley Zilka,Sergey Khlynovskiy,Allie Wang,Martin Jagersand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Human-In-The-Loop Diffusion (HITL-D)共享控制框架，结合扩散策略与人类控制，在多步任务中减少40%完成任务时间、降低37%感知工作负荷，并提升独立性、直观性和信心评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前自主操控系统能力显著，但人类专业知识与基于扩散策略的共享控制之间的整合仍相对未被探索，存在如何降低用户心理负担、提升精细操控任务效率的需求。

Method: 提出HITL-D框架，结合扩散策略与人类控制，以场景点云和末端执行器笛卡尔位置为条件，提供自主的末端执行器方向更新，减少所需的摇杆控制轴数量。

Result: 12人用户研究显示：平均任务完成时间减少40%，感知工作负荷降低37%，独立性、直观性和信心等李克特量表评分均优于传统遥操作方法。

Conclusion: HITL-D能有效整合人类专业知识与自主辅助，在遥操作的客观指标和主观体验上均有显著提升。

Abstract: Autonomous manipulation systems have achieved remarkable capabilities, yet the integration of human expertise with diffusion-based policies in shared control remains relatively unexplored. In this paper, we propose Human-In-The-Loop Diffusion (HITL-D), a shared control framework that enhances user performance in multi-step, insertion, and fine manipulation tasks. HITL-D leverages a novel combination of diffusion-based policies and human control to provide autonomous end effector orientation updates conditioned on a scene point cloud and the Cartesian position of the end effector. This approach reduces the number of joystick control axes required, thereby lowering mental workload. In a multi-task user study with 12 participants, HITL-D reduced average task completion times by 40%, decreased perceived workload by 37%, and improved Likert-scale ratings for independence, intuitiveness, and confidence compared to traditional teleoperation methods. These results demonstrate that HITL-D effectively integrates human expertise with autonomous assistance, improving both objective and subjective aspects of teleoperation.

</details>


### [201] [DISC: Decoupling Instruction from State-Conditioned Control via Policy Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20856)
*Hanxiang Ren,Pei Zhou,Xunzhe Zhou,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DISC提出一种基于超网络的语言条件操控策略，通过从指令直接生成任务特定策略参数而非共享网络参数，从根本上消除视觉捷径（观测泄露）问题，在LIBERO-90和Meta-World上超越所有基线方法，甚至超过大规模预训练模型π₀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件操控策略中，语言指令和观测通过共享网络参数处理，导致任务-状态纠缠——网络学会场景到动作的捷径，绕过语言理解。这种"观测泄露"使模型依赖视觉线索而非真正遵循语言指令，特别是在视觉场景高度相似的现实场景中问题突出。

Method: DISC采用两阶段超网络架构：第一阶段从语言指令生成完整策略网络参数，第二阶段以梯度优化的结构作为前馈归纳偏置来精炼参数，无需实际梯度计算即可生成全局一致的参数。生成的策略网络完全不直接接触语言输入，因此必须通过语言本身获取任务感知能力。

Result: 在LIBERO-90和Meta-World上超越所有纠缠基线方法，在复杂长时任务上优势更大；不依赖任何外部预训练数据就超越了大规模预训练模型π₀；在视觉上下文完全相同的真实世界基准任务上，大幅优于纠缠方法；超网络学会语义结构化参数流形，支持少量演示的少样本适应和释义指令的鲁棒泛化。

Conclusion: DISC证明了语言生成的策略参数而非视觉捷径驱动行为的有效性，为语言条件操控策略提供了一种消除观测泄露的结构性方案，同时展示了超网络参数生成方法的强大潜力。

Abstract: Language-conditioned manipulation policies typically process instructions and observations through shared network parameters. This task-state entanglement provides a pathway for observation leakage -- networks learn scene-to-action shortcuts that bypass language grounding entirely. DISC eliminates this failure structurally. Rather than conditioning a universal policy on language, DISC uses a hypernetwork to generate the entire parameter set of a task-specific visuomotor policy from the instruction alone. The generated policy never directly accesses language; therefore, its task-awareness must come from the language. Consequently, observation leakage has no pathway to emerge. On the other hand, generating coherent high-dimensional policy weights is itself a challenging problem. We address it with a two-stage hypernetwork whose refinement stage embeds the structure of gradient-based optimization as a feed-forward inductive bias, producing globally consistent parameters without actual gradient computation. Trained entirely from scratch on standard data budgets, DISC outperforms all entangled baselines on LIBERO-90 and Meta-World, with advantages that widen on complex, long-horizon tasks -- and surpasses the large-scale pretrained $π_0$ despite using no external pretraining data. On a real-world benchmark where all tasks share identical visual context, DISC substantially outperforms entangled alternatives, directly confirming that language-generated policy parameters, not visual shortcuts, drive behavior. The hypernetwork further learns a semantically structured parameter manifold that enables few-shot adaptation from minimal demonstrations and robust generalization across paraphrased instructions. Our code is available at: {https://github.com/ReNginx/DISC}.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [SOLAR: A Self-Optimizing Open-Ended Autonomous Agent for Lifelong Learning and Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20189)
*Nitin Vetcha,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SOLAR——一个基于参数级元学习和多级强化学习的自主推理代理，能够在流式数据中自我优化、持续适应新领域，无需梯度微调，在多个推理任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在动态、真实世界部署中面临概念漂移和高成本梯度适应两大瓶颈，传统微调无法有效适应非平稳数据流，且存在灾难性遗忘或需要大量人工数据标注的问题。

Method: 将模型权重视为探索环境，使用参数级元学习；通过多级强化学习自主发现适应策略；维护演化的知识库作为情景记忆缓冲，平衡可塑性与稳定性。

Result: 在常识、数学、医学、编程、社会与逻辑推理任务上均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: SOLAR代表了向具备终身适应能力的自主代理迈出的重要一步，能够在动态变化环境中持续自我进化。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of large language models (LLMs), they still face bottlenecks while deploying in dynamic, real-world settings with primary challenges being concept drift and the high cost of gradient-based adaptation. Traditional fine-tuning (FT) struggles to adapt to non-stationary data streams without resulting in catastrophic for getting or requiring extensive manual data curation. To address these limitations within the streaming and continual learning paradigm, we propose the Self-Optimizing Lifelong Autonomous Reasoner (SOLAR) which is an open-ended autonomous agent that leverages parameter-level meta-learning to self-improve, treating model weights as an environment for exploration. It initiates the process by consolidating a strong prior over common-sense knowledge making it effective for transfer-learning. By utilizing a multi-level reinforcement learning approach, SOLAR autonomously discovers adaptation strategies, enabling efficient test-time adaptation to unseen domains. Crucially, SOLAR maintains an evolving knowledge base of valid modification strategies, implicitly acting as an episodic memory buffer to balance plasticity (adaptation to new tasks) and stability (retention of meta-knowledge). Experiments demonstrate that SOLAR outperforms strong baselines on common-sense, mathematical, medical, coding, social and logical reasoning tasks, marking a significant step toward autonomous agents capable of lifelong adaptation in evolving environments.

</details>


### [203] [Tool-Augmented Agent for Closed-loop Optimization,Simulation,and Modeling Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20190)
*Liyuan Deng,Shujian Deng,Yongkang Chen,Yongkang Dai,Zhihang Zhong,Linyang Li,Xiao Sun,Yilei Shi,Huaxi Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出COSMO-Agent框架，通过工具增强的强化学习训练小模型，使其能自主完成CAD-CAE闭环优化，在可行性、效率上超越大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAD-CAE之间存在语义鸿沟，仿真反馈难以自动转化为符合多约束的几何编辑，导致迭代设计优化效率低下。

Method: 将CAD生成、CAE求解、结果解析和几何修改构建为交互式RL环境，LLM学习调用外部工具；并设计多约束奖励函数（可行性、工具链鲁棒性、输出有效性），配合工业级数据集训练。

Result: 训练后的小型开源LLM在约束驱动设计任务上，可行性和稳定性超过大型开源模型甚至强闭源模型。

Conclusion: COSMO-Agent方法有效弥合了CAD-CAE语义鸿沟，证明通过RL训练小模型也能在工业设计优化中取得优异表现。

Abstract: Iterative industrial design-simulation optimization is bottlenecked by the CAD-CAE semantic gap: translating simulation feedback into valid geometric edits under diverse, coupled constraints. To fill this gap, we propose COSMO-Agent (Closed-loop Optimization, Simulation, and Modeling Orchestration), a tool-augmented reinforcement learning (RL) framework that teaches LLMs to complete the closed-loop CAD-CAE process. Specifically, we cast CAD generation, CAE solving, result parsing, and geometry revision as an interactive RL environment, where an LLM learns to orchestrate external tools and revise parametric geometries until constraints are satisfied. To make this learning stable and industrially usable, we design a multi-constraint reward that jointly encourages feasibility, toolchain robustness, and structured output validity. In addition, we contribute an industry-aligned dataset that covers 25 component categories with executable CAD-CAE tasks to support realistic training and evaluation. Experiments show that COSMO-Agent training substantially improves small open-source LLMs for constraint-driven design, exceeding large open-source and strong closed-source models in feasibility, efficiency, and stability.

</details>


### [204] [OSCToM: RL-Guided Adversarial Generation for High-Order Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20423)
*Sharmin Sultana Srishty,Kazi Mahathir Rahman,Malaika Parizat Sakkhi,Samia Shahid Prianna,Shaikhul Islam Sinat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSCToM提出了一种基于强化学习、扩展领域语言和组合代理模型的方法，用于生成观察者-自我信念冲突，以提升LLM在复杂社交场景中的心智理论（ToM）推理能力。OSCToM-8B在多个ToM基准上表现优异，尤其在信息不对称的FANToM上达到76%准确率（相比ExploreToM的0.2%），且数据合成效率提升6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在复杂社交场景下的ToM推理能力仍不均衡，现有基准（如ExploreToM）未能充分测试递归信念和信息不对称带来的挑战。作者希望建模观察者信念与自我信念之间的嵌套冲突，从而推动LLM在更真实的社交推理场景中的表现。

Method: OSCToM结合强化学习（RL）、扩展的领域特定语言（DSL）和组合代理模型（compositional surrogate models），用于生成观察者-自我冲突（observer-self conflicts）的训练数据。该方法聚焦于观察者对他人信念的判断与自身信念存在冲突的情形，要求模型进行递归、多层次的推理。

Result: OSCToM-8B在FANToM、Hi-ToM和BigToM上均表现优异，尤其在信息不对称的FANToM上达到76%准确率，远超ExploreToM的0.2%。数据合成效率提升6倍，表明定向训练数据能帮助小模型处理高级认知推理任务。

Conclusion: 通过针对性地生成观察者-自我冲突数据，OSCToM有效提升了LLM在复杂社交推理场景中的ToM能力，证明了数据质量与效率对提升模型认知推理的重要性，为未来小模型处理高级认知任务提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform well on many language tasks, but their Theory of Mind (ToM) reasoning is still uneven in complex social settings. Existing benchmarks, including ExploreToM, do not always test the recursive beliefs and information asymmetries that make these settings difficult. This paper presents OSCToM (Observer-Self Conflict Theory of Mind), an approach for modeling nested belief conflicts in LLM-based ToM tasks. The key case is one in which an observer's view of another agent conflicts with the observer's own belief state. Such cases go beyond simple perspective-taking and require recursive, multi-layered reasoning. OSCToM combines reinforcement learning (RL), an extended domain-specific language, and compositional surrogate models to generate observer-self conflicts. In our experiments, OSCToM-8B gives the best overall result among the systems tested. It improves on the reported ExploreToM results on FANToM and remains competitive on Hi-ToM and BigToM. On the information-asymmetric FANToM benchmark, OSCToM reaches 76% accuracy, compared with the 0.2% reported by ExploreToM. The data-synthesis procedure is also 6x more efficient, indicating that targeted training data can help smaller models handle advanced cognitive reasoning. The project code is available at https://github.com/sharminsrishty/osct.

</details>


### [205] [AgentCo-op: Retrieval-Based Synthesis of Interoperable Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20425)
*Shuaike Shen,Wenduo Cheng,Shike Wang,Mingqian Ma,Jian Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentCo-op 提出了一种基于检索的合成框架，通过类型化的工件传递组合可复用的技能、工具和外部智能体，并在执行失败时对相关组件进行局部修复，从而在开放科学场景下自动构建可执行的多智能体工作流。在基因组学案例和多个基准测试中，该方法展现了优于传统多智能体基线的性能和成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放的科学环境中，多智能体工作流的设计面临三大挑战：缺乏精心设计的训练集、可靠的标量评估指标，以及现有工具与智能体之间缺乏标准化接口。现有方法依赖全局拓扑搜索或基准优化，难以适应开放世界场景。

Method: AgentCo-op 包含三个核心环节：(1) 通过检索合成，将可复用的技能、工具和外部智能体组合成工作流，并利用类型化工件进行传递；(2) 执行过程中若出现失败证据，对有问题的组件进行有界自引导局部修复；(3) 可将搜索到的工作流作为结构先验导入，通过检索组件进行节点接地和局部修复来改进。

Result: 在空间转录组学和单细胞多组学两个开放世界基因组学案例中成功构建可审计工作流；在六个编码、数学和问答基准上，AgentCo-op 在四个基准上取得最佳结果，平均得分最高，且持续降低了每任务成本。

Conclusion: 基于检索的合成能够将自动化智能体工作流设计从基准优化扩展至开放世界场景，且合成与搜索具有互补性，为构建由现有智能体、工具和类型化工件组成的开放世界工作流提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Designing multi-agent workflows is especially difficult in open-ended scientific settings where tasks lack curated training sets, reliable scalar evaluation metrics, and standardized interfaces between existing tools and agents. We propose AgentCo-op, a retrieval-based synthesis framework that composes reusable skills, tools, and external agents into executable workflows through typed artifact handoffs, then applies bounded self-guided local repair to implicated components when execution evidence indicates failure. In two open-world genomics case studies, AgentCo-op composes independently developed scientific agents and external tool repositories into auditable workflows without redesigning them or running global topology search. It coordinates specialized agents for spatial transcriptomics and gene-set interpretation to enable collaborative discovery from spatial transcriptomics data, and builds a parallel workflow for cross-modality marker analysis on single-cell multiome data. AgentCo-op can also import a searched workflow as a structural prior and improve it by grounding nodes with retrieved components and applying local repair, showing that synthesis and search are complementary. On six coding, math, and question-answering benchmarks, AgentCo-op achieves the best result on four benchmarks and the best average score under a unified backbone setting, while consistently reducing per-task cost relative to multi-agent baselines. Together, these results suggest that retrieval-based synthesis can extend automated agentic workflow design beyond benchmark-optimized agent graphs to open-world workflows built from existing agents, tools, and typed artifacts.

</details>


### [206] [High Quality Embeddings for Horn Logic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20467)
*Yifan Zhang,Yasir White,Dean Clark,Joseph Sanchez,Jevon Lipsey,Ashely Hirst,Jeff Heflin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三种改进嵌入训练的方法（重复项锚点生成、平衡难度样本、周期性强调难例），以提升逻辑推理中神经网络的排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练神经网络来排序逻辑推理器的选择，需要有效的逻辑语句数值表示（嵌入），但目前缺乏生成高质量嵌入的系统性方法。

Method: 1) 生成包含更多重复项的锚点；2) 在正负样本中保持简单/中等/困难样本的平衡；3) 训练中周期性强调最难的样本。

Result: 通过实验比较了不同知识库上的嵌入效果，验证了所提方法能提升下游推理任务性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的三元组损失训练策略能有效改进逻辑推理嵌入的质量，且不同知识库下嵌入特性与推理任务之间存在关联。

Abstract: Neural networks can be trained to rank the choices made by logical reasoners, resulting in more efficient searches for answers. A key step in this process is creating useful embeddings, i.e., numeric representations of logical statements. This paper introduces and evaluates several approaches to creating embeddings that result in better downstream results. We train embeddings using triplet loss, which requires examples consisting of an anchor, a positive example, and a negative example. We introduce three ideas: generating anchors that are more likely to have repeated terms, generating positive and negative examples in a way that ensures a good balance between easy, medium, and hard examples, and periodically emphasizing the hardest examples during training. We conduct several experiments to evaluate this approach, including a comparison of different embeddings across different knowledge bases, in an attempt to identify what characteristics make an embedding well-suited to a particular reasoning task.

</details>


### [207] [\ECUAS{n}: A family of metrics for principled evaluation of uncertainty-augmented systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20490)
*Lautaro Estienne,Erik Ernst,Matías Vera,Pablo Piantanida,Luciana Ferrer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出现有不确定性增强系统评估方法存在缺陷，提出了一种新的指标家族ECUAS(n)，通过参数n控制错误预测与不完美不确定性之间的权衡，并在分类和生成数据集上验证了其理论优势和实证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对不确定性增强系统的评估方式不恰当，无法有效衡量系统在不确定性下的决策性能。

Method: 提出ECUAS(n)指标家族，将不确定性评分融入任务相关的适当评分规则中，通过参数n调节错误预测成本与不确定性质量之间的权重。

Result: 在分类和生成数据集（含TriviaQA人工标注子集）上的实验验证了该指标的理论优势与实证优越性。

Conclusion: ECUAS(n)为不确定性增强系统提供了更合理的整体性能评估框架。

Abstract: In high-stakes automated decision-making, access to predictive uncertainty is essential for enabling users -- human or downstream systems -- to accept or reject predictions based on application-specific cost trade-offs. Such uncertainty-augmented (UA) systems -- i.e., systems that output both predictions and uncertainty scores -- are currently being assessed in the literature in a variety of ways, using separate metrics to evaluate the predictions and the uncertainty scores, setting a cost function with a fixed rejection cost or integrating over a coverage-risk curve. We argue that these evaluation approaches are inadequate for assessing overall performance of the UA system for decision making under uncertainty and propose a novel family of metrics, \ECUAS{n}, formulated as proper scoring rules for the task of interest. The parameter $n$ controls the trade-off between the cost of incorrect predictions and imperfect uncertainties depending on the needs of the use-case. We demonstrate the advantages of the \ECUAS{n} metrics both theoretically and empirically, through experiments on diverse classification and generation datasets, including a manually annotated subset of TriviaQA.

</details>


### [208] [Open-World Evaluations for Measuring Frontier AI Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20520)
*Sayash Kapoor,Peter Kirgis,Andrew Schwartz,Stephan Rabanser,J. J. Allaire,Rishi Bommasani,Harry Coppock,Magda Dubois,Gillian K Hadfield,Andrew B. Hall,Sara Hooker,Seth Lazar,Steve Newman,Dimitris Papailiopoulos,Shoshannah Tekofsky,Helen Toner,Cozmin Ududec,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“开放世界评估”作为传统基准测试的补充方法，通过长时间、真实世界的任务进行小样本定性分析，并以AI代理独立开发并发布iOS应用到App Store为例，展示了该方法能预警即将普及的AI能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试在评估前沿AI能力时存在局限性：它偏向于可精确定义、自动评分、易优化、低预算和短周期的任务，因此既可能高估也可能低估AI在现实部署中的能力。需要一种互补的评估范式。

Method: 作者提出“开放世界评估”方法，其核心是选择长时间、复杂、真实的开放任务，通过小样本定性分析（而非大规模自动化基准测试）进行评估。作为实例，他们让AI代理完成开发并发布一个简单的iOS应用到苹果App Store的全流程任务。

Result: AI代理成功完成了从开发到发布iOS应用的全部任务，过程中仅需一次可避免的人工干预。这表明开放世界评估能够提供关于AI能力即将普及的早期预警信号。

Conclusion: 开放世界评估是对传统基准测试的重要补充，能够更真实地反映AI在现实场景中的能力。作者建议未来应更系统地设计和报告此类评估，并介绍了CRUX项目以定期开展相关工作。

Abstract: Benchmark-based evaluation remains important for tracking frontier AI progress. But it can both overstate and understate deployed capability because it privileges tasks that can be precisely specified, automatically graded, easy to optimize for, and run with low budgets and short time horizons. We advocate for a complementary class of evaluations, which we term open-world evaluations: long-horizon, messy, real-world tasks assessed through small-sample qualitative analysis rather than benchmark-scale automation. In this paper we survey recent open-world evaluations, identify their strengths and limitations, and introduce CRUX (Collaborative Research for Updating AI eXpectations), a project for conducting such evaluations regularly. As a first instance, we task an AI agent with developing and publishing a simple iOS application to the Apple App Store. The agent completed the task with only a single avoidable manual intervention, suggesting that open-world evaluations can provide early warning of capabilities that may soon become widespread. We conclude with recommendations for designing and reporting open-world evals.

</details>


### [209] [AgentAtlas: Beyond Outcome Leaderboards for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20530)
*Parsa Mazaheri,Kasra Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentAtlas提出了一个用于评估可部署大语言模型智能体的多维分析框架，包含六状态控制决策分类、九类轨迹失败分类、分类感知与盲测方法论，以及基准覆盖审计。实验表明，去除显式标签菜单后，所有模型的轨迹准确率下降14-40个百分点，收敛到0.54-0.62的低位，且没有模型在所有维度上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体评估基准碎片化，各自侧重不同的评估指标（如任务成功率、工具调用有效性、一致性、安全性、鲁棒性），单一的准确率指标不足以衡量可部署智能体的真实能力。

Method: (i) 六状态控制决策分类（Act/Ask/Refuse/Stop/Confirm/Recover）；(ii) 九类轨迹失败分类（含两个正交层次标签：主要错误来源、影响）；(iii) 分类感知与分类盲测方法（衡量模型能力中来自提示中监督信息的比例）；(iv) 基准覆盖审计（映射15个智能体基准到6个行为轴）。

Result: 在8个模型（4个闭源前沿+4个开源）的1342个样本上，去除显式标签菜单后，所有模型的轨迹准确率下降14-40个百分点，收敛到0.54-0.62的狭小区间；没有模型在控制准确率、轨迹诊断和工具上下文效用保持这三个维度上同时表现最佳。

Conclusion: 单一的准确率指标不足以评估可部署智能体，需要多维度的分析框架；当前模型对提示中显式监督信息依赖严重，且不同模型在不同能力维度上各有优劣。

Abstract: Large language model agents now act on codebases, browsers, operating systems, calendars, files, and tool ecosystems, but the benchmarks used to evaluate them are fragmented: each emphasizes a different unit of measurement (final task success, tool-call validity, repeated-pass consistency, trajectory safety, or attack robustness). A line of 2024-2025 work has converged on the diagnosis that a single accuracy column is no longer the right unit of comparison for deployable agents. AgentAtlas extends this line of work with four components: (i) a six-state control-decision taxonomy (Act / Ask / Refuse / Stop / Confirm / Recover); (ii) a nine-category trajectory-failure taxonomy with two orthogonal hierarchical labels (primary_error_source, impact); (iii) a taxonomy-aware vs. taxonomy-blind methodology that measures how much of a model's apparent capability comes from the supervision in the prompt; and (iv) a benchmark-coverage audit mapping fifteen agent benchmarks against six behavioral axes. To demonstrate the methodology we run a small fixed eight-model set (1,342 generated items, four frontier closed and four open-weight) under both prompt modes. Removing the explicit label menu drops every model's trajectory accuracy by 14-40 pp to a tight 0.54-0.62 floor regardless of family, and no single model wins on all three of control accuracy, trajectory diagnosis, and tool-context utility retention. We treat the synthetic run as a measurement-protocol demonstration, not a benchmark release.

</details>


### [210] [Personality Engineering with AI Agents: A New Methodology for Negotiation Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20554)
*Michelle A. Vaccaro,Jared R. Curhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“人格工程”方法论，利用AI代理精确参数化、操纵和评估谈判者人格，以人际环状模型（温暖与支配两个核心维度）为基础坐标体系，为谈判理论提供严格测试方法并指导AI谈判代理的人格设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谈判理论强调平衡共情与坚持、关心他人与关心自我等矛盾需求，但人类难以控制这些张力，导致无法在受控条件下严格检验理论预测。AI代理的精确性、多样性、一致性和可扩展性为克服这一局限提供了新可能。

Method: 提出人格工程（Personality Engineering）方法论：利用AI代理对谈判者人格进行精确参数化、操纵和评估。以人际环状模型（Interpersonal Circumplex）的温暖（warmth）和支配（dominance）两个核心维度作为基础坐标系统。

Result: 该方法论能够严格测试经典谈判理论，并为AI谈判代理的人格设计提供实用指南。

Conclusion: 人格工程为谈判研究开辟了新实验范式，既解决了传统研究难以严格检验理论的问题，又为实际设计AI谈判代理提供了可操作框架。

Abstract: According to canonical negotiation theory, people's success in a negotiation depends on how well they balance competing demands--empathizing and asserting, demonstrating concern for other and concern for self, being soft on the people and hard on the problem. Yet people struggle to manage these tensions, so researchers have lacked the ability to rigorously test the field's prescriptions under controlled conditions. AI agents do not face the same limitations, and their precision, repertoire, consistency, and scalability enable a new class of experiments to contribute to negotiation theory. In this article, we introduce personality engineering: a methodology that uses AI agents to precisely parameterize, manipulate, and evaluate negotiator personality. We propose using the interpersonal circumplex--and its two core dimensions of warmth and dominance--as a foundational coordinate system for the field. This approach offers both a rigorous methodology for testing classic negotiation theories and a practical guide for designing the personalities of AI negotiation agents.

</details>


### [211] [Mahjax: A GPU-Accelerated Mahjong Simulator for Reinforcement Learning in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20577)
*Soichiro Nishimori,Shinri Okano,Keigo Habara,Sotetsu Koyamada,Eason Yu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Mahjax，一个基于 JAX 的完全向量化立直麻将环境，支持 GPU 大规模并行 rollout，吞吐量可达每秒 200 万步，并验证了其在强化学习中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立直麻将具有随机性、高维状态空间和不完美信息等挑战，类似复杂现实决策问题。以往研究依赖人类对局日志进行监督预训练，而从头学习的算法（如 AlphaZero 系列）更具通用潜力，因此需要一个能支持大规模并行训练的高效环境。

Method: 提出 Mahjax——基于 JAX 的完全向量化立直麻将环境，利用 GPU 实现大规模 rollout 并行化；同时提供高质量可视化工具辅助调试与交互。

Result: 在 8 块 NVIDIA A100 GPU 上，无赤牌规则下达到每秒 200 万步、有赤牌规则下达到每秒 100 万步的吞吐量；且在该环境中训练的智能体能有效提升对战基线策略的排名。

Conclusion: Mahjax 为立直麻将的 tabula rasa 强化学习研究提供了高效、可扩展的基础设施，验证了其在大规模并行训练中的实用价值。

Abstract: Riichi Mahjong is a multi-player, imperfect-information game characterized by stochasticity and high-dimensional state spaces. These attributes present a unique combination of challenges that mirror complex real-world decision-making problems in reinforcement learning. While prior research has heavily relied on supervised learning from human play logs to pre-train the policy, algorithms capable of learning \textit{tabula rasa} (from scratch) offer greater potential for general applicability, as evidenced by the AlphaZero lineage. To facilitate such research, we introduce \textbf{Mahjax}, a fully vectorized Riichi Mahjong environment implemented in JAX to enable large-scale rollout parallelization on Graphics Processing Units (GPUs). We also provide a high-quality visualization tool to streamline debugging and interaction with trained agents. Experimental results demonstrate that Mahjax achieves throughputs of up to \textbf{2 million} and \textbf{1 million steps per second} on eight NVIDIA A100 GPUs under the no-red and red rules, respectively. Furthermore, we validate the environment's utility for reinforcement learning by showing that agents can be trained effectively to improve their rank against baseline policies.

</details>


### [212] [From Automated to Autonomous: Hierarchical Agent-native Network Architecture (HANA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20608)
*Binghan Wu,Shoufeng Wang,Yunxin Liu,Ya-Qin Zhang,Joseph Sifakis,Ye Ouyang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向L4/L5自治网络的层次化多智能体架构，通过双驱动编排器、公共记忆体和智能体自我意识机制，在5G核心网场景中实现自愈与弹性运维，MTTR降低86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络运维依赖静态脚本，缺乏处理非标称条件的认知能力，无法支撑L4/L5级高度自治网络。

Method: 提出层次化多智能体参考架构：核心是双驱动编排器（协调策略规划与故障恢复）、专用执行智能体、公共记忆体（统一领域知识），并创新性地引入智能体自我意识机制。

Result: 在5G核心网环境中验证，拥塞时可维持关键吞吐量，MTTR降低86%。

Conclusion: 该架构有效统一了战略规划与运维韧性，是实现高水平网络自治的有效路径。

Abstract: Realizing Level 4/5 Autonomous Networks (AN) demands a shift from static automation to agent-native intelligence. Current operations, reliant on rigid scripts, lack the cognitive agency to handle off-nominal conditions. To address this, this letter proposes a hierarchical multi-agent reference architecture enabling high-level autonomy. The framework features a Dual-Driven Orchestrator that coordinates specialized Executive Agents, supported by a shared Public Memory for unified domain knowledge. A key innovation is the integration of agent self-awareness, which empowers the system to harmonize deliberative strategic governance with reflexive fault recovery. We instantiate and validate this architecture within a 5G Core environment. Case studies demonstrate that the system sustains critical throughput under congestion and reduces Mean Time to Repair (MTTR) by 86%, confirming its efficacy in unifying strategic planning with operational resilience.

</details>


### [213] [COAgents: Multi-Agent Framework to Learn and Navigate Routing Problems Search Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20618)
*Oleksandr Yakovenko,Mahdi Mostajabdaveh,Cheikh Ahmed,Abdullah Ali Sivas,Xiaorui Li,Zirui Zhou,Mao Kun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出COAgents，一个协作多智能体框架，通过构建部分搜索图(PSG)来引导VRP求解，结合节点选择、移动选择和跳跃代理，在CVRP和VRPTW基准上达到或超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VRP启发式依赖手工规则的局部改进和跳跃来逃离局部最优，难以泛化到多样实例；端到端学习方法缺乏可迁移性。作者希望设计一个既保持搜索可控性又能跨任务适应的框架。

Method: 将搜索过程建模为图（节点=解，边=局部优化或大扰动），动态构建部分搜索图(PSG)；训练三个智能体：节点选择代理、移动选择代理（指导强化搜索）和跳跃代理（触发探索新区域）；实现问题无关的搜索控制与领域特定编码的分离。

Result: 在CVRP上与多种学习搜索基线持平；在VRPTW上达到学习方法的SOTA：N=100时相比POMO减少14%最优解差距，相比ALNS减少21%；N=50时分别减少44%和40%。

Conclusion: COAgents通过多智能体协作和搜索图结构，有效结合了问题无关的控制与领域知识，在复杂约束的VRPTW上显著超越现有学习方法，验证了分解式搜索框架的通用性。

Abstract: Although Vehicle Routing Problems (VRP) are essential to many real-world systems, they remain computationally intractable at scale due to their combinatorial complexity. Traditional heuristics rely on handcrafted rules for local improvements and occasional \textit{jumps} to escape local minima, but often struggle to generalize across diverse instances. We introduce \textbf{COAgents}, a cooperative multi-agent framework that models the search process as a graph: nodes represent solutions, and edges correspond to either local refinements or large perturbations for diversification (i.e., jumps). A \textit{Partial Search Graph} (PSG) is dynamically constructed during search, enabling COAgents to train a Node Selection Agent and a Move Selection Agent to guide intensification, and a Jump Agent to trigger well-timed explorations of new regions. Unlike end-to-end learning approaches, COAgents cleanly separates problem-agnostic search control from compact domain-specific encoding, facilitating adaptability across tasks. Extensive experiments on the CVRP and VRPTW benchmarks show that COAgents remains competitive with several learn-to-search baselines on CVRP and sets a new state of the art among learning-based methods on the more challenging VRPTW instances, reducing the gap to the best-known solutions by 14\% at $N\!=\!100$ and 44\% at $N\!=\!50$ relative to the strongest neural solver (POMO), and by 21\% and 40\% respectively relative to ALNS.
  Code is available at https://github.com/mahdims/COAgents.

</details>


### [214] [Evaluating Temporal Semantic Caching and Workflow Optimization in Agentic Plan-Execute Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20630)
*Alimurtaza Mustafa Merchant,Krish Veera,Sajal Kumar Goyla,Shambhawi Bhure,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对工业资产运营工作流中延迟敏感的问题，提出了AssetOpsBench（AOB）基准测试，并设计了两层优化：时间语义缓存和MCP工作流优化，分别实现了30.6倍和1.67倍的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业资产运营工作流对延迟高度敏感，单个用户查询需要协调传感器数据、工单、故障模式、预测工具和特定领域智能体。现有LLM缓存技术（如KV-cache复用、基于嵌入的语义缓存）为聊天服务设计，在输出有效性依赖于时间、资产或传感器参数时效果不佳。

Method: 提出两种互补优化层：1）时间语义缓存（temporal semantic cache）；2）MCP工作流优化组合，包括基于磁盘的工具发现缓存（disk-backed tool-discovery caching）和依赖感知的并行步骤执行（dependency-aware parallel step execution）。在AssetOpsBench基准上评估这些方法。

Result: MCP工作流优化实现1.67倍加速，中位端到端延迟降低约40.0%；时间语义缓存在缓存命中时实现中位30.6倍加速。同时揭示了纯语义缓存在参数丰富的工业查询中的具体失败模式。

Conclusion: 研究不仅展示了显著的速度提升，还暴露了纯语义缓存在面向工业参数的查询中的具体失效模式，为MCP支持的智能体基准测试中缓存选择如何与评估正确性相互作用提供了关键分析。

Abstract: Industrial asset operations workflows are latency-sensitive because a single user query may require coordination over sensor data, work orders, failure modes, forecasting tools, and domain-specific agents. We evaluate this problem on AssetOpsBench (AOB), an industrial agent benchmark whose plan-execute pipeline exposes repeated overhead from tool discovery, LLM planning, MCP tool execution, and final summarization. Existing LLM caching techniques such as KV-cache reuse and embedding-based semantic caching were designed for chatbot serving and break down when output validity depends on time, asset, or sensor parameters. We propose two complementary optimization layers for AOB plan-execute pipelines: a temporal semantic cache and a set of MCP workflow optimizations combining disk-backed tool-discovery caching and dependency-aware parallel step execution. MCP workflow optimizations corresponded to a 1.67x speedup and reduced median end-to-end latency by about 40.0% while the temporal-cache benchmark achieved a median of 30.6x speedup on cache hits. Beyond the speedup, our results expose a concrete failure mode of pure semantic caching for parameter-rich industrial queries, providing a critical analysis of how caching choices interact with evaluation correctness in MCP-backed agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [215] [Declarative Data Services: Structured Agentic Discovery for Composing Data Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20690)
*Shanshan Ye,Duo Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出声明式数据服务（DDS）架构，通过分层类型契约将异构数据系统组合的搜索问题分解为有界子搜索，解决了无界智能体发现在复杂数据后端组合中难以收敛的问题，在交易后端工作负载上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的智能体发现在异构多系统数据后端场景中面临三大难题：搜索空间异构、验证器是部署栈是否实际运行、组合知识在预训练中分布不均。无界智能体发现即使增加迭代和显式组合知识也无法一致收敛。

Method: DDS框架设计了四个层级的有类型契约（意图层、算子DAG层、系统技能层、运行时归因层），将全局搜索分解为有界子搜索；子智能体在各类型空间中搜索，框架提供知识前向传播（内联技能引用）和错误反向传播（类型化信号）通道。

Result: 在交易后端工作负载上，DDS成功收敛而无界发现失败；运行时故障转化为技能补丁，被后续部署内联引用。

Conclusion: DDS作为早期原型，为解决真实世界数据系统组合问题提供了可行思路，证明了结构化分解对多系统组合发现的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic discovery has shown that LLM-driven search can find novel algorithms, designs, and code under benchmark conditions. Translating the paradigm to multi-system data backends surfaces a harder problem: the search space is heterogeneous, the verifier is whether a deployed stack actually runs, and composition knowledge is unevenly captured in pretraining. Unbounded agentic discovery, a coding agent iterating on failure-log feedback, fails to converge consistently on a working stack even when iteration and explicit composition knowledge are added. We propose Declarative Data Services (DDS), an architecture for structured agentic discovery of data-system compositions from declarative user intent. The framework owns four typed contracts at successive layers (intent, operator DAG, per-system skills, runtime attribution) that decompose the global search into bounded sub-searches; sub-agents search each typed space, while the framework provides the channels by which knowledge flows forward as inline skill citations and errors route backward as typed signals. As a proof of life on a trading-backend workload, DDS converges where unbounded discovery does not; runtime failures become skill patches that the next deployment cites inline. We position this as an early prototype reporting lessons from real-world data-system composition.

</details>


### [216] [VBFDD-Agent for Electric Vehicle Battery Fault Detection and Diagnosis: Descriptive Text Modeling of Battery Digital Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20742)
*Joey Chan,Zhen Chen,Ershun Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VBFDD-Agent，一种基于大语言模型的车辆电池故障检测与诊断智能体，通过将电池信号转化为结构化文本描述，实现跨域异常检测与可解释的维护建议生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电池故障诊断方法针对特定场景设计，缺乏跨域适应性和人机协作能力，且缺少开源电池故障报告语料库与统一的维护知识表示。

Method: 先将电池监测信号、统计特征、异常记录等转化为结构化自然语言描述，构建语言语料库；再基于此提出VBFDD-Agent，集成描述性电池状态文本、历史案例检索、本地维护手册与LLM推理，生成结构化诊断结果与维护建议。

Result: 实验表明VBFDD-Agent能基于描述性文本表示准确进行异常监测，提供灵活高效的维护建议，专家评估确认了生成建议的实用价值。

Conclusion: VBFDD-Agent将传统电池诊断从标签预测扩展为可解释、面向维护的决策支持，提升了跨域适应性与人机协作能力。

Abstract: With the rapid proliferation of electric vehicles, the safety and reliability of lithium-ion batteries have become critical concerns. Effective anomaly detection is essential for ensuring safe battery operation. However, as battery systems and operating scenarios become increasingly complex, battery fault diagnosis and maintenance require stronger cross-domain adaptability and human-AI collaboration. Traditional fault detection and diagnosis methods are usually designed for specific scenarios and predefined workflows, making them less effective in complex real-world applications.
  To address the scarcity of open-source battery fault report corpora and the lack of unified maintenance knowledge representation, this study proposes a descriptive text modeling approach for battery signal reports. Monitoring signals, statistical features, anomaly records, and state assessment results are transformed into structured and readable natural language descriptions, forming a language corpus for battery health diagnosis and maintenance.
  Based on this corpus, we propose VBFDD-Agent, a vehicle battery fault detection and diagnosis agent for automotive-grade battery systems. VBFDD-Agent integrates descriptive battery-state texts, historical case retrieval, local maintenance manuals, and large language model reasoning to generate structured diagnostic results and maintenance recommendations. Experiments show that the proposed framework can accurately perform anomaly monitoring based on descriptive textual representations and provide flexible, efficient, and actionable maintenance suggestions. Expert evaluation further confirms the practical value of the generated recommendations. Overall, VBFDD-Agent extends traditional battery diagnosis from label prediction to interpretable and maintenance-oriented decision support.

</details>


### [217] [Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance for Flow Models under Compositional Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20758)
*Xuehui Yu,Fucheng Cai,Meiyi Wang,Xiaopeng Fan,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance (g^car)，一种轻量级可学习方法，用于解决扩散/流模型在多约束引导生成时的偏离流形漂移问题，通过动态检测和解决梯度冲突来提升生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时引导采样方法在组合多个约束时容易偏离真实数据流形，作者希望识别这种现象的根源并提出解决方案。

Method: 首先分析偏离流形漂移的根本原因，发现近似误差随梯度不匹配严重放大；进而提出 Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance (g^car)，通过动态检测和解决梯度冲突来主动修正偏离流形漂移。

Result: 在合成数据集、图像编辑以及生成式决策规划等多个领域的实验表明，g^car 有效纠正了偏离流形漂移，在生成保真度上超越基线方法，且计算开销轻量。

Conclusion: g^car 是一种高效、轻量的多约束引导方法，能够显著提升生成质量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Inference-time guided sampling steers state-of-the-art diffusion and flow models without fine-tuning by interpreting the generation process as a controllable trajectory. This provides a simple and flexible way to inject external constraints (e.g., cost functions or pre-trained verifiers) for controlled generation. However, existing methods often fail when composing multiple constraints simultaneously, which leads to deviations from the true data manifold. In this work, we identify root causes of this off-manifold drift and find that the approximation error scales severely with gradient misalignment. Building on these findings, we propose Conflict-Aware Additive Guidance ($g^\text{car}$), a lightweight and learnable method, which actively rectifies off-manifold drift by dynamically detecting and resolving gradient conflicts. We validate $g^\text{car}$ across diverse domains, ranging from synthetic datasets and image editing to generative decision-making for planning and control. Our results demonstrate that $g^\text{car}$ effectively rectifies off-manifold drift, surpassing baselines in generation fidelity while using light compute. Code is available at https://github.com/yuxuehui/CAR-guidance.

</details>


### [218] [Interaction Locality in Hierarchical Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20784)
*Yosuke Miyanishi,Tetsuro Morimura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"交互局部性"（interaction locality）框架，用于衡量空间推理中信息流动是局限于邻近单元/语义片段还是跨越它们。通过在HRM、TRM等模型上的实验发现：递归模型的高层状态倾向于局部写入信息，重复递归更新将局部写入累积为全局解；而在3D具身模型MTU3D中，因果空间局部性主要集中在视觉特征向下游模块传递的边界处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理需要兼具局部计算（如局部移动）和全局结构（如路径/约束/对象级规划），但缺乏一个可量化的框架来测量信息流动的局部性程度。

Method: 提出交互局部性框架，通过稀疏自编码器特征消融和有限噪声激活修补（activation patching）来测量信息流动范围，辅以结构雅可比和注意力检查，应用于HRM、TRM（层次/递归推理模型）和MTU3D（3D场景接地模型）。

Result: 递归模型中高层状态倾向于局部写入，递归更新累积为全局解；3D模型中因果空间局部性集中在模块边界处，而非整个视觉编码器均匀分布。

Conclusion: 交互局部性将直觉上的"局部执行/全局规划"故事转化为可复现的测量框架，揭示了递归推理与具身3D模型在空间信息处理上的关键差异。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning requires both location-bound computation and location-invariant structure: agents must make local moves while preserving route, object, or constraint-level plans. We propose interaction locality, a task-geometry-aware framework for measuring whether information flow stays within nearby cells or semantic segments, or crosses them. We instantiate the framework with sparse-autoencoder feature ablations and finite-noise activation patching, with structural Jacobian and attention checks reported in the appendix, and apply it to HRM and TRM, two compact hierarchical and recursive reasoning models, on Maze-Hard, Sudoku Extreme, and ARC-AGI. Across these models, activation patching gives the clearest architectural fingerprint: high-level recurrent states tend to write information within nearby cells or same-segment units, while repeated recursive updates accumulate these local writes into broader solution structure. This pattern holds across maze paths, Sudoku constraints, and ARC-AGI object neighborhoods, with the strongest concentration in TRM. To test whether interaction locality extends beyond toy-yet-challenging grid benchmarks, we also apply it to MTU3D, a large-scale embodied 3D scene-grounding model. In this MTU3D setting, causal spatial locality appears primarily at the transition where visual scene features are handed to the downstream grounding module, rather than uniformly throughout the visual encoder. This contrast suggests that the local-to-global handoff observed in HRM and TRM is tied to explicit recursive reasoning dynamics, while embodied 3D models may concentrate causal spatial structure at module boundaries. Interaction locality turns the intuitive local-execution/global-planning story into a reproducible measurement framework for recursive and embodied spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [219] [Conditional Equivalence of DPO and RLHF: Implicit Assumption, Failure Modes, and Provable Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20834)
*Zhiqin Yang,Yonggang Zhang,Wei Xue,Dong Fang,Bo Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明DPO与RLHF的理论等价性是有条件的，当隐含假设被违反时，DPO会优化与人类偏好不一致的目标，导致病态收敛；作者提出CPO方法，通过约束确保可证明的对齐，并达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO虽被视为RLHF的简化替代，但其理论等价性依赖于一个在实践中常被违反的隐含假设（RLHF最优策略必须偏好人类偏好的回答）。当该假设不成立时，DPO与RLHF优化不同目标，可能导致策略偏好不被偏好的回答。

Method: 1) 理论分析DPO与RLHF等价的充要条件，指出隐含假设被违反的情况；2) 证明存在病态解空间，DPO优化的是相对优势而非绝对对齐；3) 提出约束偏好优化（CPO），在RLHF中加入约束以实现可证明的对齐；4) 通过软间隔排序进行几何解释。

Result: 理论证明DPO在假设违反时存在病态收敛；CPO在标准基准测试上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: DPO的等价性是有条件的而非普适的，CPO通过约束保持了DPO的简洁性同时实现了可证明的对齐。
